<p class='hn-title'>(1) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thereallo.dev/blog/claude-code-prompt-steganography' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Code is steganographically marking requests</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Code 以隐写方式标记请求</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48734373' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 2395</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48734373' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 731</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers demonstrate how Claude Code can embed hidden signals in prompts, enabling steganographic tagging of requests. This raises questions about model privacy, misuse, and provenance in AI-assisted workflows. The finding highlights the need for robust controls around prompt encoding.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者展示了 Claude Code 如何在提示中嵌入隐写信号，给请求标记隐藏标识。这引发对模型隐私、滥用和工作流可追溯性的担忧，凸显了对提示编码的鲁棒控制的必要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48755965' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(2) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://f-droid.org/2026/07/01/adv-malware.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Android Developer Verification: Threat masquerading as protection</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Android 开发者验证：伪装成保护的威胁</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48755965' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1665</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48755965' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 714</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Android developer verification can be abused by threat actors who disguise malware as protective or security tools. Such tactics exploit trust signals around security features to mislead users or bypass vetting, undermining platform defenses. The takeaway is a push for tighter vetting, clearer indicators of app behavior, and user skepticism when apps claim to protect you.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Android 开发者验证可能被威胁者利用，伪装成保护性工具的恶意软件。此类手段利用安全功能的信任信号误导用户或规避审核，削弱平台防护。要点在于加强审核、提供更清晰的应用行为指示，并提高用户在遇到自称保护工具的应用时的警惕。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://f-droid.org/assets/fdroid-logo_bfHl7nsLHOUQxzdU8-rGIhn4bAgl6z7k2mA3fWoCyT4=.png' data-full='https://f-droid.org/assets/fdroid-logo_bfHl7nsLHOUQxzdU8-rGIhn4bAgl6z7k2mA3fWoCyT4=.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic released Claude Sonnet 5, the newest member of its Claude family. The update emphasizes stronger reasoning capabilities and tighter safety controls for enterprise AI deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 发布 Claude Sonnet 5，这是 Claude 家族的最新成员，强调改进的推理能力与企业部署的安全控制。它面向企业场景，优化推理质量并提升对安全约束的可控性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(5) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://quesma.com/blog/qwen-36-is-awesome/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Qwen 3.6 27B is the sweet spot for local development</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Qwen 3.6 27B：本地开发的最佳平衡点</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48721903' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1171</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48721903' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 732</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Qwen 3.6 27B hits a practical sweet spot for local AI development. It provides a capable, smaller model that runs on modest GPUs, easing offline prototyping and testing without cloud latency. For developers, this balance between performance and footprint matters as it accelerates iteration cycles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Qwen 3.6 27B 在本地 AI 开发中找到了实用的平衡点。它具备足够能力、体积适中，能在普通显卡上运行，降低离线原型与测试对云端延迟的依赖。对开发者而言，这种性能与资源的权衡能显著提升迭代速度。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(6) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://semgrep.dev/blog/2026/we-have-mythos-at-home-glm-52-beats-claude-in-our-cyber-benchmarks/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GLM 5.2 beats Claude in our benchmarks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GLM 5.2 在基准测试中击败 Claude</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48709670' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1084</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48709670' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 503</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GLM 5.2 beat Claude in our benchmarks, showing that newer open models are narrowing the gap with top proprietary rivals. The result reinforces that cost, licensing, and ecosystem maturity can influence real-world decisions as AI hardware and tooling evolve.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GLM 5.2 在我们的基准测试中击败 Claude，显示较新的开源模型正在缩小与顶级专有模型的差距。该结果强调，在推理硬件和工具集持续演进的背景下，成本、许可与生态系统成熟度可能成为实际选型的关键因素。</p>

</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48714529' data-tags='Privacy,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(7) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nonogra.ph/age-verification-is-just-a-precursor-to-attribution-of-speech-06-29-2026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Age verification is just a precursor to automated attribution of speech</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>年龄验证只是自动化语音归因的前提</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48714529' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 989</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48714529' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 608</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that age verification schemes may simply enable automatic attribution of who said what in speech. As identity becomes tied to voice data, safeguards around privacy, consent, and misuse become critical, with implications for policy and AI system design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中指出，年龄验证可能只是实现对语音进行自动归因的前提条件。当声音数据与身份绑定时，隐私、同意和滥用防护就变得尤为关键，对政策和 AI 系统设计都会产生影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48713832' data-tags='Open Source,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(8) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://danunparsed.com/p/hackerrank-open-source-ats' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>HackerRank open sourced its ATS. My resume scored 90/100. Oh wait 74. No – 88</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>HackerRank 开源其 ATS，简历评分波动引发关注</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48713832' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 983</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48713832' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 421</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>HackerRank released its ATS as open source, exposing how resumes are scored in practice. A single resume yielded a string of conflicting scores (90, then 74, then 88), illustrating instability and potential bias in automated screening. The takeaway: hiring AI needs better transparency, reproducibility, and fairness checks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>HackerRank 将其 ATS 开源，使人们能审视简历评分的全过程。一次测试的简历竟得到 90、74、88 等不同分数，凸显自动筛选的不稳定性和潜在偏见。要点是招聘 AI 需加强透明性、可重复性与公平性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!S-dx!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fbab22a57-acb5-4fe4-929f-132059bcb466_1800x900.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!S-dx!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fbab22a57-acb5-4fe4-929f-132059bcb466_1800x900.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48740771' data-tags='Legal,Business,AI,Security,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(9) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/AnthropicAI/status/2072106151890809341' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Department of Commerce has lifted export controls on Claude Fable 5 and Mythos 5</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>商务部取消对 Claude Fable 5 与 Mythos 5 的出口管制</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48740771' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 946</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48740771' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 669</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Department of Commerce has eased export controls on Claude Fable 5 and Mythos 5, enabling broader international distribution. The move could accelerate global AI deployment and competition, while inviting regulatory and security considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>商务部放松了对 Claude Fable 5 和 Mythos 5 的出口管制，使其在全球范围内的分发更为广泛。这一举措可能加速全球 AI 的部署与竞争，同时带来监管与安全方面的考量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1798110641414443008/XP8gyBaY_200x200.jpg' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1798110641414443008/XP8gyBaY_200x200.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48767347' data-tags='Privacy,Legal,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(10) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.hunton.com/privacy-and-cybersecurity-law-blog/virginia-bans-sale-of-geolocation-data' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Virginia bans sale of precise geolocation data</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>弗吉尼亚州禁止出售地理位置数据</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48767347' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 941</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48767347' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 137</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Virginia&#x27;s new privacy rule bans the sale of geolocation data collected from devices. The move tightens location data markets and will impact marketers, data brokers, and app developers who rely on location signals. It also heightens regulatory fragmentation as more states consider similar restrictions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>弗吉尼亚州出台隐私新规，禁止出售设备收集的地理位置信息数据。此举收紧了地理位置信息市场，影响依赖位置信号的广告商、数据经纪人与应用开发者。同时，这也可能推动其他州采取类似限制，加剧合规碎片化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.hunton.com/privacy-and-cybersecurity-law-blog/assets/images-t1783027116/206510.png' data-full='https://www.hunton.com/privacy-and-cybersecurity-law-blog/assets/images-t1783027116/206510.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48747304' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(11) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.quantamagazine.org/for-the-first-time-a-cell-built-from-scratch-grows-and-divides-20260701/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>For first time, a cell built from scratch grows and divides</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>首次从零构建的细胞能够生长并分裂</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48747304' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 940</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48747304' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 295</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers have built a cell from scratch that can grow and divide, marking a milestone in bottom-up synthetic biology. By assembling functional cellular life from individual components, the work advances the dream of programmable biology and could unlock new biotechnologies and origins-of-life insights. Yet, challenges around stability, safety, and control remain as researchers push toward more complex designs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者首次从零构建出可生长和分裂的细胞，标志着自下而上的合成生物学里程碑。通过从单个组件组装功能性细胞生命，这项工作推动了可编程生物学的实现，并可能为生物技术和起源研究带来新机遇。当然，稳定性、安全性与可控性仍是未来进一步复杂化设计时需要面对的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/SyntheticLife-cr-Ada-Zejun-Shen-social.jpg' data-full='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/SyntheticLife-cr-Ada-Zejun-Shen-social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(12) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.pragmaticengineer.com/pollen-tried-to-remove-my-article-about-callum-negus-fancey-and-google-is-assisting-to-it/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pollen tried to remove my article and Google is assisting with it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Pollen 试图删除我的文章，Google 正在协助</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48716902' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 897</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48716902' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 126</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An investigative post claims Pollen tried to suppress a journalist&#x27;s article about Callum Negus-Fancey, with Google allegedly aiding the effort. The episode highlights how platform power can influence publication visibility and the fragility of independent reporting in high-stakes tech stories. It also raises questions about transparency and due process in takedown cooperation between platforms and search engines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称，Pollen 试图删除一篇关于 Callum Negus-Fancey 的文章，谷歌据称在协助此事。此事揭示了平台权力对内容可见性和独立报道的脆弱性，引发对透明度与程序正义的担忧。事件也凸显了在高风险科技报道中协作取证过程的复杂性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(13) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://organicmaps.app/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Organic Maps</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Organic Maps 地图应用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48794446' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 888</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48794446' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 264</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Open-source offline maps that respect privacy and work without a network, emphasizing lightweight design and open data. It offers offline routing and data transparency, appealing to privacy-minded travelers and developers alike.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开源的离线地图，注重隐私且无需联网即可使用，强调轻量与开放数据。提供离线导航和透明的数据结构，适合关注隐私的旅行者与开发者。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48783117' data-tags='Health,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(14) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.mikebowler.ca/2026/07/03/co2-and-decision-making/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The bottleneck might be the air in the room</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>瓶颈可能在房间里的空气</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48783117' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 789</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48783117' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 455</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CO2 buildup in poorly ventilated spaces can impair decision-making and cognitive performance, setting a hidden bottleneck for teams. The post argues that improving ventilation and air quality is a cost-effective way to boost focus and judgment in meetings and workplaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在密闭空间里二氧化碳积聚会削弱决策能力和认知表现，成为团队的潜在瓶颈。文章强调通过改善通风和空气质量是提升专注力与判断力的成本效益办法，尤其在会议和工作场所。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://files.gargoylesoftware.com/images/mike_bowler.jpg' data-full='https://files.gargoylesoftware.com/images/mike_bowler.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48745456' data-tags='Gaming,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(15) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.playstation.com/2026/07/01/physical-disc-production-ending-in-january-2028-for-new-games-releasing-on-playstation-consoles/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Physical disc production ending in Jan 2028 for new games on PlayStation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PlayStation 将于 2028 年 1 月停止新作实体光盘生产</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48745456' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 733</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48745456' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 742</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sony will end physical disc production for new PlayStation releases in January 2028, accelerating its digital-first strategy. The move reduces manufacturing and distribution costs but may affect collectors, retailers, and regions with stronger physical markets. It signals a lasting shift in how console games are distributed and consumed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>索尼将于 2028 年 1 月停止新作实体光盘的生产，进一步推动数字化优先策略。此举降低了制造与发行成本，但可能影响收藏者、零售商以及实体市场较强的地区，预示着主机游戏发行与消费方式的长期转变。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.playstation.com/tachyon/2026/07/70bb22030b29f4234fd812253bf1129b00609c4c.jpg' data-full='https://blog.playstation.com/tachyon/2026/07/70bb22030b29f4234fd812253bf1129b00609c4c.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(17) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.patrick-breyer.de/en/double-threat-to-private-communications-undemocratic-chat-control-backroom-deals-and-imminent-concessions-spark-relaunch-of-fightchatcontrol-eu/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EU to legislate about Chat Control behind closed doors</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧盟在幕后推动聊天监控立法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48707719' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 726</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48707719' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 428</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports indicate the EU plans to legislate on Chat Control behind closed doors, raising concerns about transparency and civil liberties. The move signals a push to regulate private communications and could set norms that influence global tech policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，欧盟正在幕后推动“聊天控制”相关立法，引发对透明度和公民自由的担忧。此举表明欧洲正推动对私人通信的规制，可能对全球科技政策产生重要影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.patrick-breyer.de/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/240130_Breyer-Alternativen-engl.png' data-full='https://www.patrick-breyer.de/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/240130_Breyer-Alternativen-engl.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48746445' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(18) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://wangcong.org/2026-06-30-why-i-stopped-arguing-with-people.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Most arguments are about ego, not ideas</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大多數爭論源於自尊心，而非觀點</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48746445' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 712</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48746445' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 556</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Most online disagreements stem from ego rather than substantive ideas. The piece argues for stepping away from heated exchanges and focusing on listening and shared goals, which can make discussions more productive and informative for tech communities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>大多數在線爭論源於自尊心，而非實質性觀點。文章倡導在情緒升溫時停止爭辯，專注傾聽並明確共同目標，讓技術社群的討論更高效、具啟發性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48730729' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(19) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://waag.org/en/article/european-digital-id-wallets-are-gift-google-and-apple/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>European digital ID wallets rely on safety services of Google and Apple</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧洲数字身份证钱包依赖谷歌和苹果的安全服务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48730729' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 695</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48730729' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 299</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>European digital ID wallets rely on the safety services from Google and Apple, tying cross-border identity tech to US platform ecosystems. While this brings strong security and interoperability, it raises questions about data sovereignty, governance, and vendor dependence as Europe pushes self-sovereign identity models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧洲数字身份钱包在安全服务上高度依赖谷歌与苹果，依托成熟的安全生态提升跨境互通。与此同时，这种依赖也引发数据主权、治理和对厂商依赖的担忧，或影响未来的自我主权身份框架的推进。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cms.waag.org/sites/waag/files/styles/1200x630/public/2026-06/scherm-afbeelding_2026-06-23_om_10.31.35.png' data-full='https://cms.waag.org/sites/waag/files/styles/1200x630/public/2026-06/scherm-afbeelding_2026-06-23_om_10.31.35.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48711981' data-tags='Politics,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(20) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://theintercept.com/2026/06/26/daniel-sanchez-estrada-zines-prairieland-free-speech/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>30-year sentence for transporting zines is a five-alarm fire for free speech</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>运输 zines 获30年监禁：对言论自由的严重警报</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48711981' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 681</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48711981' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 392</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The case of a man sentenced to 30 years for transporting zines highlights how anti-distribution laws can chill free speech and independent media. The severity underscores concerns about criminal penalties for sharing alternative viewpoints, spurring debates on reform and civil liberties.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名男子因运输 zines 被判处30年监禁，凸显打击分发的法律如何对言论自由和独立媒体造成威慑。如此严厉的刑罚引发对相关法律改革与公民自由的广泛关注与讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://theintercept.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/IMG_4281-e1782406948568.jpg?fit=2048%2C1024&amp;w=1200&amp;h=800' data-full='https://theintercept.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/IMG_4281-e1782406948568.jpg?fit=2048%2C1024&amp;w=1200&amp;h=800' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48730608' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://europeancorrespondent.com/en/r/the-us-ambassador-had-belgian-police-stop-our-reporting' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The US ambassador had Belgian police stop our reporting</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美大使让比利时警方叫停报道</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48730608' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 679</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48730608' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 319</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A US ambassador allegedly prompted Belgian police to halt our reporting, stoking debate about diplomatic influence on journalism in Europe. The episode highlights the risks to press freedom when state actors attempt to control coverage abroad and may invite scrutiny of diplomatic immunity and journalistic independence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称美国大使促使比利时警方叫停报道，引发欧洲范围内对外交干预新闻自由的讨论。此事件凸显当国家行为体试图干预境外报道时，媒体自由可能受到的寒蝉效应，以及对外交豁免与言论自由监管的关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.europeancorrespondent.com/attRp54hjyayl13fH/public' data-full='https://images.europeancorrespondent.com/attRp54hjyayl13fH/public' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48724230' data-tags='Web,Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(22) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hccf.onmy.cloud/2026/06/21/reclaiming-our-digital-selves-hccfs-vision-for-a-human-centered-top-level-domain/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>.self: A new top-level domain designed to support self-hosting</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>.self：为自托管设计的新顶级域名</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48724230' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 667</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48724230' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 378</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>.self is proposed as a human-centered top-level domain intended to empower individuals to run personal, self-hosted services with less reliance on centralized providers. The idea signals a shift in DNS governance toward user-owned namespaces and could spur tooling and registries to support personal data sovereignty, though practical adoption and security models remain to be worked out.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>“.self” 被提出作为以人为本的顶级域名，旨在让个人能够在较少依赖中心化服务商的情况下运营自托管服务。这一构想推动域名系统向用户自有命名空间的方向发展，可能促成支持个人数据主权的工具与注册体系，但实际落地的采纳度与安全模型尚待完善。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://hccf.onmy.cloud/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/HCCF_Logo_new_snug_square_transparent-3.png' data-full='https://hccf.onmy.cloud/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/HCCF_Logo_new_snug_square_transparent-3.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48797916' data-tags='Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(23) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.opentools.studio/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenPrinter</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenPrinter：开放打印机项目</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48797916' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 651</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48797916' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 159</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenPrinter describes an open-source printing project with modular firmware and hardware design, aiming to reduce vendor lock-in and empower communities to customize printers. The initiative could accelerate repairability, transparency, and ecosystem innovation for makers and businesses alike.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenPrinter 是一个开放打印机项目，强调可模块化固件与硬件设计，旨在降低厂商锁定并让社区参与定制。该方向可能促进可修复性、透明度和生态创新，但也需关注可持续性与供应链问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.opentools.studio/web/image/1096-6c03f418/Open-printer_2_black-prints.webp' data-full='https://www.opentools.studio/web/image/1096-6c03f418/Open-printer_2_black-prints.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48786781' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(24) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://javoriuski.com/post/youtube' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Leaking YouTube creators&#x27; private videos</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>泄露 YouTube 创作者的私人视频</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48786781' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 646</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48786781' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 368</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Private videos of YouTube creators have surfaced, underscoring how gaps in privacy controls and access management can leak sensitive content. The incident spotlights the friction between creator workflows, platform safeguards, and third-party storage or collaboration tools. It reinforces the need for strict access auditing, multi-factor authentication, and encrypted pipelines in large creator ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有些 YouTube 创作者的私人视频被泄露，暴露出隐私控制与访问管理的薄弱环节。事件凸显了创作者工作流、平台防护与第三方存储/协作工具之间的矛盾。需要加强访问审计、启用多因素认证，以及在大型创作者生态系统中实施端到端加密和更严格的数据管控。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://javoriuski.com/assets/youtube.jpg' data-full='https://javoriuski.com/assets/youtube.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48698908' data-tags='Open Source,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(25) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://weblog.rogueamoeba.com/2026/06/26/free-the-icons/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Free the Icons</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>解放图标资源</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48698908' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 645</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48698908' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 239</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rogue Amoeba argues for freeing the icons used in software from restrictive licenses, advocating permissive, open licensing to accelerate UI design and interoperability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rogue Amoeba 提出释放图标资源的主张，主张对图标采用更开放、宽松的许可，以加速用户界面设计与跨项目协作。此举可能降低开发者的授权门槛，促进自由/开源项目的可用性，但具体的许可方案与落地路径仍待讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://weblog.rogueamoeba.com/wp-content/uploads/2026-06-iconshapes/banner@2x.png' data-full='https://weblog.rogueamoeba.com/wp-content/uploads/2026-06-iconshapes/banner@2x.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48788283' data-tags='Gaming,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(26) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/ammaarreshi/Generals-Mac-iOS-iPad/tree/main' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Command and Conquer Generals natively ported to macOS, iPhone, iPad using Fable</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>使用 Fable 将《Command &amp; Conquer: Generals》原生移植到 macOS、iPhone、iPad</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48788283' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 644</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48788283' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 269</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fans have natively ported Command &amp; Conquer Generals to macOS, iPhone, and iPad using Fable. The port demonstrates how modern cross-compile toolchains can revive classic PC games on Apple devices, but licensing and control questions remain.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>粉丝项目将《Command &amp; Conquer Generals》原生移植到 macOS、iPhone 与 iPad，使用 Fable 实现跨平台运行。此举展示了当代跨平台工具链在复活经典 PC 游戏方面的潜力，同时也引发关于授权和设备支持的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6feb14451994ca7caac0049e817aeae3b73c0801317c3c71d8f08bcc47b12e7a/ammaarreshi/Generals-Mac-iOS-iPad' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6feb14451994ca7caac0049e817aeae3b73c0801317c3c71d8f08bcc47b12e7a/ammaarreshi/Generals-Mac-iOS-iPad' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(27) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://det.social/@lostgen/116820546568940358' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The CEO of Mullvad is the main financer of the Swedish Örebro party</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mullvad 的 CEO 是瑞典极右翼 Örebro 党的主要资助人</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48717469' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 638</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48717469' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1415</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Mullvad VPN&#x27;s CEO is reported as the main financier of the far-right Örebro party in Sweden, raising questions about platform neutrality and trust for privacy-focused services. The revelation prompts discussion about funding sources and corporate ethics in the privacy space.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，Mullvad VPN 的 CEO 是瑞典极右翼 Örebro 党的主要资助人，对注重隐私的服务的中立性和信任度带来质疑。此事引发对资金来源与科技隐私领域企业伦理的关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://det.social/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' data-full='https://det.social/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(28) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Chocobozzz/PeerTube' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PeerTube is a free, decentralized and federated video platform</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PeerTube 是一个免费、去中心化且联邦化的视频平台</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48759634' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 636</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48759634' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 310</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PeerTube is a free, decentralized and federated video platform</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PeerTube 提供一个免费、去中心化且联邦化的视频平台，强调社区主机与隐私保护，体现对大型中心化平台的替代愿景。</p>
