<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48762098' data-tags='DevOps,Open Source,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(29) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.podman.io/2026/07/introducing-podman-v6-0-0/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Podman v6.0.0</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Podman 6.0.0 版本发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48762098' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 633</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48762098' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 248</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Podman 6.0.0 marks a major milestone with expanded rootless container support, improved OCI compatibility, and streamlined tooling. It emphasizes security and developer ergonomics, aligning Podman with modern container workloads and Kubernetes workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>6.0.0 版本标志着 Podman 的一个重要里程碑，增强了无根容器支持、改进的 OCI 兼容性以及更简化的工具链。此版本强调安全性与开发者体验，使 Podman 更适合现代容器工作负载和 Kubernetes 场景。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s0.wp.com/i/blank.jpg' data-full='https://s0.wp.com/i/blank.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48747389' data-tags='Legal,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(30) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://reclaimthenet.org/sony-deletes-551-studiocanal-movies-playstation-owners-paid-for' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sony Deletes 551 Movies PlayStation Owners Paid For</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>索尼删除了 PlayStation 用户购买的 551 部电影</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48747389' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 627</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48747389' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 289</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sony reportedly removed 551 StudioCanal titles from PlayStation libraries, affecting owners who thought they had purchased those films. The incident underlines how digital ownership hinges on licensing and can disappear with rights changes. It raises questions about the permanence of digital libraries and consumer protections.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报导，索尼从 PlayStation 库中移除了 551 部 StudioCanal 的电影，影响了以为自己购买过的用户。事件凸显数字所有权依赖于许可，权利变更就可能导致内容消失。引发对数字馆藏永久性与消费者保护的思考。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.reclaimthenet.org/2026/06/uS2Yrsa3uRef.jpg' data-full='https://media.reclaimthenet.org/2026/06/uS2Yrsa3uRef.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48683098' data-tags='Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(31) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://opensourcelowtech.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Open Source Low Tech</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>开源低技术</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48683098' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 618</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48683098' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 129</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Open Source Low Tech advocates practical, accessible open-source approaches to everyday tech. It emphasizes simplicity, transparency, and reusability as core values, not just flashy features.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Open Source Low Tech 提倡以实用、易获得的开源方法来构建日常技术。它强调简单性、透明度和可重复使用性作为核心价值，而不仅仅是花哨的功能。文章还介绍了为何低门槛、开源的选择能够为开发者和创客带来更易维护的系统。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opensourcelowtech.org/slides/VAWT_slide1.jpg' data-full='https://opensourcelowtech.org/slides/VAWT_slide1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48761944' data-tags='Open Source,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(32) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/immich-app/immich/discussions/29439' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Immich 3.0</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Immich 3.0 版本发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48761944' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 617</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48761944' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 287</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Immich 3.0 ships major improvements to the open-source self-hosted photo backup app, including a refreshed UI, faster sync, and stronger privacy controls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Immich 3.0 为开源自托管照片备份应用带来重大改进，包括焕然一新的界面、更快的同步速度以及更强的隐私控制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/43df31b2b03ac8b0b532671c3f0fd16f1511be401d54679825f977baf7137261/immich-app/immich/discussions/29439' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/43df31b2b03ac8b0b532671c3f0fd16f1511be401d54679825f977baf7137261/immich-app/immich/discussions/29439' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48720924' data-tags='Privacy,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(33) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/jun/29/supreme-court-geofence-warrants-case-decision' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US Supreme Court rules geofence warrants require constitutional protections</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国最高法院裁定地理围栏检 warrants 需宪法保护</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48720924' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 610</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48720924' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 293</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Supreme Court ruled geofence warrants must be protected by Fourth Amendment standards, reinforcing privacy safeguards for location data. The decision shapes how law enforcement can access location information and how tech platforms respond to such requests.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最高法院裁定地理围栏搜查须适用第四修正案标准，加强对定位数据的隐私保护。这一判决影响执法获取位置信息的边界以及科技平台对相关请求的应对。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/ddb87e813bdb11401cf5abac3654ec9ff5b15d85/328_0_5001_4002/master/5001.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=4dde1080bea475527e46e854344cca12' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/ddb87e813bdb11401cf5abac3654ec9ff5b15d85/328_0_5001_4002/master/5001.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=4dde1080bea475527e46e854344cca12' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48774518' data-tags='Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(34) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.gamingonlinux.com/2026/07/valve-open-source-the-steam-machine-e-ink-screen-so-you-can-make-your-own/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Valve open-source the Steam Machine e-ink screen so you can make your own</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Valve 将 Steam Machine 的电子墨水屏开源，教你自制</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48774518' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 601</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48774518' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 114</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Valve releases the e-ink display design from Steam Machine as open source, inviting creators to reuse and remix for their own projects. This openness lowers barriers for hardware experimentation and highlights a community driven approach to hardware prototyping.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Valve 将 Steam Machine 的电子墨水屏设计开源，鼓励创作者在自定义项目中复用与改造。这一开源举措降低了硬件试验门槛，凸显硬件原型设计的社区驱动性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.gamingonlinux.com/uploads/articles/tagline_images/130717818id29323gol_og.jpg' data-full='https://www.gamingonlinux.com/uploads/articles/tagline_images/130717818id29323gol_og.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48755731' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(35) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tedium.co/2026/07/01/online-web-forums-retrospective/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bring back crappy forums</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>让糟糕论坛卷土重来</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48755731' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 564</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48755731' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 347</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective argues for reviving the rough-and-tumble forum culture that predates modern platforms—less polish, more candid discussion, and simpler moderation. It examines what early forums did well and what modern networks could borrow, from lightweight threading to signal-to-noise management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该文回顾并主张让糟糕但充满活力的论坛文化回归——强调直率讨论、低门槛与简化的社区治理。它讨论早期论坛的优点，以及现代社群应借鉴的经验，如简化的主题讨论与信号噪声管理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://v1.screenshot.11ty.dev/https%3A%2F%2Ftedium.co%2Fog-images%2F2026%2F07%2F01%2Fonline-web-forums-retrospective%2F/opengraph/?v=1782964396' data-full='https://v1.screenshot.11ty.dev/https%3A%2F%2Ftedium.co%2Fog-images%2F2026%2F07%2F01%2Fonline-web-forums-retrospective%2F/opengraph/?v=1782964396' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48769397' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(36) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.caseyliss.com/2026/7/2/carplay-is-additive-you-dolts' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CarPlay Is Additive</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CarPlay 是增补而非替代</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48769397' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 562</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48769397' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 698</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author contends that CarPlay augments existing car interfaces rather than replacing them, adding convenience without solving deeper UX and safety trade-offs in automotive software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为 CarPlay 是对车载界面的补充，而非替代，带来便利但并未解决汽车软件中的深层 UX 与安全权衡。文章强调苹果的策略改变了用户预期和生态格局，迫使厂商在整合与保留车辆专属控制间寻找平衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.caseyliss.com/images/2026/no.gif' data-full='https://www.caseyliss.com/images/2026/no.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48735770' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(37) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://claude.com/product/claude-science' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Science</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Science</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48735770' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 559</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48735770' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 173</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Science is a new product focused on scientific workloads, offering specialized reasoning and data-handling features for researchers. It aims to speed up literature review, modeling, and experimentation workflows for scientists.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Science 是面向科学工作负载的新产品，提供专门的推理和数据处理能力，帮助研究人员加速分析与建模。面向研究人员和数据科学家，旨在优化文献综述、实验设计与数据分析的工作流。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6889473510b50328dbb70ae6/6a429b9909aa1b063a203021_life-sciences-tn.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6889473510b50328dbb70ae6/6a429b9909aa1b063a203021_life-sciences-tn.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48708941' data-tags='AI,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(38) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://antoine.fi/mri-analysis-using-claude-code-opus' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I used Claude Code to get a second opinion on my MRI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我用 Claude Code 为我的 MRI 做了第二意见</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48708941' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 556</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48708941' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 690</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A clinician uses Claude Code to review an MRI, illustrating how AI copilots can augment radiology while raising caveats. This kind of usage emphasizes AI as an assistive, not replacement, tool in clinical workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者用 Claude Code 对 MRI 进行了二次意见的评估，展示了 AI 辅助工具在放射学领域的潜在作用。文中指出了如快速获取第二意见和广泛模式识别等实际好处，同时警示了可靠性、幻觉输出与隐私方面的局限性，强调 AI 仅应作为临床流程中的辅助工具。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://antoine.fi/rails/active_storage/blobs/proxy/eyJfcmFpbHMiOnsiZGF0YSI6Mzk4LCJwdXIiOiJibG9iX2lkIn19--d3e6efb19dc4b4dc72f3345968d90c00d2d027fb/avatar.jpeg' data-full='https://antoine.fi/rails/active_storage/blobs/proxy/eyJfcmFpbHMiOnsiZGF0YSI6Mzk4LCJwdXIiOiJibG9iX2lkIn19--d3e6efb19dc4b4dc72f3345968d90c00d2d027fb/avatar.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48776044' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(39) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://phenomenalworld.org/analysis/the-anti-amazon/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Costco is the anti-Amazon</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Costco：与亚马逊截然不同的商业模式</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48776044' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 556</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48776044' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 556</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores how Costco’s membership model, bulk-buying, limited SKUs, and disciplined logistics create a different kind of customer loyalty and price discipline than Amazon’s marketplace approach. The piece argues these choices position Costco as the anti-Amazon in the modern retail era.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章分析 Costco 的会员制、批量采购、有限 SKU 和严密的物流管理，展示其与亚马逊的不同商业模式与竞争优势，称其为现代零售中的‘反亚马逊’。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://phenomenalworld.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Copy-of-2024-NEW-PW-Twitter-Vertical-Article-Preview-8.png' data-full='https://phenomenalworld.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Copy-of-2024-NEW-PW-Twitter-Vertical-Article-Preview-8.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48744518' data-tags='Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(40) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://asahilinux.org/2026/06/progress-report-7-1/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Asahi Linux 7.1 Progress Report</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Asahi Linux 7.1 进展报告</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48744518' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 553</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48744518' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 210</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Asahi Linux 7.1 delivers new kernel improvements, broader Apple Silicon support, and performance tweaks aimed at making Macs run Linux more smoothly. The update narrows hardware gaps and enhances stability for everyday use and development on Apple hardware. It signals ongoing momentum for Linux on consumer devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Asahi Linux 7.1 的进展包括内核改进、对 Apple Silicon 的更广泛支持以及性能优化，旨在让在 Mac 上运行 Linux 更加顺畅。此次更新缩小了硬件兼容性差距、提升了日常使用与开发的稳定性，继续推动 Linux 在消费级设备上的应用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://asahilinux.org/img/AsahiLinux_logomark_256px.png' data-full='https://asahilinux.org/img/AsahiLinux_logomark_256px.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48743472' data-tags='Open Source,Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(41) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pcgamer.com/gaming-industry/open-source-game-engine-godot-will-no-longer-accept-ai-authored-code-contributions-we-cant-trust-heavy-users-of-ai-to-understand-their-code-enough-to-fix-it/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Godot will no longer accept AI-authored code contributions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Godot 将不再接受 AI 编写的代码贡献</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48743472' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 544</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48743472' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 387</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Godot Foundation bans AI-authored code contributions to the core, citing concerns about reliability and maintainability. The policy underscores the ongoing tension in OSS between AI-assisted tooling and the need for human oversight in critical code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Godot 基金会宣布不再接受 AI 编写的代码提交，理由是对可靠性和可维护性的担忧。此举凸显开源社区在 AI 辅助编码和人类审阅之间的持续张力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/9fe6i3bvMxcRA6sb2TJAS-1600-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/9fe6i3bvMxcRA6sb2TJAS-1600-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48708991' data-tags='AI,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://english.elpais.com/education/2026-06-28/ai-fraud-at-brown-university-academic-integrity-is-at-risk.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Professor denounces mass AI fraud on an exam at Brown</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>布朗大学教授谴责考试中的大规模 AI 作弊</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48708991' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 543</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48708991' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 713</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Brown University professor condemns widespread AI-assisted cheating on an exam, highlighting threats to academic integrity. The discussion explores policy responses, detection strategies, and the need for robust assessment design in the AI era.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>布朗大学一位教授谴责在考试中广泛使用 AI 作弊，强调对学术诚信的威胁。讨论探讨了政策应对、检测策略以及在 AI 时代加强评估设计的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://imagenes.elpais.com/resizer/v2/J6ITLVPHQNHI3BHNR3FPIX5URE.jpg?auth=29cc39cd3e10fbe5a5266dd40100f1d61e4fe145ccd2e4ab13b6a6bd0b67f1dd&amp;width=1200' data-full='https://imagenes.elpais.com/resizer/v2/J6ITLVPHQNHI3BHNR3FPIX5URE.jpg?auth=29cc39cd3e10fbe5a5266dd40100f1d61e4fe145ccd2e4ab13b6a6bd0b67f1dd&amp;width=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48745113' data-tags='Business,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(43) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mynintendonews.com/2026/06/26/nintendo-has-raised-its-employees-base-salary-by-10/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nintendo has raised its employees base salary by 10%</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>任天堂将员工基本薪资提高10%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48745113' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 535</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48745113' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 326</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nintendo announced a 10% raise to base salaries, signaling wage growth in a major entertainment tech company. The move may aid retention and morale amid tight labor markets while squeezing margins modestly. It also mirrors broader wage-tightening and inflation-adaptation trends in the industry.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>任天堂宣布将员工基本工资提高10%，显示出在劳动力紧张的市场中公司对人才的重视。此举有助于提升留任与士气，但可能对利润率产生一定挤压，反映出行业的薪资调整与通胀应对趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/mynintendonews.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/09/nintendo_logo.jpg?fit=2048%2C1341&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/mynintendonews.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/09/nintendo_logo.jpg?fit=2048%2C1341&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48770647' data-tags='Politics,Business,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(44) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stefan.schueller.net/posts/the-free-market-lie/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why Switzerland has 25 gbit internet and America doesn&#x27;t</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自由市场的谬误：为何瑞士有25 Gbit互联网而美国没有</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48770647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 534</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48770647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 429</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that Switzerland’s fast, ubiquitous fiber is the result of deliberate public policy, infrastructure sharing, and local competition rather than a laissez-faire telecom market. It contrasts that with structural barriers in the U.S.—incumbent incentives, pole and rights-of-way issues, and regulatory choices—that have kept speeds and deployment lagging despite market rhetoric.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出，瑞士的高速光纤来自有意的公共政策、基础设施共享和地方性竞争，而不是放任的自由市场。与之相对，美国因既得利益、线路与通行权问题及监管选择，导致部署和速率长期落后。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://stefan.schueller.net/posts/the-free-market-lie/title-image.png' data-full='https://stefan.schueller.net/posts/the-free-market-lie/title-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48763035' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(45) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mathstodon.xyz/@iblech/116769502749142438' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Since Linux 6.9, LUKS suspend stopped wiping disk-encryption keys from memory</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自 Linux 6.9 起，LUKS 休眠不再从内存擦除磁盘加密密钥</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48763035' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 531</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48763035' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 222</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Linux 6.9 introduced a change where LUKS suspend no longer clears in-memory keys, trading security for faster resume. The decision highlights the ongoing tension between safeguarding encryption keys and preserving usability in sleep states.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>自 Linux 6.9 起，LUKS 休眠不再从内存中清除磁盘加密密钥，提升恢复速度的同时扩大了内存中密钥暴露的风险。这一取舍凸显了在安全性与可用性之间的持续权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mathstodon.xyz/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' data-full='https://mathstodon.xyz/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48768951' data-tags='Privacy,Legal,AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(46) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://righttointelligence.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Protect your right to run local AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>保护本地运行 AI 的权利</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48768951' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 524</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48768951' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 185</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Campaigns argue users should be allowed to run AI models locally, offline, on their own hardware without cloud dependence. That shift would boost privacy, data control, and resilience, though hardware costs and licensing remain barriers. The piece frames why this matters to developers and enthusiasts who want autonomy over their AI tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>倡导者认为人们应能在本地硬件上离线运行 AI 模型，避免对云端的依赖。这一转变能提升隐私、数据掌控和系统韧性，但硬件成本和许可证限制仍是现实障碍。文章聚焦对于希望掌控 AI 工具的开发者和爱好者为何重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://righttointelligence.org/og.png' data-full='https://righttointelligence.org/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48761118' data-tags='Career'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(47) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pradyuprasad.com/writings/how-to-ask-for-help/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How to ask for help from people who don&#x27;t know you</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如何向不认识你的人寻求帮助</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48761118' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 516</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48761118' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 79</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ask for help from people who do not know you can work if you build credibility quickly, keep requests concise, and offer value or an easy path to say yes. The guidance emphasizes clear, respectful asks and the strategic use of warm introductions to overcome friction.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>向陌生人求助往往需要先建立可信度、给出明确的请求并提供回报价值。文章给出一系列实用策略：简短有力的表述、附带背景信息、以及便于对方快速答复的具体问题。把对方从“陌生人”变成愿意帮助的对象，核心在于高效沟通和善用引介。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pradyuprasad.com/card.png' data-full='https://pradyuprasad.com/card.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48706560' data-tags='Privacy,Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(48) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/06/kids-act-would-require-age-checks-get-online' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The KIDS Act would require age checks to get online</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>KIDS Act 将强制在线年龄验证</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48706560' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 504</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48706560' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 403</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The KIDS Act proposes mandatory online age verification, aiming to shield minors but raising privacy and profiling concerns. Critics warn it could normalize surveillance and create security risks for users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>KIDS Act 提议对在线服务强制进行年龄验证，旨在保护未成年人；但也引发隐私与画像化担忧。批评者警告这可能使监控常态化并带来安全风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/ageverificationbanner-3.png' data-full='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/ageverificationbanner-3.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48786838' data-tags='Data,Legal,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(49) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://software.annas-archive.gl/AnnaArchivist/annas-archive/-/work_items/234' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google Books (or similar) all book scans – $200k bounty (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌图书（或同类）对完整书籍扫描的20万美元悬赏（2025）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48786838' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 503</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48786838' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 290</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A $200k bounty aims to incentivize scanning of an entire book corpus, similar to Google Books. This initiative could accelerate digitization, OCR accuracy, and AI training data availability, while raising licensing, copyright, and provenance questions. Expect debates over public-domain scope, fair use, and the balance between accessibility and rights.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项价值20万美元的悬赏旨在推动对整套书籍进行数字化扫描，这类似于谷歌图书的计划。此举可能加速书籍的数字化、光学字符识别的准确性，以及可供机器学习训练的数据集，但也引发版权、授权与来源等问题。公众领域、使用权与可获取性的平衡将成为讨论焦点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://software.annas-archive.gl/assets/twitter_card-570ddb06edf56a2312253c5872489847a0f385112ddbcd71ccfa1570febab5d2.jpg' data-full='https://software.annas-archive.gl/assets/twitter_card-570ddb06edf56a2312253c5872489847a0f385112ddbcd71ccfa1570febab5d2.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48753715' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(50) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://zcode.z.ai/en' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ZCode – Harness for GLM-5.2</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ZCode：GLM-5.2 的测试套件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48753715' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 503</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48753715' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 350</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ZCode releases a harness for GLM-5.2, offering an end-to-end toolkit to run prompts, collect metrics, and compare outputs. This reduces the friction of experimentation and makes benchmarking more reproducible for ML researchers and developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ZCode 推出 GLM-5.2 的测试套件，提供从输入到结果评估的一体化工具，便于执行提示、收集指标和对比输出。降低研究者的试验门槛，便于可重复的基准比较。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://zcode.z.ai/images/summary_large_image.png?v=20260628' data-full='https://zcode.z.ai/images/summary_large_image.png?v=20260628' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48745445' data-tags='Open Source,Programming,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(51) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://box2d.org/posts/2026/06/announcing-box3d/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Box3D, an open source 3D physics engine</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>宣布发布 Box3D：开源的 3D 物理引擎</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48745445' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 499</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48745445' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 118</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Box3D arrives as an open-source successor in 3D physics engines, following Box2D lineage. It aims to deliver robust rigid-body dynamics with a modular design and easier integration for games and simulations, inviting community contributions. If you build 3D physics into your project, Box3D could become a go-to option without licensing friction.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Box3D 作为 3D 物理引擎的开源继任者加入，沿袭 Box2D 的传统，目标是提供健壮的刚体动力学、模块化设计以及更易的集成，鼓励社区贡献。若你的项目需要 3D 物理，这个引擎可能成为无许可负担的首选选项。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://box2d.org/images/logo.svg' data-full='https://box2d.org/images/logo.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48734234' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(52) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://libertystreeteconomics.newyorkfed.org/2026/06/the-post-covid-decline-in-the-labor-share/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The labor share of income in the US is at its lowest post-war level</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国劳动收入份额降至战后最低水平</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48734234' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 498</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48734234' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 540</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>US labor’s share of income has declined to a postwar low, signaling that productivity gains and capital income are capturing a growing slice of economic output. The trend implies slower wage growth for workers and potential pressure on consumer demand, with implications for inequality and policy debates. Analysts see a shift in income distribution driven by automation, globalization, and pricing power.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国劳动者在国民总收入中的份额降至战后最低水平，反映生产率增益和资本收入正在分得更大份额。此趋势意味着劳动者工资增长可能放缓，消费力受压，且可能加剧收入不平等。分析师将关注结构性因素如何影响工资与投资回报的长期分配。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://libertystreeteconomics.newyorkfed.org/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/06/LSE_2026_labor-share_schuh_460.jpg' data-full='https://libertystreeteconomics.newyorkfed.org/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/06/LSE_2026_labor-share_schuh_460.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48790689' data-tags='Programming,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(53) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://unsung.aresluna.org/if-youre-a-button-you-have-one-job/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>If you&#x27;re a button, you have one job</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>按钮只有一个职责</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48790689' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 495</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48790689' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 244</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative take on the role of a button: it has one job—to trigger actions. The piece argues for proper semantics and accessibility, urging developers to reserve button elements for actions and use links where appropriate.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章强调按钮的本质是执行操作，应正确使用按钮元素以提升可访问性与可预测性，避免将用于导航的元素错用成按钮。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://unsung.aresluna.org/_media/if-youre-a-button-you-have-one-job/ogimage.png' data-full='https://unsung.aresluna.org/_media/if-youre-a-button-you-have-one-job/ogimage.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48710232' data-tags='Open Source,Privacy,Security,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(54) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/librepods-org/librepods' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Librepods: AirPods liberated</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Librepods：AirPods 自由化项目</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48710232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 491</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48710232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 179</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Librepods aims to liberate AirPods by removing vendor-imposed constraints and enabling user-controlled firmware experiments. The project advocates for ownership and privacy but may raise legal, warranty, and security questions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Librepods 通过移除厂商设定的限制、让用户能够控制固件来实现 AirPods 的自由化。该项目倡导拥有权与隐私，但也可能带来法律、保修与安全方面的问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/863717537/80b4f2f1-52f9-4c3e-b32a-779933701089' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/863717537/80b4f2f1-52f9-4c3e-b32a-779933701089' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48761229' data-tags='Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(55) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thebignewsletter.com/p/crime-pays-the-egg-bandits-made-a' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Egg Bandits Made a Thousand Times the Fine They Just Paid for Price Fixing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>蛋价共谋者缴纳的罚款远不及其收益</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48761229' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 491</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48761229' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 246</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fines for egg-price fixers are typically dwarfed by the profits they earned from the scheme. This piece argues for tougher penalties, and possibly criminal charges, to deter cartels and protect consumers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>蛋价垄断案中，罚款往往只是短期成本，远低于参与者从价格操纵中获得的长期收益。文章强调只有提高处罚力度、甚至引入刑事责任，才能真正抑制此类共谋。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48784777' data-tags='DevOps,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(56) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://peteris.rocks/blog/htop/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Explanation of everything you can see in htop/top on Linux (2019)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Linux 中 htop/top 的全部字段解读</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48784777' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 485</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48784777' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 61</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A comprehensive guide decodes every field in htop and top on Linux, explaining what each metric means and how to interpret them in practice. It helps practitioners diagnose performance bottlenecks beyond surface readings. Includes tips on CPU/memory usage, process states, and how sampling affects observations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇详尽指南逐一解释 Linux 上 htop/top 的每个字段和指标，以及如何据此解读性能信号、定位瓶颈。提供实用建议，帮助读者理解 CPU/内存、进程状态和 I/O 等数据在监控中的含义及刷新对观察的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://peteris.rocks/blog/htop/canyoukillit-before.png' data-full='https://peteris.rocks/blog/htop/canyoukillit-before.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48719485' data-tags='Space,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://investors.rocketlabcorp.com/news-releases/news-release-details/rocket-lab-acquire-iridium-historic-deal-creating-fully' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rocketlab acquires Iridium</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rocket Lab 收购 Iridium：历史性交易</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48719485' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 465</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48719485' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 307</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rocket Lab&#x27;s planned acquisition of Iridium Communications would create a vertically integrated space services company, combining launch, satellite operations, and communications. The deal signals ongoing consolidation in the space economy and could reshape small-satellite market dynamics. If approved, it may accelerate end-to-end offerings for customers building constellations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rocket Lab 将收购 Iridium，整合发射、卫星运营与通信，可能打造垂直整合的太空服务公司。此举反映了太空产业的持续整合，并可能重塑小卫星市场格局。若获批，或将推动面向星座构建的端到端解决方案加速落地。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://investors.rocketlabcorp.com/sites/g/files/knoqqb100131/themes/site/client_site/dist/images/Rocket-Lab-Logo-RGB-Reversed.png' data-full='https://investors.rocketlabcorp.com/sites/g/files/knoqqb100131/themes/site/client_site/dist/images/Rocket-Lab-Logo-RGB-Reversed.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48755005' data-tags='Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(58) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://makerspet.com/blog/building-an-open-source-robot-vacuum-meet-oomwoo/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Oomwoo, an open-source robot vacuum you build yourself</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Oomwoo：一个你自己组装的开源机器人吸尘器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48755005' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 463</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48755005' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 95</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Oomwoo is an open-source, build-it-yourself robot vacuum kit that lets hobbyists swap hardware and customize software. It showcases the growing maker movement around home robots, providing transparent designs, firmware, and code. Community-driven projects like this push for accessible robotics and faster iteration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Oomwoo 是一个可自行组装的开源机器人吸尘器套件，允许用户定制硬件与软件。它体现了家用机器人开源潮流，通过透明的硬件、固件与代码促进社区协作与快速迭代。这样的项目降低入门门槛，推动更多爱好者参与硬件创新。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://makerspet.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/oomwoo-vacuum_model_top.webp' data-full='https://makerspet.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/oomwoo-vacuum_model_top.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48747116' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(59) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hydrogenaudio.org/index.php/topic,129691.0.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FFmpeg 9.1&#x27;s new AAC encoder</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FFmpeg 9.1 的新 AAC 编码器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48747116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 443</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48747116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 144</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>FFmpeg 9.1 introduces a new AAC encoder with improved encoding quality and efficiency, offering better stereo handling and options for low-latency workflows. The update may influence streaming pipelines and compliance considerations for AAC encoding in media workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FFmpeg 9.1 带来全新的 AAC 编码器，提升了编码质量和效率，并在立体声处理与低延迟工作流方面提供改进。此更新对媒体工作流中的 AAC 编码兼容性与流式传输管线可能产生影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48782435' data-tags='Education,Career'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(60) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.marginalia.nu/log/a_135_learn/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Maybe you should learn something</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>也许你应该学点东西</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48782435' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 443</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48782435' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 204</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A quick nudge toward lifelong learning as a driver of career resilience and innovation. The piece advocates picking up new skills or domains to stay adaptable in rapidly changing tech landscapes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>鼓励以终身学习驱动职业韧性与创新，强调在快速变化的科技领域不断学习新技能的重要性。建议从新领域入手，提升跨领域适应能力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48735444' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(61) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://deepmind.google/models/gemini-image/flash-lite/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nano Banana 2 Lite</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Nano Banana 2 Lite</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48735444' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 433</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48735444' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 192</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nano Banana 2 Lite is a lightweight image model in the Gemini image lineup designed for fast, on-device inference with a smaller compute footprint. It enables faster prototyping and on-device AI work without cloud latency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Nano Banana 2 Lite 是 Gemini 图像模型家族的轻量版本，设计用于快速推理并降低计算开销。它是一款面向快速推理的轻量级图像模型，适合在本地设备上使用，减小算力需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/lb-MMov_96xz_rXSHcC3fOU91ehheRSkXmiGudc3PWnLtmB5YN1-NInY5Le9JOmY2LBIIeAUwBhlhcskmv90eSFxz8gNh2TvWcnegYoq98NmuOEL=w1200-h630-n-nu-rw' data-full='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/lb-MMov_96xz_rXSHcC3fOU91ehheRSkXmiGudc3PWnLtmB5YN1-NInY5Le9JOmY2LBIIeAUwBhlhcskmv90eSFxz8gNh2TvWcnegYoq98NmuOEL=w1200-h630-n-nu-rw' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48734699' data-tags='Energy,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(62) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.404media.co/henrico-virginia-datacenter-energy-cost-email/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>County with 37 Data Centers Asks Schools to &#x27;Conserve Electricity&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>拥有37座数据中心的县呼吁学校节电</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48734699' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 421</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48734699' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 185</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A county housing 37 data centers is asking schools to conserve electricity amid rising power costs. The move highlights how large-scale digital infrastructure can strain public energy budgets and grid demand, pushing districts to optimize usage and scheduling. It also underscores broader questions about energy pricing, resilience, and the cost of data-driven services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>拥有37座数据中心的县在能源成本上升之际呼吁学校节电。这一举措凸显大型数字基础设施对公共能源预算和电网负担的影响，促使学区优化用电与时段安排，也暴露出能源定价与系统韧性的更广泛问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/0f/76/0f76b548-bc58-4f25-abc3-3f5ebca07da4/content/images/size/w1200/2026/06/photo-1610028290816-5d937a395a49.jpeg' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/0f/76/0f76b548-bc58-4f25-abc3-3f5ebca07da4/content/images/size/w1200/2026/06/photo-1610028290816-5d937a395a49.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48779683' data-tags='Security,Politics,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(63) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://citizenlab.ca/research/member-of-committee-investigating-spyware-hacked-with-pegasus/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Espionage Against the European Parliament</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>针对欧洲议会的间谍活动</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48779683' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 418</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48779683' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 129</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A member of a European Parliament committee investigating spyware was hacked with Pegasus, underscoring how even high level policymakers face sophisticated surveillance. The incident highlights the espionage risks at EU institutions and the need for stronger protections and incident response.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧洲议会调查间谍软件的委员会成员遭遇 Pegasus 攻击，凸显高级政治人物也可能成为高端监控目标。此事件暴露欧盟机构面临的情报威胁和对策需求，以及对隐私保护与事件响应的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://citizenlab.ca/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/olive-oil-report-web.jpg' data-full='https://citizenlab.ca/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/olive-oil-report-web.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48750710' data-tags='Programming,Education,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(64) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.demofox.org/2026/07/01/what-to-learn-to-be-a-graphics-programmer/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What to learn to be a graphics programmer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>成为图形程序员要学什么</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48750710' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 417</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48750710' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 240</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical roadmap that covers the fundamentals: math (linear algebra, vectors, matrices), GPU pipelines, shading languages, and APIs (OpenGL/Vulkan/DirectX). It emphasizes building real projects (ray tracers, small engines) and a portfolio to demonstrate skill progression, plus tips on debugging shader code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇实用路线图列出成为图形程序员的核心要点，包括线性代数等数学基础、GPU 管线、着色语言以及 OpenGL/Vulkan/DirectX 等 API。作者建议以小型引擎或光线追踪实现等实战项目来积累作品集，附带调试着色器的实战要点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.demofox.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/image.png' data-full='https://blog.demofox.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(65) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.blog/changelog/2026-07-01-kimi-k2-7-is-now-available-in-github-copilot/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kimi K2.7 Code is generally available in GitHub Copilot</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GitHub Copilot now broadly supports Kimi K2.7 code, expanding access to advanced AI-assisted coding capabilities. This milestone signals the maturing ecosystem for AI copilots, with implications for how developers source, trust, and review AI-generated code, alongside licensing and privacy considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GitHub Copilot 现已广泛支持 Kimi K2.7 代码，扩展了对高级 AI 代码助手的使用。此里程碑标志着 AI 助手生态的成熟，影响开发者对 AI 生成代码的获取、信任与审查，以及相关的许可和隐私问题。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://torrentfreak.com/european-isps-want-rightsholders-held-accountable-for-overblocking-damage/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>European ISPs Want Rightsholders Held Accountable for Overblocking Damage</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧洲 ISP 希望权利方对过度拦截造成的损害负责</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48721072' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 412</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48721072' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 124</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>European ISPs are pushing back on overblocking by rights holders and seeking accountability for damages, signaling ongoing tension between enforcement and civil liberties. The push could influence EU policy on safe harbors, liability, and the economics of content blocking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧洲的运营商正就权利方过度拦截造成的损害追究责任，凸显执法与公民自由之间的持续冲突。这一举动可能影响欧盟关于安全港、法律责任及内容拦截经济性的相关政策。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://torrentfreak.com/images/wall-feat.jpg' data-full='https://torrentfreak.com/images/wall-feat.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(67) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.sedaily.com/international/2026/06/29/samsung-sk-hynix-micron-sued-in-us-over-memory-price-fixing' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Samsung, SK Hynix, Micron Sued in US over Memory Price Fixing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>三星、SK 海力士、美光在美国因内存价格垄断被起诉</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48718102' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 412</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48718102' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 188</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A US lawsuit accuses Samsung, SK Hynix, and Micron of memory price fixing, potentially affecting pricing dynamics and competition in the memory market. The case adds to antitrust scrutiny of chipmakers and could influence regulatory actions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国对三星、SK海力士和美光提起的价格垄断诉讼指控其串通操纵内存价格，可能改变市场定价与竞争格局，并增加对芯片厂商的监管压力。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(68) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pudding.cool/2026/06/menu-story/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>5k menus from the New York Public Library’s Buttolph Collection (1880-1920)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>纽约公共图书馆 Buttolph 收藏的五千份菜单（1880-1920）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48707763' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 411</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48707763' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 107</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The NYPL’s Buttolph Collection includes around 5,000 menus from 1880–1920, offering a rich dataset for historians and data scientists. Text mining these menus reveals shifts in cuisine, pricing, and social norms across a transformative era.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纽约公共图书馆的 Buttolph 收藏约五千份菜单，覆盖 1880–1920 年代，为历史研究者与数据科学家提供丰富的数据集。通过对菜单中的菜名、价格与语言进行挖掘，可以揭示这一变革时期的饮食、定价与社会风俗的变化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pudding.cool/2026/06/menu-story/assets/social-facebook.jpg' data-full='https://pudding.cool/2026/06/menu-story/assets/social-facebook.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(71) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/jamesob/local-llm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Jamesob&#x27;s guide to running SOTA LLMs locally</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48775921' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 403</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48775921' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 181</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical guide to running state of the art LLMs on local hardware. It covers model selection, quantization, offloading, and tooling to optimize memory and compute, enabling researchers to prototype without cloud dependence while noting tradeoffs in accuracy and resource use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一份在本地硬件上运行 SOTA 大语言模型的实用指南，涵盖模型选择、量化、离载与工具链等方面的内存和算力优化。帮助研究者在不依赖云端的情况下进行原型开发，同时指明精度与资源成本之间的取舍。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48768992' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(72) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://scottaaronson.blog/?p=9902' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An American Privacy Emergency</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国的隐私紧急状态</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48768992' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 402</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48768992' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 131</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The essay warns that current U.S. laws and enforcement are failing to contain pervasive data collection and surveillance, arguing for urgent legislative and regulatory reforms. It emphasizes that without stronger privacy protections and accountability, harms from misuse of personal data will keep accelerating across commercial and governmental actors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中警告美国现有法律和执法未能遏制无处不在的数据收集与监控，呼吁紧急的立法与监管改革。文章强调若无更强的隐私保护与问责机制，个人数据滥用带来的伤害将在商业与政府层面持续加剧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scottaaronson.blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/image-2.png' data-full='https://scottaaronson.blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/image-2.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(73) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dam.stanford.edu/memory-prices.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Historical memory prices 1960-2026</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48710092' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 399</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48710092' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 156</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Stanford memory prices history chart tracks the long-run decline in memory prices, including RAM and storage. The data show dramatic declines over decades, with spikes tied to supply shocks and tech cycles, informing hardware budgeting and product lifecycles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这份数据集揭示了记忆体（如 RAM）价格在 1960 年到 2026 年的长期下降趋势，并受供给冲击与科技周期的影响出现波动。这些数据为硬件预算与产品生命周期提供了参考。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48761536' data-tags='AI,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(75) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://japannews.yomiuri.co.jp/science-nature/technology/20260306-314930/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI can&#x27;t be listed as inventor on patent applications, Japan&#x27;s top court rules</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>日本最高法院裁定 AI 不能作为发明人列入专利申请</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48761536' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 394</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48761536' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 207</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Japan&#x27;s top court rules AI can&#x27;t be listed as inventor on patent applications. That means humans must be named as inventors, keeping a clear line between human ingenuity and AI contributions with implications for IP governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>日本最高法院裁定 AI 不能作为发明人出现在专利申请中，意味着发明人必须是人类。这对 AI 辅助的发明及未来的知识产权治理有现实影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48707673' data-tags='Privacy,Security,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(76) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.engadget.com/2203000/flock-cameras-recording-license-plate/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Flock cameras track more than your license plate, and they&#x27;re spreading fast</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Flock 摄像头不仅追踪车牌，扩张速度惊人</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48707673' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 391</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48707673' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 324</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New reporting suggests Flock cameras collect more than license plate data, and deployments are rapidly expanding across cities. Privacy advocates warn that expanding surveillance features could track individuals beyond their vehicles, prompting regulatory scrutiny. The rapid rollouts could reshape public-space monitoring norms and vendor expectations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Flock 摄像头的监控范围已超越车牌数据，且部署正迅速在各城市扩张。隐私倡导者警告，日益扩大的监控能力可能超出车辆范围，触发监管审查。快速扩张可能重塑公共空间的监控规范以及厂商预期。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.engadget.com/img/gallery/flock-cameras-track-more-than-your-license-plate-and-theyre-spreading-fast/l-intro-1782508847.jpg' data-full='https://www.engadget.com/img/gallery/flock-cameras-track-more-than-your-license-plate-and-theyre-spreading-fast/l-intro-1782508847.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
