<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://popcar.bearblog.dev/its-about-ownership/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>It&#x27;s not about physical vs. digital games, it&#x27;s about ownership</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不是实体/数字游戏之争，而是关于所有权的问题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48794750' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 389</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48794750' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 291</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that ownership—not format—drives value in gaming, covering DRM, portability, and platform control that shape how players access and keep games.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出，游戏真正的分水岭在于所有权，而非物理还是数字的形式；包括数字版权管理、跨平台可移植性和平台控制等因素，决定玩家对游戏的长期拥有与访问。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A searchable directory aggregating over 22,000 products from worker-owned co-ops, making ethically produced goods easier to discover. The project lowers barriers for consumers and researchers to support cooperative businesses and study their impact.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个聚合了来自员工共同所有制合作社的2.2万多件产品的可检索目录，帮助用户更容易发现符合伦理的商品。该项目降低了消费者与研究者支持合作社企业的门槛，并便于研究其影响。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Zanagrams is a Show HN project that offers a fast, minimalist platform for generating and solving word anagrams. Its focused UX and emphasis on keyboard-first interaction illustrate how small, well-executed ideas can stand out on Hacker News.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Zanagrams 是一个 Show HN 项目，提供一个快速、简洁的单词变位生成与解答平台。它以聚焦的用户体验和键盘优先的交互方式，展示了一个小而精的点子如何在 Hacker News 上脱颖而出。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(80) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/FractalFir/crustc' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>crustc: entirety of `rustc`, translated to C</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>crustc：将整个 rustc 翻译为 C</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48768464' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 385</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48768464' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 86</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>crustc is an ambitious project that translates the entirety of the Rust compiler into C, aiming to make rustc more portable and easier to bootstrap on unusual toolchains. The repo is experimental but useful as a study in compiler architecture, portability trade-offs, and alternate bootstrapping strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>crustc 是个雄心勃勃的工程，把整个 Rust 编译器翻译成 C，目标是提升 rustc 在非常规工具链上的可移植性和引导能力。该项目处于实验性阶段，但对研究编译器架构、可移植性权衡以及替代引导策略很有价值。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/456846db12f319cf54a264ee3398c1047298bf6a983329296bb4009c0c6f059c/FractalFir/crustc' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/456846db12f319cf54a264ee3398c1047298bf6a983329296bb4009c0c6f059c/FractalFir/crustc' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Leanstral 1.5 broadens automated proof capabilities, increasing the amount of verifiable proofs and improving tooling. This lowers the bar for formal verification and could impact software safety and correctness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Leanstral 1.5 扩展了自动证明能力，证明产出增加、工具链更完善。此举可能降低形式化验证门槛，对软件安全和正确性有潜在帮助。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A native graphical shell for SSH enables GUI-based remote sessions, simplifying workflows for users who prefer visuals over a terminal. It demonstrates how GUI front-ends can complement SSH by integrating file transfer, terminal emulation, and session management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SSH 的原生图形界面外壳让远程会话以图形界面形式展开，提升偏好图形界面的用户的工作流。该方案展示了图形前端如何与 SSH 结合，提供文件传输、终端仿真和会话管理等一体化功能。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(83) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mathstodon.xyz/@mjd/115096720350507897' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The primary purpose of code review is to find code that will be hard to maintain</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>代码评审的首要目的是发现难以维护的代码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48759870' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 361</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48759870' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 174</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Code review should primarily reveal maintainability risks rather than only catching bugs. By focusing on readability, modularity, and clear interfaces, teams reduce future debt and speed advancement.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>代码评审的核心目标是揭示那些后续难以维护的设计与实现问题，如高耦合、模糊的接口和不明显的依赖关系。通过关注可维护性，团队可减少技术债务并提升长期开发效率。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mathstodon.xyz/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' data-full='https://mathstodon.xyz/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Advances in hardware and algorithms are delivering more computational performance per dollar for AI workloads, making larger models more accessible.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>硬件与算法的进步让单位成本上的计算性能持续提升，使得更大规模的模型也更易取得。文中提到包括基于 AMD 的系统和高效推理在内的进步，推动更便宜的实验和落地应用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(85) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://johnsalvatier.org/blog/2017/reality-has-a-surprising-amount-of-detail' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Reality has a surprising amount of detail (2017)</a></p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48789428' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(86) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/openai/codex/issues/30364' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GPT-5.5 Codex reasoning-token clustering may be leading to degraded performance</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPT-5.5 Codex 推理令牌聚类可能导致性能下降</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48789428' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 348</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48789428' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 144</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers report that clustering reasoning tokens in GPT-5.5 Codex-style workflows may degrade performance, suggesting a misalignment between token organization and inference efficiency. The finding has implications for how developers architect long-chain reasoning or tool-assisted prompts in production systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究指出在 GPT-5.5/Codex 风格工作流中对推理令牌进行聚类可能降低性能，表明令牌组织方式与推理效率之间存在错配。这一发现对在生产系统中设计长链推理或工具辅助提示的架构设计具有重要意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/0277b862307eff90a0e3d10811a2cfd992db400cde9a352dc23fee186b6770d4/openai/codex/issues/30364' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/0277b862307eff90a0e3d10811a2cfd992db400cde9a352dc23fee186b6770d4/openai/codex/issues/30364' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(88) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/i/status/2071277885646868536' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Instagram is incorporating users&#x27; photos in ads for Meta Glasses</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48719027' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 346</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48719027' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 147</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Instagram plans to use users’ photos in ads for Meta Glasses, raising concerns about consent and image rights in brand campaigns. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Instagram 将用户照片用于 Meta 眼镜广告，引发对同意与形象权在品牌推广中的问题。此举体现了用户内容的商业化，同时让隐私问题在社交媒体驱动的硬件体验中成为焦点。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48744232' data-tags='Health,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(89) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thefocalpoints.com/p/new-study-frog-derived-gut-bacterium' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Frog-derived gut bacterium eradicates tumors in mice</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>来自青蛙肠道的细菌在小鼠体内根除肿瘤</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48744232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 346</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48744232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 196</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A gut bacterium derived from frogs has eliminated tumors in mice, suggesting a microbiome-based approach to cancer therapy. While results are preclinical, the study highlights how manipulating gut microbes could complement existing treatments and prompts further mechanism research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>来自青蛙肠道的细菌在小鼠身上实现肿瘤清除，显示了微生物组干预在癌症治疗中的潜力。尽管尚处于前期实验阶段，该研究凸显了通过调控肠道微生物来辅助现有治疗的可能性，并需要进一步机制研究。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!Cc-z!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F1e9690ca-bd6f-4f8c-8ee1-8a1f1ecd76a2_2409x1314.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!Cc-z!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F1e9690ca-bd6f-4f8c-8ee1-8a1f1ecd76a2_2409x1314.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48746914' data-tags='Web,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(90) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/monetization-gateway/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Monetization Gateway: Charge for any resource behind Cloudflare via x402</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>货币化网关上线</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48746914' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 346</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48746914' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 250</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare launches Monetization Gateway, a centralized service to monetize online content through payments, gating, and access control. It streamlines revenue workflows for publishers, reducing integration friction and enabling more consistent monetization across sites. The product highlights the industry’s move toward turnkey monetization tools for the open web.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 推出 Monetization Gateway，提供一个集中化服务，通过支付、门控和访问控制来实现内容变现。它简化了出版商的收入工作流，降低集成难度，并实现跨站点更一致的变现。该产品凸显了在线内容领域向“端到端”变现工具的趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/7wA6Z3Df13eeHZkxM3Fokt/088b64cd947100b70bd0e49c845fc984/Announcing_the_Monetization_Gateway-_charge_for_any_resource_behind_Cloudflare_via_x402-OG.png' data-full='https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/7wA6Z3Df13eeHZkxM3Fokt/088b64cd947100b70bd0e49c845fc984/Announcing_the_Monetization_Gateway-_charge_for_any_resource_behind_Cloudflare_via_x402-OG.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48754963' data-tags='Health,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(91) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ubc.ca/2026/06/mrna-vaccines-are-safe-effective-and-full-of-promise/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Global review confirms mRNA vaccines are safe, effective and full of promise</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>全球综述确认 mRNA 疫苗安全、有效且充满前景</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48754963' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 335</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48754963' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 448</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A global synthesis concludes that mRNA vaccines are safe, effective, and hold promise for tackling current and future diseases. The review underscores robust safety data across diverse populations and highlights potential for rapid development and deployment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>全球综合评估显示 mRNA 疫苗安全、有效，且在应对当前及未来疾病方面前景广阔。评估强调在多样人群中的强健安全性数据，并凸显其在快速开发和部署方面的潜力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ubc.ca/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/ubc_20210713_pj_9100_feature-image.jpg' data-full='https://news.ubc.ca/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/ubc_20210713_pj_9100_feature-image.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(92) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ngrok.com/blog/i-ported-kubernetes-to-the-browser' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I ported Kubernetes to the browser</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我把 Kubernetes 移植到了浏览器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48738985' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 330</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48738985' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 102</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Porting Kubernetes to the browser demonstrates running components in WebAssembly or similar in-browser runtimes, enabling offline demos or teaching orchestration concepts. It surfaces challenges around networking, storage, and security when moving cluster infrastructure into the client.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>把 Kubernetes 移植到浏览器演示了在浏览器运行组件的可能性，便于离线演示和教学编排概念。也暴露了将集群基础设施移至客户端时在网络、存储和安全方面的挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我必须离开：谷歌管理层已丧失道德指南针</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48758970' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 330</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48758970' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 251</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者直言对谷歌管理层的道德指引失去信任，认为决策与企业文化偏离价值底线。呼吁更清晰的领导原则与改进行动，透露自己即将离开的原因。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(94) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/world/china/alibaba-ban-claude-code-workplace-over-alleged-backdoor-risks-source-says-2026-07-03/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Alibaba to ban Claude Code in workplace over alleged backdoor risks, source says</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>阿里巴巴拟在工作场所禁用 Claude Code，称存在后门风险</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48772443' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 329</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48772443' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 278</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Alibaba reportedly banned Claude Code in workplace use over alleged backdoor risks, signaling growing caution around enterprise tools. This move could influence how other large employers approach AI coding assistants.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>阿里巴巴据称因后门风险，在工作场所禁用 Claude Code，凸显企业在 AI 编码工具上的风险管理日益严格。这一举措可能影响其他企业对 AI 编码助手的采用。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48765663' data-tags='Gaming,Entertainment'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(95) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.zachtronics.com/exapunks/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Exapunks (2018)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Exapunks（2018）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48765663' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 327</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48765663' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 120</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Exapunks is a 2018 puzzle game from Zachtronics that tasks players with coding-like instructions to hack into systems. Its meticulous, systems-thinking puzzles made it a cult classic among players who enjoy programming challenges and cyberpunk lore.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Exapunks 是 Zachtronics 于2018年推出的一款以编码风格指令来入侵系统的解谜游戏。其严谨的系统思维难题和赛博朋克氛围让它在喜欢编程挑战的玩家中成为小众经典。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.zachtronics.com/img/logo-white.png' data-full='https://www.zachtronics.com/img/logo-white.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48735633' data-tags='Culture,Education,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(96) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://unix.foo/posts/last-people-who-know-how-it-works/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We Are the Last People Who Know How It Works</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们是最后一个知道它如何运作的人</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48735633' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 325</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48735633' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 268</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece argues that increasing complexity in software and systems means only a shrinking circle truly understands how things work. It warns about fragile infrastructure built on tacit knowledge and advocates better documentation, tooling, and knowledge sharing to reduce risk. The takeaway: preserve institutional memory before it&#x27;s lost.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章认为，软件与系统日益复杂，真正懂得运作原理的人越来越少，依赖隐性知识使基础设施变得脆弱。作者呼吁加强文档、工具和知识共享，降低运维风险，提醒要在知识流失前留住记忆。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48732056' data-tags='Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(97) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.knopper.net/knoppix/index-en.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Knoppix</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Knoppix</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48732056' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 325</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48732056' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 116</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Knoppix, the pioneer live Linux distribution, is surveyed—its approach to hardware autodetection and running from removable media influenced modern portability and recovery workflows. The piece traces why Knoppix mattered for the era of live CDs and USB booting. It offers context for current portable Linux tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Knoppix 作为开创性实时/现场运行的 Linux 发行版的概览，展示其硬件自检测与从可移动介质启动的理念如何影响现代便携与救援场景。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.knopper.net/pics/de.gif' data-full='https://www.knopper.net/pics/de.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48785485' data-tags='Security,Data,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(99) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/74066' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Potential session/cache leakage between workspace instances or consumer accounts</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>工作区实例或消费账户间的会话/缓存泄漏潜在风险</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48785485' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 311</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48785485' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 129</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A bug report highlights potential session or cache leakage across Claude Code workspace instances or consumer accounts, threatening data isolation in multi-tenant deployments. If real, it could let one user access another&#x27;s session data or cached prompts. Mitigations focus on stricter tenant isolation, scoped caching, shorter TTLs, and robust audit trails.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于 Claude Code 的工作区实例之间或消费账户之间可能出现会话/缓存泄漏的问题报道，暴露多租户环境的数据隔离风险。若属实，可能让一个用户访问到另一用户的会话数据或缓存提示。应对措施包括更严格的租户隔离、缓存作用域控制、缩短生存期以及完善的审计日志。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/2cdc39bbb7c20b8648c2761682b6515313e35bc73132ba817d448163fd3a49ec/anthropics/claude-code/issues/74066' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/2cdc39bbb7c20b8648c2761682b6515313e35bc73132ba817d448163fd3a49ec/anthropics/claude-code/issues/74066' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(100) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://wordgard.net/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Wordgard: In-browser rich-text editor from the creator of ProseMirror</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>WordGard：来自 ProseMirror 创作者的新浏览器端富文本编辑器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48772573' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 307</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48772573' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 102</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>WordGard is a new in-browser rich-text editor by the ProseMirror creator, aiming to streamline building modern editing experiences with collaboration and formatting features. It promises a modular architecture and ergonomic APIs that could accelerate web-based content tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>WordGard 是 ProseMirror 作者推出的全新浏览器端富文本编辑器，旨在简化现代协作与排版功能的编辑体验开发。它承诺模块化架构与易用 API，可能加速网页内容工具的开发。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Leanstral 1.5 更新带来性能提升与扩展功能，使其在轻量级语言模型部署中更易于执行指令式任务。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48776464' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(102) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/teamchong/pxpipe' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>60% Fable cost cut by converting code to images and having the model OCR it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过将代码转换为图像并让模型进行 OCR，Fable 成本降低 60%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48776464' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 303</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48776464' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 98</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A cost-cutting experiment shows that encoding code as images and using OCR can dramatically reduce token usage in Fable, cutting costs by about 60%. The approach highlights an unusual, input-format hack to optimize model usage, with trade-offs in accuracy and workflow.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 Fable 的成本优化尝试中，將代码转成图像再用模型 OCR，大约降低 60% 的成本。这一做法揭示了通过输入形式的创新来降低模型成本的潜在可能性，同时也伴随准确性和工作流的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/23eb39244f071f14d64e6d0e113921c5bc8a1adfa0b90f9831f0deaa203053c8/teamchong/pxpipe' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/23eb39244f071f14d64e6d0e113921c5bc8a1adfa0b90f9831f0deaa203053c8/teamchong/pxpipe' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(103) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tidal.com/ai-policy' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tidal AI Policy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tidal AI 政策</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48718840' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 302</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48718840' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 342</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tidal AI Policy outlines how the music streaming service uses AI, including training data rights and permissioning for artist content. The policy signals how the industry is grappling with copyright, royalties, and user privacy as AI-generated music becomes more common.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Tidal 的 AI 政策界定了音乐流媒体在人工智能领域的使用规则，包括训练数据权利与艺术家内容授权。该政策折射出行业在 AI 生成音乐日益增多的背景下，如何处理版权、版税和用户隐私等问题。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CERN 向大型强子对撞机道别，进入长期停机3阶段</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48723484' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 302</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48723484' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 97</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CERN begins Long Shutdown 3, a multi-year maintenance and upgrades window for the LHC; essential for addressing hardware aging and enabling next-gen experiments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CERN 开始长期停机3阶段，进行 LHC 的维护和升级，旨在解决硬件老化并为下一轮实验做准备。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(105) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/google/copybara' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google copybara: moving code between repositories</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Google Copybara：跨代码库迁移代码的工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48740698' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 296</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48740698' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Copybara automates code migration across repositories with customizable transformations and history preservation. It helps teams maintain consistent workflows when moving assets, enabling cleaner separation of projects and easier collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Copybara 能在不同代码库之间自动迁移代码，支持自定义转换并保留提交历史。它帮助团队在跨仓库迁移时保持工作流的一致性，使项目分离更清晰、协作更高效。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The EU’s antitrust ruling against Google stands after an appeal, highlighting ongoing regulatory pressure on tech giants in Europe. The decision reinforces the EU’s willingness to enforce competition rules in digital markets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟对谷歌的反垄断裁决在上诉后维持，凸显欧盟对科技巨头持续施压的监管态势。此决定重申欧盟在数字市场执行竞争规则的意愿。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(108) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cbsnews.com/detroit/news/workplace-boundaries-act-employees-after-hours/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Michigan bill would bar employers from requiring after-hours coms with workers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>密歇根州法案将禁止雇主强制工人在工作时间之外沟通</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48707769' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 281</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48707769' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 221</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Michigan bill would bar employers from requiring after-hours communications with workers, protecting personal time and reducing after-hours monitoring pressure. If enacted, the law could shape workplace norms in tech and other sectors, influencing how organizations handle responsiveness and remote work expectations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项密歇根州法案拟禁止雇主强制工人在工作时间之外进行沟通，以保护个人休息时间并缓解下班后监控的压力。若通过，该法案可能改变科技及其他行业的工作场所规范，影响公司对响应与远程工作的要求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://assets1.cbsnewsstatic.com/hub/i/r/2026/06/24/9b48b90e-f513-4e46-8570-c5c84176a82d/thumbnail/1200x630/590a05cc61fb964846830134064fc5d1/gettyimages-2218579503.jpg' data-full='https://assets1.cbsnewsstatic.com/hub/i/r/2026/06/24/9b48b90e-f513-4e46-8570-c5c84176a82d/thumbnail/1200x630/590a05cc61fb964846830134064fc5d1/gettyimages-2218579503.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(109) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.arxiv.org/2026/06/30/arxivs-next-chapter/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ArXiv&#x27;s Next Chapter</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ArXiv outlines its plan for the next chapter, focusing on governance, tooling improvements, and sustainable operations. The post emphasizes scaling moderation, API enhancements, and community governance to support open-access research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ArXiv 公布未来发展路线，聚焦治理、工具改进与长期可持续运营。文章强调扩大内容审查与元数据/API 的改进，以及社区治理，以支持开放获取的研究。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(110) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.flipper.net/future-of-flipper-zero-development/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The future of Flipper Zero development</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Flipper Zero 开发的未来</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48796552' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 279</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48796552' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 116</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Outlines the roadmap for Flipper Zero hardware-hacking firmware, including new modules, security enhancements, and community-driven features; notes safety concerns and responsible use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>概述 Flipper Zero 的开发路线图，涵盖固件、新模块、安保改善以及社区驱动的功能，同时提出对安全与负责任使用的关注。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48718863' data-tags='Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(111) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fergusfinn.com/blog/what-happens-when-you-run-a-gpu-kernel/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What happens when you run a CUDA kernel?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>运行 CUDA 内核时会发生什么？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48718863' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 278</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48718863' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Launching a CUDA kernel schedules thousands of threads across blocks on GPU cores, using SIMT execution with warps and shared memory. Understanding occupancy, memory access patterns, and synchronization is key to performance, while common pitfalls include race conditions and poor memory coalescing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CUDA 内核启动后，GPU 将在多个流处理器上按块和线程调度执行，使用 SIMT 模型、warp 及共享内存。提升性能的关键在于把握占用率、内存访问模式与同步机制，常见坑包括竞态条件与内存访问不当造成的性能损失。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://fergusfinn.com/_astro/cover.jG4AhdiB.jpg' data-full='https://fergusfinn.com/_astro/cover.jG4AhdiB.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48744606' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(112) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://easyoptouts.com/guides/apple-hide-my-email-is-leaking-email-addresses' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple &#x27;Hide My Email&#x27; vulnerability reveals peoples&#x27; real email addresses</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果 Hide My Email 漏洞暴露真实邮箱地址</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48744606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 278</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48744606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 80</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A vulnerability in Apple&#x27;s Hide My Email can reveal users&#x27; real email addresses under certain flows, compromising the privacy promise of the feature. It highlights edge-case gaps in privacy abstractions and the need for quick fixes and transparency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果的 Hide My Email 在某些流程下可能泄露用户真实邮箱地址，削弱该隐私功能的承诺。此缺陷暴露了隐私抽象的边缘场景漏洞，需迅速修复并提高透明度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://easyoptouts.com/og-logo.png' data-full='https://easyoptouts.com/og-logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48776035' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(113) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://interconnected.org/home/2026/07/03/factories' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Factories are just rooms</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>工厂不过是空间</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48776035' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 277</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48776035' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 121</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative reframing: factories as simple spaces where work happens. Efficiency, the piece argues, comes from layout, processes, and people rather than flashy machinery.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文将工厂重新理解为仅仅是工作场所，作者认为效率来自空间布置、工序流程和人员协调，而非炫目的新设备。它促使人们重新思考制造业，把问题放在布局与约束上，而非技术噱头。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://interconnected.org/home/2026/07/03/factories.png?v=1' data-full='https://interconnected.org/home/2026/07/03/factories.png?v=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(114) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://longcat.chat/blog/longcat-2.0/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LongCat-2.0, a large-scale MoE model with 1.6T total and 48B Active</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LongCat-2.0：总参数1.6万亿、活跃参数48亿的超大规模MoE模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48727116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 276</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48727116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 82</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LongCat-2.0 raises the bar with a 1.6T total-parameter MoE model and 48B active parameters, showcasing sparse activation to scale AI workloads efficiently.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LongCat-2.0 以总参数1.6万亿、活跃参数48亿的MoE模型为标杆，展示通过稀疏激活来高效扩展AI工作负载。文章介绍了架构、训练规模以及在自然语言处理等领域的潜在应用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s3plus.meituan.net/aigc-media-resources/longcat/big-cat-logo.png' data-full='https://s3plus.meituan.net/aigc-media-resources/longcat/big-cat-logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(115) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://zcode.z.ai/cn' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ZCode: Claude Code from the Makers of GLM</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ZCode：GLM 创作者推出的 Claude Code</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48751752' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 276</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48751752' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ZCode is Claude Code, a coding-oriented model from the makers of GLM, aiming to assist developers with code generation and explanation. Early demonstrations show promise but also reveal limitations in handling complex logic and unusual syntax. It could become a competitive developer tool if integrated well with workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ZCode 是 Claude Code，由 GLM 创作者推出，旨在帮助开发者进行代码生成与讲解。初步演示显示了一定潜力，同时也暴露在处理复杂逻辑和非标准语法时的局限性。如果与工作流集成良好，未来可能成为有力的开发者工具。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://zcode.z.ai/images/summary_large_image.png?v=20260628' data-full='https://zcode.z.ai/images/summary_large_image.png?v=20260628' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48779454' data-tags='Open Source,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(116) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/searxng/searxng' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SearXNG: A free internet metasearch engine</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SearXNG：一个免费的互联网元搜索引擎</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48779454' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 274</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48779454' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 78</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SearXNG is a free, open source metasearch engine that aggregates results from multiple sources while preserving user privacy. It is a modern fork with improved privacy defaults and straightforward self hosting, appealing to privacy minded researchers and admins.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SearXNG 是一个免费且开源的元搜索引擎，汇聚多家源端结果并强调用户隐私。作为现代分支，它提供更严格的隐私默认和便于自托管的架构，适合注重隐私的研究者与系统管理员。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f6a680d5206b6eb3a9a07f46c686877f51616131619e261f17ba8afd8e9780f6/searxng/searxng' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f6a680d5206b6eb3a9a07f46c686877f51616131619e261f17ba8afd8e9780f6/searxng/searxng' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48706389' data-tags='Security,Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(117) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/tech/947157/passports-data-breach-cannabis-club-systems-nefos-puffpal' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>One million passports leaked online</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>100万份护照信息泄露上网</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48706389' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 273</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48706389' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 157</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A data breach exposed more than a million passport records online, highlighting the risk of identity theft and the need for stronger data protections in government and corporate databases. The leak intensifies calls for better breach disclosure, encryption, and access controls across identity documents.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>公开的数据泄露暴露了超过一百万份护照记录，凸显身份盗用风险以及政府与企业在身份信息保护方面的薄弱环节。此事促使人们加强对数据泄露披露、加密与访问控制等措施的需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cambridgeanalytica.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/passports-driver-licenses-exposed-public-internet-2026.jpg' data-full='https://cambridgeanalytica.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/passports-driver-licenses-exposed-public-internet-2026.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48740971' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(118) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://aakash.substack.com/p/why-jet-engines-arent-made-in-china' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why jet engines aren&#x27;t made in China</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何喷气发动机不会在中国大规模生产</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48740971' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 273</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48740971' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 242</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Jet engine production requires precision manufacturing, certification, and a global supply chain; building full-scale jet engine manufacturing domestically is costly and complex, explaining why it&#x27;s not common in China.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>喷气发动机的生产依赖于高精密制造、严格认证和全球化的供应链，国内要建立完整产能成本高、周期长，因此难以在短期内实现大规模本土化生产。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48758048' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(119) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://davidbessis.substack.com/p/the-fall-of-the-theorem-economy' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The fall of the theorem economy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>定理经济的衰落</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48758048' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 268</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48758048' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 115</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that the so-called theorem economy, which prioritizes formal proofs and guarantees, may be losing its primacy in tech and science. It advocates pragmatic engineering, testing, and real-world outcomes over pursuit of perfect formal guarantees.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为以形式化证明和保证为核心的“定理经济”可能正在失去主导地位。倡导务实的工程实践、测试与实际结果，避免过度追求完美的形式化保证。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48744655' data-tags='Privacy,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(120) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cleberg.net/blog/internet.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A retrospective of my time on the internet</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我的网络时光回顾</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48744655' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 266</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48744655' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 293</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal retrospection on the author&#x27;s time on the internet, reflecting on how online life shaped thinking, privacy, and career. It distills lessons on mindful consumption, community, and reclaiming time from digital noise.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者对自己在互联网上的经历进行个人回顾，反思线上生活如何影响思考、隐私与职业。文章总结了关于自我约束、社区互动以及从数字噪声中夺回时间的经验教训。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.vice.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2019/08/1565048739851-image3.png' data-full='https://www.vice.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2019/08/1565048739851-image3.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48769639' data-tags='Web,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(121) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://webkit.org/blog/18136/introducing-the-safari-mcp-server-for-web-developers/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Safari MCP server for web developers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>面向网页开发者的 Safari MCP 服务器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48769639' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 264</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48769639' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 74</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple&#x27;s WebKit team introduces the Safari MCP Server for web developers to test MCP-based features in a local environment. It accelerates debugging and iteration by letting developers pair their workflows with Safari&#x27;s tooling, improving support for new web capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果 WebKit 团队为网页开发者推出 Safari MCP 服务器，方便在本地环境测试 MCP 特性。通过与 Safari 的工具链协同，提升调试效率和迭代速度，帮助开发者更好地支持新的网页能力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://webkit.org/wp-content/themes/webkit/images/preview-card.jpg' data-full='https://webkit.org/wp-content/themes/webkit/images/preview-card.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48780870' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(122) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.exeter.ac.uk/faculty-of-environment-science-and-economy/giant-trees-have-no-trouble-pumping-water-to-top-branches/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Giant trees have no trouble pumping water to top branches: new research</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>巨树向最高枝条输送水分毫不吃力：最新研究</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48780870' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 263</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48780870' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 116</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New research shows giant trees can efficiently pump water to the highest branches, revealing surprising tree hydraulics. The findings inform models of forest resilience and carbon cycling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新研究显示巨树能高效将水输送到最高枝条，揭示树木水力系统的惊人能力。研究结果有助于改进对森林干旱韧性和碳循环的建模。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.exeter.ac.uk/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/A-57m-tall-Dipterocarp-tree-with-a-double-rope-technique-DRT-rigged-for-canopy-access.-Credit-Palasiah-Jotan-x.jpg' data-full='https://news.exeter.ac.uk/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/A-57m-tall-Dipterocarp-tree-with-a-double-rope-technique-DRT-rigged-for-canopy-access.-Credit-Palasiah-Jotan-x.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(123) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/deepreinforce-ai/Ornith-1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ornith-1.0: self-improving open-source models for agentic coding</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ornith-1.0：具备代理能力的自我改进开源模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48722052' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 260</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48722052' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ornith-1.0 pushes open-source models toward self-improvement for agentic coding. The project explores mechanisms for models to refine their behavior and tooling autonomously, reducing human-labeled overhead. If successful, it could accelerate autonomous programming tasks and bring more capable AI-assisted development to the community.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ornith-1.0 将开源模型推向自我改进，聚焦具备代理能力的编码任务。它尝试让模型自主优化行为和工具使用，降低人工标注的开销。一旦成熟，可能显著加速自动化编程和 AI 辅助开发的能力落地。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/a8f2bc6e457547e434482434874aa1e5d6d5de271b2c78b3a0a82707cb5b1636/deepreinforce-ai/Ornith-1' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/a8f2bc6e457547e434482434874aa1e5d6d5de271b2c78b3a0a82707cb5b1636/deepreinforce-ai/Ornith-1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(124) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mechanical-pencil.com/products/car' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How does a pull-back car work? Illustrated teardown</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>拉回式玩具车如何工作？图解拆解</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48712289' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 259</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48712289' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pull-back cars use a spring-loaded motor and simple gears; the teardown reveals how stored energy translates into rolling propulsion. The illustrated breakdown highlights the elegance and limits of toy engineering, plus how small parts coordinate to make the car move.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>拉回式玩具车通过弹簧驱动的马达和简单齿轮实现推进。图解拆解揭示了玩具工程的简洁性与局限，以及小零件如何协同作用让玩具起作用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(125) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/ai/2026/06/south-korea-to-spend-1t-on-more-memory-chip-production-and-humanoid-robots/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>South Korea to spend $1T on more memory chip production and humanoid robots</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>韩国拟斥资1万亿美元扩产内存芯片并推进人形机器人</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48726102' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 257</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48726102' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 207</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>South Korea unveiled a trillion-dollar plan to boost memory-chip production and deploy humanoid robots, signaling a strong push to secure AI hardware and automate the workforce. The move could reshape regional chip incentives and technology ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>韩国发布规模达1万亿美元的计划，扩大内存芯片产能并推进人形机器人产业，显示其在 AI 硬件与劳动力自动化方面的强力布局，或将改变区域芯片激励与科技生态。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(126) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ui.shadcn.com/docs/changelog' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Shadcn/UI now defaults to Base UI instead of Radix</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Shadcn/UI 现已把默认使用 Base UI，而非 Radix</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48791328' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 256</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48791328' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 138</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Shadcn/UI has switched its default UI primitives from Radix to Base UI. This change reduces reliance on Radix components and aligns the library with Base UI&#x27;s design tokens. It can affect how you scaffold and theme apps using shadcn/ui.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该更新将默认的 UI 基元从 Radix 改为 Base UI，减少对 Radix 组件的依赖并统一设计令牌。开发者在使用 shadcn/ui 搭建和主题化应用时将受到影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(127) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://phys.org/news/2026-06-newly-australian-ballista-spider-snare.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Newly discovered spider builds spring loaded snare to catch ants</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新发现的蜘蛛用弹簧陷阱捕蚁</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48711052' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 253</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48711052' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 60</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A newly documented spider uses a spring-loaded snare to catch ants, showcasing a novel predation strategy in arachnids. The rapid, mechanical trap highlights evolved specialization and could inspire bio-inspired trap design in engineering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员发现一种新蜘蛛，利用弹簧式陷阱捕捉蚂蚁，这是一种罕见的捕食策略。其快速的机械捕捉机制体现了进化的专业化，并可能为生物启发的捕捉装置提供灵感。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(130) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://eshumarneedi.com/2026/07/03/zuckerberg-admits-metas-layoffs-were.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zuckerberg &#x27;Admits&#x27; Meta&#x27;s Layoffs Were Ineffective</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>扎克伯格承认 Meta 的裁员并非有效</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48774454' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 247</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48774454' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 229</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Zuckerberg acknowledges that Meta&#x27;s layoffs did not deliver the expected efficiency or morale benefits. The admission signals a potential pivot in staffing strategy as the company doubles down on AI as a growth driver, suggesting more focus on retaining talent and re-skilling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>扎克伯格承认 Meta 的裁员未达到预期的效率和士气改善。此举可能促使公司调整人力策略，将重点放在以 AI 为增长驱动上，并强调对人才保留与再培训的关注。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(131) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://aresluna.org/the-curious-case-of-the-disappearing-polish-s/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The curious case of the disappearing Polish S (2015)</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A typography/encoding quirk where the Polish letter S vanishes in certain renderings, illustrating Unicode and font shaping quirks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于在某些文本渲染中波兰字母 S 消失的排版/编码怪现象，揭示了 Unicode 与字体排版的细节问题。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(132) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48737226' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tell HN: Installing Cursor on iOS irreversibly changes your privacy settings</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 iOS 上安装 Cursor 会不可逆地改变隐私设置</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48737226' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 245</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48737226' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A post warns that installing Cursor on iOS can irreversibly alter privacy settings by requesting broad permissions. It underscores the risk of third-party tooling that promises convenience while expanding data access, prompting readers to scrutinize permissions and data flows. The note serves as a cautionary reminder about app-level privacy hygiene.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇帖子警告：在 iOS 上安装 Cursor 可能不可逆地改变隐私设置，因其请求广泛权限而扩展数据访问。这提醒读者在使用第三方工具时要仔细审查权限与数据流，并保持对应用隐私的警惕。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(133) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://noyb.eu/en/us-supreme-court-just-blew-eu-us-data-transfers' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US Supreme Court Just Blew Up EU-US Data Transfers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国最高法院刚刚瓦解欧盟-美国数据传输规则</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48728740' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 241</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48728740' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 187</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Supreme Court decision disrupts the framework enabling EU-US data transfers, forcing reliance on additional safeguards and mechanisms like SCCs. It accelerates data localization pressures and reshapes how global services move data.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最高法院的裁决中断了EU-US数据传输的框架，迫使企业更多地依赖附加保护条款及数据本地化手段。此举改变了全球服务的数据移动策略与合规路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://noyb.eu/sites/default/files/styles/facebook/public/2026-05/header_EU-US-Data-Transfers_0.jpg?h=c9a3a702&amp;itok=ih1PTPh3' data-full='https://noyb.eu/sites/default/files/styles/facebook/public/2026-05/header_EU-US-Data-Transfers_0.jpg?h=c9a3a702&amp;itok=ih1PTPh3' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(134) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://wozniak.ca/blog/2014/08/03/1/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What ORMs have taught me: just learn SQL (2014)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ORM 给我的教训：还是要学 SQL（2014）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48742175' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 241</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48742175' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 276</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critique of ORMs arguing that learning SQL remains essential for real-world performance and control. The piece advocates understanding data access patterns, indexing, and query optimization beyond ORM abstractions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章批评 ORM 的局限，主张在实际开发中仍应掌握 SQL，因其对性能、索引和查询优化的控制力更强。强调深入理解数据访问模式胜过被动依赖框架。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(135) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.savingourstars.org/darkskylighting#whatisdarkskylighting' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dark Sky Lighting</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>暗天天空照明</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48675653' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 238</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48675653' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dark Sky Lighting advocates for lighting designs that minimize light pollution and preserve star visibility, combining policy, urban design, and tech guidelines. The effort discusses practical lighting standards, such as shielding, color temperature, and adaptive controls, to protect ecosystems and support astronomy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>“暗天天空照明”倡导通过降低光污染、保护星空可见性来设计照明，结合政策、城市设计与技术指南。该行动讨论了实际的照明标准，如遮光、色温与自适应控制等，以保护生态并支持天文观测。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/581a982b46c3c4492a241020/1480248658712-JS5339FMQWXJIQRQP0YO/1sos+logo-final-shadow-dipper.png?format=1500w' data-full='https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/581a982b46c3c4492a241020/1480248658712-JS5339FMQWXJIQRQP0YO/1sos+logo-final-shadow-dipper.png?format=1500w' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(137) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.elenaverna.com/p/please-stop-the-ai-confidence-theater' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Please stop the AI confidence theater</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>请停止 AI 的自信表演</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48774414' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 233</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48774414' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 257</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI systems increasingly emit confident-sounding outputs that mislead users about reliability. The piece argues for calibrating trust, better UX signals, and safeguards to prevent overtrust in automated reasoning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>质疑 AI 系统输出过于自信、难以区分真伪的问题。呼吁在设计中降低对 AI 自信的错觉，改进用户提示信号与保护机制，防止用户对自动推断过度依赖。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(139) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/tech-industry/data-centers/cheyenne-suspends-data-center-fill-and-flush-and-closed-loop-discharges-after-meta-contractor-contaminated-its-reuse-water-system' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta data center water discharges suspended for contaminating water supply</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 数据中心因污染供水暂停排水作业</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48786782' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 232</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48786782' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta&#x27;s Cheyenne data center paused its water discharge and closed-loop reuse operations after a contractor contaminated the facility’s water system. Regulators and operators are reviewing safety protocols and reuse processes to prevent a repeat incident. The event underscores environmental and operational risks tied to water cycling in large-scale data centers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>切尼数据中心在承包商污染再利用水系统后暂停了排水与闭环回用操作。监管机构与运营方正在评估安全与合规措施，以防止类似事件再次发生。此事凸显大型数据中心水循环的环境与运营风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/5DrUKzfgk2vDQzjnVtVpkJ-2000-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/5DrUKzfgk2vDQzjnVtVpkJ-2000-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(140) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.playstation.com/en-gb/legal/psvideocontent/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Studio Canal Movies purchased on PlayStation Store removed without refund</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PlayStation Store 下架 StudioCanal 电影且无退款</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48718967' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 229</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48718967' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 127</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Studio Canal titles purchased on PlayStation Store were removed from the catalog without refunds. This illustrates how digital licenses can disappear from storefronts, leaving buyers with inaccessible content and underscoring the fragility of digital ownership.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 PlayStation Store 购买的 Studio Canal 电影被下架，且未提供退款。这凸显数字内容的授权而非真正拥有的本质，购买后可能因版权变化而不可用，提醒消费者数字库的脆弱性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://gmedia.playstation.com/is/image/SIEPDC/facebook-shared-social-thumbnail?$facebook$' data-full='https://gmedia.playstation.com/is/image/SIEPDC/facebook-shared-social-thumbnail?$facebook$' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48753979' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(141) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/technology/safety-security/opening-up-zero-knowledge-proof-technology-to-promote-privacy-in-age-assurance/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Opening up &#x27;Zero-Knowledge Proof&#x27; technology to promote privacy in age assurance</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>开放零知识证明技术以提升年龄认证隐私</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48753979' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 229</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48753979' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 273</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google outlines opening up zero-knowledge proof technology to improve privacy in age verification, enabling platforms to prove age without exposing birth dates or identity details. This shift could unlock privacy-preserving access controls in social apps and e-commerce.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Google 提出开放零知识证明技术以提升年龄认证中的隐私保护，使平台在不暴露出生日期或身份信息的情况下证明用户年龄。此举可能为社交、电子商务等场景带来隐私友好且可验证的准入控制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/Screenshot_2025-07-03_12.59.53_PM.width-1300.png' data-full='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/Screenshot_2025-07-03_12.59.53_PM.width-1300.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48735376' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(142) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/world/crypto-firms-have-spent-189-million-so-far-2026-us-election-report-says-2026-06-30/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Crypto firms have spent $189M so far on 2026 US election, report says</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>加密公司在2026年美国大选中已支出1.89亿美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48735376' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 228</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48735376' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 110</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Crypto firms have spent about $189 million so far on the 2026 US elections, reflecting a growing push to influence policy and regulation. The level of spending signals how the industry seeks clarity and favorable rules as it matures, drawing scrutiny from policymakers and investors. It underscores the entanglement of tech money and politics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>加密公司迄今在2026年美国大选中花费约1.89亿美元，显示其推动政策与监管的诉求不断增强。如此水平的支出反映行业寻求明确规则与有利环境的意图，同时也引发政策制定者与投资者的关注，凸显科技资金与政治的交汇。</p>
</div>
