<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48776345' data-tags='Science,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(143) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20415' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Markets are competitive if and only if P != NP</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>只有当 P ≠ NP 时，市场才具备竞争性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48776345' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 228</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48776345' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 158</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative theoretical result links market competitiveness to a fundamental problem in computer science: if P != NP. If P != NP, certain efficient market equilibria may be possible; if P = NP, others may arise. The result invites economists and AI researchers to consider computational limits when modeling competition.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对市场竞争性与计算复杂性之间的关系提出富有争议的观点：若 P ≠ NP，市场可能存在某些高效的均衡；若 P = NP，情况又会不同。该研究促使经济学家与 AI 研究者在建模竞争时考虑计算界限。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(144) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://inpreparation.substack.com/p/opinion-i-was-not-allowed-to-type' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I Wasn&#x27;t Allowed Prompting ChatGPT During My Chalk Talk: This Is Discrimination (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我在 chalk talk 中不被允许提示 ChatGPT：这算歧视</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48777728' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 227</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48777728' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 139</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A speaker recounts being barred from prompting ChatGPT during a conference chalk-talk, framing it as an access issue and arguing that AI-assisted work should be treated as a baseline expectation, not a privilege. The piece raises questions about equity in AI enablement at technical events.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者在 chalk talk 中被禁止使用 ChatGPT 提示，认为这是对 AI 辅助的歧视与不平等待遇，呼吁把 AI 助力视为基本工具而非特权。文章提出技术活动中的公平性问题。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vite+ Beta introduces a set of ecosystem enhancements to the Vite tooling, aiming to speed up startup and improve hot module replacement. It also expands plug-in capabilities, shaping how developers build apps in the near term.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Vite+ 测试版带来一组对开发体验有明显改进的功能，聚焦启动速度、热更新和插件生态的提升。开发者可以在 Beta 阶段试玩，评估对现有工作流的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48720190' data-tags='Programming,Hardware,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(146) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://humphri.es/blog/WATaBoy/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>WATaBoy: JIT-Ing Game Boy Instructions to WASM Beats a Native Interpreter</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>WATaBoy：将 Game Boy 指令 JIT 编译为 WASM，胜过原生解释器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48720190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 225</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48720190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>WATaBoy demonstrates that compiling Game Boy instructions to WebAssembly at runtime can outperform a hand-written native interpreter on common hardware. The project showcases WASM as a high-performance backend for emulation, offering portability across platforms. It also raises questions about further optimizations and toolchain choices for emulators.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>WATaBoy 展示了在运行时将 Game Boy 指令 JIT 编译为 WebAssembly，甚至超越了某些硬件上的本地解释器的速度。此做法凸显 WASM 作为高性能后端在仿真领域的可行性与跨平台性，同时也引发对进一步优化与工具链选择的思考。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://humphri.es/blog/WATaBoy/wataboy-screenshot.png' data-full='https://humphri.es/blog/WATaBoy/wataboy-screenshot.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(147) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22283' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple Neural Engine: Architecture, Programming, and Performance</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果神经引擎：架构、编程与性能</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48702825' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 225</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48702825' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This arXiv preprint details the Apple Neural Engine&#x27;s architectural design, its programming model, and performance characteristics across iOS/Mac workloads. It sheds light on how Apple stacks and accelerates ML workloads on device, with implications for developers targeting on-device AI and privacy-preserving inference.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这份 arXiv 预印本揭示了 Apple 神经引擎的架构设计、编程模型以及在设备端的性能特征，聚焦于 iOS/macOS 上的机器学习工作负载。对开发者在设备端实现隐私保护推理和高效 AI 推理具有重要参考意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(148) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ubuntu.com/blog/hunting-a-16-year-old-sqlite-bug-with-tla-is-dqlite-affected' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hunting a 16-year-old SQLite WAL bug with TLA+</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 TLA+ 发现 SQLite WAL 的 16 年旧漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48730953' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 225</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48730953' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A case study showing how formal methods helped uncover a longstanding SQLite WAL bug. By modeling the write ahead log with TLA+, the team reasoned about correctness and edge cases beyond conventional testing, underscoring the value of formal verification in database engines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个案例研究，展示通过形式化方法用 TLA+ 对 SQLite 的写前日志进行建模，揭示长期存在的漏洞并对正确性和边界情况进行了推理，凸显形式化验证在数据库引擎中的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(149) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://apnews.com/live/birthright-citizenship-decision-supreme-court-updates-06-30-2026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Supreme Court upholds broad conception of birthright citizenship</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>最高法院维持对出生公民权的广义认定</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48733406' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 223</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48733406' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 614</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Supreme Court preserves a broad interpretation of birthright citizenship, keeping citizenship eligibility widely accessible for those born in the country. The ruling constrains potential reforms that could narrow eligibility and has implications for immigration policy, education, and labor markets. It solidifies a long-standing constitutional interpretation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最高法院维持对出生公民权的广义认定，确保在境内出生的人大多数获得公民身份。此判决收窄了可能收紧公民资格的改革空间，并对移民政策、教育和劳动力市场带来广泛影响，巩固了这一长期的宪法解释。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A reflective piece on the role of restarts in modern systems, arguing that periodic reboots remain valuable for stability, helping clear memory and reset stuck processes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文主张即使现代系统改用热更新，定期重启仍有助于清理内存、解决卡死进程并提升稳定性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48765639' data-tags='Programming,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(151) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.dbos.dev/blog/co-locating-workflow-state-with-your-data' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Postgres transactions are a distributed systems superpower</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PostgreSQL 事务是分布式系统的超级能力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48765639' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 217</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48765639' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 92</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PostgreSQL transactions enable strong consistency across distributed workflows by co-locating state with data, reducing cross-service coordination and failure modes. This approach can simplify building reliable, scalable systems with complex data interactions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PostgreSQL 的事务模型让在分布式工作流中实现强一致性成为可能，通过将工作流状态与数据并置，降低跨服务协调的复杂度。此做法有助于构建在数据交互复杂的场景下更可靠、可扩展的系统。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/672411cbf038560468c9e68f/6a302850af242b318fadeb41_Transactional-outbox-idempotency-atomicity2.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/672411cbf038560468c9e68f/6a302850af242b318fadeb41_Transactional-outbox-idempotency-atomicity2.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48733031' data-tags='Programming,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(152) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.snowflake.com/en/blog/engineering/postgresql-19-features-beta/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Looking Ahead to Postgres 19</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>展望 Postgres 19</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48733031' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 216</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48733031' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 131</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Postgres 19 is shaping up in beta with features aimed at performance and ecosystem tooling. Anticipated improvements include better parallelism, indexing options, and cloud-friendly tooling, continuing PostgreSQL&#x27;s momentum in enterprise deployments. The release signals sustained competition with managed databases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Postgres 19 的测试版聚焦性能与生态工具，预计将提升并行查询、索引和云端工作流等能力，继续推动 PostgreSQL 在企业部署中的地位与竞争力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.snowflake.com/content/dam/snowflake-site/engineering-blog/featured-images/sf-eng-blog-ML-3.png' data-full='https://www.snowflake.com/content/dam/snowflake-site/engineering-blog/featured-images/sf-eng-blog-ML-3.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48786638' data-tags='Programming,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(153) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ziglang.org/devlog/2026/#2026-06-30' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zig: All Package Management Functionality Moved from Compiler to Build System</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Zig：所有包管理功能从编译器移至构建系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48786638' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 216</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48786638' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 72</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Zig&#x27;s devlog announces a shift of all package management functionality from the compiler into the build system, aiming to simplify language tooling and improve dependency handling. The move could affect how Zig projects declare, resolve, and sandbox dependencies, with potential ripple effects on portability and build determinism.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Zig 开发日志宣布将所有包管理功能从编译器迁移到构建系统，旨在简化语言工具链并改进依赖处理。此举可能改变 Zig 项目对依赖声明、解析与隔离的方式，并影响可移植性与构建确定性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48674875' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 214</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48674875' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Thomas Dullien (Halvar) shares pragmatic, no-nonsense guidance for starting and growing a tech business, covering common pitfalls, customer engagement, and sustainable product strategy. The guide emphasizes focusing on real user value, disciplined decision-making, and avoiding overhype when building a company.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Thomas Dullien 的创业指南提供实用、直截了当的创业要点，聚焦用户价值、产品策略与绕开常见陷阱。內容强调以客户为中心、保持克制的决策，以及避免过度炒作来稳定公司成长。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48716634' data-tags='Science,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(155) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/science/2026/06/why-did-this-journal-retract-two-1940s-papers-by-max-planck/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why did this journal retract two 1940s papers by Max Planck?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何这本期刊撤回两篇1940年代的马克斯·普朗克论文？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48716634' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 210</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48716634' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A journal retracted two 1940s papers attributed to Max Planck, due to issues with attribution and data provenance that only emerged after decades. The retractions raise questions about the integrity of historical science records and the process by which journals validate older work. They also reflect how modern standards can reveal problems in long-accepted publications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一份期刊撤回了两篇20世纪40年代署名马克斯·普朗克的论文，原因涉及署名与数据来源问题，这些问题在多年后才被揭露。此举引发对历史科学记录完整性的质疑，以及现代期刊在核验早期工作时的流程改进需求，同时也反映了新标准可能暴露长期被接受的出版物中的问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/maxplanck-1152x648-1782657031.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/maxplanck-1152x648-1782657031.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(156) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/openai/codex/issues/2847' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A way to exclude sensitive files issue still open for OpenAI Codex</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI Codex 的排除敏感文件方法仍未解决</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48706714' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 210</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48706714' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 133</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An open issue around how to exclude sensitive files from Codex training or generation remains unresolved. The discussion highlights ongoing data governance and privacy challenges in deploying code-generating AI, where guarding private or proprietary content is critical.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于如何从 Codex 的训练或输出中排除敏感文件的讨论仍未有定论，尚处于未解决状态。此议题凸显了代码生成模型在数据治理和隐私保护方面的持续挑战，以及对私有或机密内容的防护需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/eccbe5e1f2a2e82c834163352ad07ef046187667a06e623afc4bd509047618d9/openai/codex/issues/2847' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/eccbe5e1f2a2e82c834163352ad07ef046187667a06e623afc4bd509047618d9/openai/codex/issues/2847' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Germany’s Infineon opens a major chip plant as the EU pursues tech autonomy. The move bolsters Europe’s semiconductor capacity and may affect supply security, jobs, and industrial policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>德国英飞凌在德国开设大型芯片工厂，配合欧盟推动的科技自主战略。此举有望增强欧洲半导体供应能力，影响供给安全、就业和产业政策。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48727177' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(158) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fil-c.org/context_switches' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Memory Safe Context Switching</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>内存安全的上下文切换</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48727177' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 208</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48727177' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece argues for memory-safe context switching in operating systems to prevent cross-process memory leakage and safety bugs. It surveys approaches like isolation boundaries and safe-language techniques, and discusses the safety-performance tradeoffs involved.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文提出在操作系统层面进行内存安全的上下文切换，以防止进程间的内存泄漏和安全缺陷。它评估了通过隔离边界、安全语言等手段实现安全性的做法，并讨论了安全性与性能之间的权衡。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project porting Linux to the Sega Mega Drive demonstrates pushing modern OS concepts onto retro hardware. The write-up discusses constraints of the 16-bit console and insights for hobbyists who want to experiment with vintage platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该项目把 Linux 移植到世嘉 Mega Drive，向这款16位控制台挑战现代操作系统概念。文中讨论了硬件限制，并为喜欢复古平台的爱好者提供实践洞见。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48788599' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(160) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lucumr.pocoo.org/2026/7/4/better-models-worse-tools/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Better Models: Worse Tools</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>更强的模型，工具却更差</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48788599' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 205</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48788599' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 73</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Better models alone don&#x27;t guarantee better outcomes without equally capable tooling; the essay argues that improvements in model accuracy must be matched by improvements in tooling and workflows to unlock real-world value. It advocates a stronger focus on tooling design and API ergonomics to leverage advancements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为仅提升模型能力不足以带来更好结果，若缺少同等水平的工具与工作流也会拖累效用。文章主张强化工具设计和 API 易用性，以充分发挥模型进步的潜力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lucumr.pocoo.org/social/2026-07-04-better-models-worse-tools-social.png' data-full='https://lucumr.pocoo.org/social/2026-07-04-better-models-worse-tools-social.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48779977' data-tags='Space,Education,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(161) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.economist.com/middle-east-and-africa/2026/07/02/africans-are-turning-to-starlink' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Africans Are Turning to Starlink</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>非洲人正转向星链</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48779977' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 205</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48779977' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 253</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Across Africa, Starlink is expanding access to high speed internet in areas where terrestrial networks lag, reshaping education, business, and emergency response. The rollout faces price, latency, and regulatory hurdles, but highlights satellite internet as a key tool for closing the digital divide.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着星链在非洲的扩张，偏远地区的高速网络逐步成为现实，推动教育、商业与应急服务的提升。定价、延迟与监管挑战仍存，但卫星互联网正成为缩小数字鸿沟的重要工具。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48783948' data-tags='Space,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(162) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.quantamagazine.org/astrophysicists-puzzle-over-webbs-new-universe-20260702/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Astrophysicists Puzzle over Webb’s New Universe</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>天体物理学家对詹姆斯·韦伯望远镜观测到的新宇宙感到困惑</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48783948' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 205</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48783948' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 123</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Astronomers analyzing JWST data find unexpected features in the early universe that don’t fit current models, challenging ideas about galaxy formation and cosmic history. The results highlight how the newest infrared observations can reveal gaps in theory and prompt new calibration and interpretation approaches.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>天体物理学家分析詹姆斯·韦伯望远镜的数据，发现早期宇宙存在一些与现有模型不符的异常特征，给星系形成和宇宙历史带来挑战。最新的红外观测揭示理论空白，促使重新校准并重新解释观测结果。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/JWSTUpdate-crKristinaArmitage-Social.jpg' data-full='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/JWSTUpdate-crKristinaArmitage-Social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(163) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wsj.com/politics/policy/trump-made-more-than-1-billion-on-crypto-deals-part-of-2025-windfall-ee917d3f' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Trump made more than $1B on crypto deals, part of 2025 windfall</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特朗普在加密交易中赚取超过10亿美元，成为2025年财富增长的一部分</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48741012' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 203</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48741012' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New reporting indicates Trump earned more than $1B from crypto deals, contributing to a sizable 2025 windfall. The figure spotlights how crypto ventures intersect with politics and personal wealth, fueling debates over disclosure, timing, and potential conflicts of interest. It underscores the influence of crypto markets on high-profile figures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新的报道显示特朗普在加密货币交易中赚取超过10亿美元，成为他2025年财富增长的一部分。这一数字凸显了加密投资与政治人物之间的关系，引发关于披露、时机以及潜在利益冲突的讨论，反映出加密市场对高知名度人物的影响力。</p>

</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48736137' data-tags='Science,Security'>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hands-on look at using millimeter-wave radar to distinguish materials by their dielectric signatures. The approach has practical implications for robotics, security, and industrial sensing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者展示了用毫米波雷达对材料进行分类的实现，材料在雷达信号中的介电特性差异帮助识别。此方法对机器人、安防和工业传感等领域有实际应用潜力。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(165) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.maturitas.org/article/S0378-5122(25)00571-7/fulltext' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Exercise intensity influences body composition in healthy older adults (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>运动强度影响健康老年人身体组成（2025年研究）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48730694' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 200</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48730694' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 175</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 2025 study finds that higher-intensity exercise more effectively improves body composition in healthy older adults than lower-intensity workouts. The result informs aging fitness guidelines and emphasizes balancing safety with difficulty to maximize benefits. It suggests tailoring programs to individual risk profiles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2025年的一项研究发现，相较于低强度训练，更高强度的运动对健康老年人的身体组成有更明显的改善。研究为老年人健身指南提供了证据，强调在安全前提下提升训练强度以获得最大化收益，并建议按个体风险制定方案。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Boeing 747, long the icon of global air travel, is nearing retirement as fleets and routes shift to more efficient jets. The piece traces the 747&#x27;s engineering legacy, economic drivers of its sunset, and the challenges of maintaining aging aircraft while demand evolves.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作为全球航空旅行的象征，波音 747 即将退役，全球航线与机队的更新推动其退出市场。文章回顾了 747 在工程与全球经济中的影响，以及在需求和维护成本变化背景下退役所面临的挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(168) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://crocidb.com/post/freebsd-ate-my-ram/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FreeBSD ate my RAM</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48778757' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 194</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48778757' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 78</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A firsthand blog post documents a FreeBSD memory exhaustion incident, exploring how memory management quirks and workload can lead to unexpected RAM usage. The story offers practical reminders for monitoring, tuning, and understanding ARC and caching on BSD systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>博主记录了 FreeBSD 内存耗尽的经历，分析了内存管理中的异常行为及工作负载如何触发高 RAM 使用。给系统管理员带来监控、调优与理解 ARC/缓存机制的实用提示。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(171) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stories.tamu.edu/news/2026/04/14/scientists-reverse-brain-aging-with-a-nasal-spray/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Scientists reverse brain aging, with a nasal spray</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科学家用鼻腔喷雾逆转大脑衰老</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48790066' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 192</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48790066' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A nasal spray treatment reverses markers of brain aging in preclinical models, offering a non-invasive path toward mitigating age-related cognitive decline. The approach highlights targeted nose-to-brain delivery and could complement neuroprotective strategies under development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>鼻腔喷雾治疗在实验模型中逆转了大脑衰老标志，提供一种无创的抗衰老路径。该方法通过鼻腔直达脑部的递送方式，或为缓解年龄相关认知衰退的策略增添新选择。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48774509' data-tags='DevOps,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(173) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ubicloud.com/blog/postgresql-and-the-oom-killer-why-we-use-strict-memory-overcommit' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PostgreSQL and the OOM killer: Why we use strict memory overcommit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PostgreSQL 与 OOM Killer：为何采用严格的内存超分配</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48774509' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 191</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48774509' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 121</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PostgreSQL deployments rely on strict memory overcommit settings to prevent unpredictable OOM conditions. This approach improves stability in containerized and cloud environments, but can lead to memory inefficiency if not tuned carefully. The piece offers practical guidance for memory budgeting, swap behavior, and kernel parameters to balance safety and performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PostgreSQL 部署常通过严格的内存超分配设置来避免不可预测的内存不足。该方法在容器化和云环境中提升了稳定性，但若未作充分调优，可能导致内存资源浪费。文中给出内存预算、swap 行为与内核参数等实用建议，帮助在安全和性能之间取得平衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/64f9d9b4e737e7b37d4e39a4/69e8a268046b54c24dff01c2_OOM%20Killer%20(1).jpg' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/64f9d9b4e737e7b37d4e39a4/69e8a268046b54c24dff01c2_OOM%20Killer%20(1).jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(174) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://moondream.ai/blog/popping-the-gpu-bubble' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Popping the GPU Bubble</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>戳破GPU泡沫</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48728729' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 190</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48728729' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that the GPU market may deflate after AI hype peaks, examining price pressure, capacity, and demand drivers for data centers and cloud providers. It cautions investors and engineers to recalibrate expectations around AI hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为在 AI 热潮褪去后，GPU 市场可能回落，分析了价格压力、产能与数据中心及云服务对显卡的需求等因素。对投资者和工程师提出了重新校准对 AI 硬件的预期的建议。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://moondream.ai/images/blog/popping-the-gpu-bubble/cover.jpg' data-full='https://moondream.ai/images/blog/popping-the-gpu-bubble/cover.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48708795' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(175) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://12gramsofcarbon.com/p/agentics-tech-things-tokenmaxxing' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tokenmaxxing is dead, long live tokenmaxxing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tokenmaxxing 已死，Tokenmaxxing 将长存</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48708795' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 190</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48708795' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 284</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tokenmaxxing has seen a pivot: the old form may be fading, but the core mindset remains—optimizing prompts, tool use, and token budgets will continue under new guises as models and pricing evolve. The post argues that while the practice changes, the incentives to maximize efficiency persist.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该文认为“Tokenmaxxing”的旧形式或已衰退，但核心思维仍在：在新模型与定价结构下，优化提示、工具使用和 token 使用的动机将以新的形式继续存在。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(177) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/thsottiaux/status/2073933490513752151' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra will be in Codex</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra 将嵌入 Codex</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48799614' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 188</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48799614' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 121</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rumors hint that GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra will be integrated into Codex, boosting coding-assistant capabilities inside the IDE. If true, developers may gain faster, more accurate code generation and smarter suggestions, but it could exacerbate concerns about dependency, licensing, and model safety as tooling proliferates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>传闻称 GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra 将嵌入 Codex，可能提升该工具在编码场景中的表现。若属实，开发者将获得更快的代码生成与更智能的建议，但也带来依赖、许可与安全等新挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48739466' data-tags='AI,Science,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(178) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ai.meta.com/blog/brain2qwerty-brain-ai-human-communication/?_fb_noscript=1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>From brain waves to words: a new path to communication without surgery</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>脑波到文字：无需手术的全新沟通路径</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48739466' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 184</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48739466' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 89</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers are turning brain activity into text without surgery by decoding brain signals with AI. The approach promises new communication options for people with paralysis or severe speech impairment, but faces accuracy, speed, and privacy challenges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员通过人工智能解码脑信号，在不进行手术的情况下将脑波转写为文字。这一路径为瘫痪或语言受限者带来新的沟通方式，但在准确性、速度与隐私方面仍存在挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scontent-iad3-1.xx.fbcdn.net/v/t39.2365-6/731147471_987177500749005_1892166673921917145_n.gif?_nc_cat=101&amp;ccb=1-7&amp;_nc_sid=e280be&amp;_nc_ohc=kt4rIA7tiyMQ7kNvwET_ibV&amp;_nc_oc=AdqBndvWlZGV3QeLon4nsnhhaCgwFDk6Ev_I12sEZ6OqJRjLIE60d6TD3vGOTHxnwS8&amp;_nc_zt=14&amp;_nc_ht=scontent-iad3-1.xx&amp;_nc_gid=0QZ0-RiA-QG5HbcxlAeIQA&amp;_nc_ss=7820f&amp;oh=00_AQByZw_hxM-cBLWwPBK3wN2cSyzaxZ0amuDyWK5RG9HtPg&amp;oe=6A5E8CAD' data-full='https://scontent-iad3-1.xx.fbcdn.net/v/t39.2365-6/731147471_987177500749005_1892166673921917145_n.gif?_nc_cat=101&amp;ccb=1-7&amp;_nc_sid=e280be&amp;_nc_ohc=kt4rIA7tiyMQ7kNvwET_ibV&amp;_nc_oc=AdqBndvWlZGV3QeLon4nsnhhaCgwFDk6Ev_I12sEZ6OqJRjLIE60d6TD3vGOTHxnwS8&amp;_nc_zt=14&amp;_nc_ht=scontent-iad3-1.xx&amp;_nc_gid=0QZ0-RiA-QG5HbcxlAeIQA&amp;_nc_ss=7820f&amp;oh=00_AQByZw_hxM-cBLWwPBK3wN2cSyzaxZ0amuDyWK5RG9HtPg&amp;oe=6A5E8CAD' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(179) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.gutenberg.org/ebooks/24518' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Memoirs of Extraordinary Popular Delusions and the Madness of Crowds (1852)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《群体的非凡错觉与众人的疯狂》（1852）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48731989' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 183</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48731989' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 62</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An 1852 classic on crowd psychology that catalogs famous delusions and speculative manias. Though old, it helps readers recognize how hype and groupthink shape technology and finance today. The work remains a useful lens for analyzing bubbles and social dynamics in tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>1852 年的经典著作，系统梳理群体心理与历史上的共识错判与群体狂热。即使年代久远，它也为理解科技与金融领域的牛熊逻辑提供洞见，帮助读者识别泡沫与群体行为对创新的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/24518/pg24518.cover.medium.jpg' data-full='https://www.gutenberg.org/cache/epub/24518/pg24518.cover.medium.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(180) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/gadgets/2026/06/sony-erases-digital-content-from-libraries-were-reminded-we-dont-own-what-we-buy/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sony erases digital content from libraries</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>索尼从数字馆藏中移除内容</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48730904' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 182</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48730904' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 71</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sony has begun removing digital content from libraries, underscoring that digital purchases are licenses, not ownership. The move means users can lose access when titles are pulled, heightening concerns about digital rights and library permanence. This episode adds to the broader DRM consumer protection discussion.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>索尼从数字馆藏中移除内容，强调数字购买是许可而非所有权。随着标题被下架，用户可能失去访问权，这凸显数字版权与馆藏长期可用性之担忧，也让 DRM 与消费者保护的讨论更为迫切。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2020/06/abomb8-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2020/06/abomb8-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece reflects on content that hasn&#x27;t attracted much attention, examining factors like algorithmic discovery, audience signals, and niche interest. It offers practical takeaways for creators about audience building, timing, and writing for discoverability in noisy feeds.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章反思了未被广泛关注的内容，探讨算法推荐、受众信号和小众兴趣对发现度的影响。对创作者而言，给出提升在嘈杂信息流中被发现的策略与经验。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A technique called optimistic provide dramatically speeds up IPFS content publishing, reportedly by about 10x. The post details the approach and its potential impact on distributed content networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过采用“乐观提供”等方法，IPFS 内容发布速度提升了约10倍。文章介绍具体技术思路及对分布式内容分发网络的潜在影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(183) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnbc.com/2026/07/02/job-seekers-giving-up-labor-force-participation-rate-falls-to-lowest-in-50-years-outside-of-covid-era.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Job seekers giving up: Labor force participation falls to lowest in 50 years</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>求职者放弃求职：劳动参与率创50年来新低</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48766076' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 180</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48766076' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 159</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Job seekers giving up pushes the labor-force participation rate to the lowest in 50 years outside the COVID era. The trend points to a tighter labor market with implications for wages and tech hiring.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>求职者放弃求职使劳动参与率降至疫情前后50年的新低。趋势指向劳动市场收紧，对薪资水平和科技行业招聘动向产生影响。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48760048' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 180</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48760048' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 144</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Hacker News thread collects signals about who is quitting in July 2026, reflecting workforce churn in tech. Contributors cite burnout, compensation, remote work, and shifting career expectations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讨论区聚集了关于谁在辞职的个人叙述，反映出科技行业的职业流动性与倦怠问题。参与者常提及工作强度、生活平衡、薪酬以及远程工作的选择等因素。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(186) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://12gramsofcarbon.com/p/agentics-memorizing-session-transcripts' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Memorizing session transcripts isn&#x27;t useful</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>记住会话记录并不有用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48776232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 179</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48776232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 156</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New analysis argues that memorizing session transcripts for agents yields little value and can raise privacy and latency concerns. It suggests alternative memory strategies and distilled summaries to improve long-term task performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新的分析认为记住会话记录对 AI 代理几乎没有实际价值，还可能带来隐私与延迟成本。文章建议采用更高效的记忆方案和摘要，以提升长期推理和任务执行能力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!p8M5!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F75caf207-0302-42e3-beba-2da2967864f8_1080x543.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!p8M5!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F75caf207-0302-42e3-beba-2da2967864f8_1080x543.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Senior SWE-Bench is an open-source benchmark that tests agents on tasks typical of senior software engineers, like architecture, code reviews, and debugging. It provides a means to measure AI tooling against human standards.</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>1 位像素艺术表情符号展示了极简图形设计中的表达力极限，以极少的细节换取标志性造型。该项目探讨在单比特色彩下能传达多少情感，适用于极小的界面、怀旧游戏或终端艺术，体现了设计的高效性。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta loses a bid to dismiss US states&#x27; claims that Facebook and Instagram addict children, allowing the case to proceed. The ruling increases regulatory scrutiny over social platforms and may push for stronger safeguards.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(194) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://danluu.com/ai-coding/#appendix-agentic-loops-and-writing-this-post' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Agentic coding notes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>具代理性的编码笔记</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48782671' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 170</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48782671' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 80</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores how AI coding can exhibit agentic, goal-directed behavior in coding tasks, outlining practical patterns and failure modes. It discusses &#x27;agentic loops&#x27; where outputs influence subsequent inputs, raising concerns about feedback, control, and alignment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文讨论 AI 编码中可能出现的具代理性、目标导向行为，梳理实际应用中的模式与风险。提出“具代理性循环”等概念，警惕输出影响后续输入的反馈环路可能带来的控制和对齐问题。</p>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>博客的崩溃：100个成功博客去哪儿了？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48758802' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 168</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48758802' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 130</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An analysis of why many once-successful blogs faded, pointing to platform shifts, audience fragmentation, monetization limits, and the rise of social media. It offers takeaways on sustainable blogging or archiving.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>分析了曾经成功的博客大量消退的原因，指出平台演变、读者分散、盈利受限以及社媒崛起等因素，并给出可持续博客模式的启示。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48708859' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(196) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.openculture.com/2020/10/daisugi.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Daisugi, the Japanese technique of growing trees out of other trees (2020)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大杉术：日本通过让树从其他树上长出新树的做法（2020）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48708859' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 167</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48708859' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 55</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Daisugi is a Japanese forestry technique where live trees are grown from the branches of other trees to harvest straight timber without cutting down the parent. It represents an early example of sustainable forestry and clever resource management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>大杉术是日本的一种林业技术，通过让活树从其他树的枝干上生长出新树，从而在不砍伐母树的情况下获取笔直木材。这一做法是可持续林业与资源管理的早期典范。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn8.openculture.com/2020/10/22225805/Daisugi.png' data-full='https://cdn8.openculture.com/2020/10/22225805/Daisugi.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(197) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.fide.com/fide-ethics-disciplinary-commission-issues-a-decision-in-case-involving-gm-vladimir-kramnik/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>International chess federation sanctions Kramnik</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>国际棋联对克拉姆尼克实施制裁</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48777266' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 167</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48777266' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 98</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The International Chess Federation has issued sanctions against former world champion Vladimir Kramnik in an ethics case, signaling ongoing governance disputes in top-level chess. The decision underscores the federation’s willingness to enforce ethics rules against prominent players, with potential implications for players, teams, and sponsorships.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>国际棋联对前世界冠军克拉姆尼克实施制裁，涉及伦理案件，显示该机构在高层棋坛治理方面的行动。此举可能影响选手、团队和赞助关系。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(198) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.mbl.edu/news/jellyfish-can-heal-wounds-minutes-scientists-want-their-secrets' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Jellyfish can heal wounds in minutes. Scientists want their secrets</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>水母能在数分钟内愈合伤口，科学家想知道它们的秘密</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48789712' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 166</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48789712' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers found that jellyfish-derived compounds can accelerate wound healing in minutes, hinting at new biomaterials and therapies. Scientists are studying these secrets to develop faster, non-invasive healing treatments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究表明某些水母来源的成分可在数分钟内促进伤口愈合，提示潜在的生物材料与治疗方向。科学家正在探寻其机理，以开发更快、非侵入性的愈合疗法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.mbl.edu/sites/default/files/2026-06/resizing%20for%20article%20header%288%29_0.jpg' data-full='https://www.mbl.edu/sites/default/files/2026-06/resizing%20for%20article%20header%288%29_0.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48673450' data-tags='AI,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(199) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://aleph-alpha.com/en/blog/model-training-as-code/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Model Training as Code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>把模型训练当作代码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48673450' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 165</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48673450' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues for encoding training pipelines as code, enabling version control, reproducibility, and CI/CD for ML. Treating training like software helps track experiments, share configurations, and audit results across teams.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张把模型训练流程写成可版本化的代码，借助版本控制、可重复性和持续集成/交付实现更可控的训练。以这种方式记录实验、共享配置并对结果进行审计。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://aleph-alpha.com/_astro/00-cover-model-training-as-code.oaKwGkcy.png' data-full='https://aleph-alpha.com/_astro/00-cover-model-training-as-code.oaKwGkcy.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48707361' data-tags='Open Source,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(200) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/open-energy-transition/open-tyndp' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EU Open Sources Ten-Year Network Development Planning Tools</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧盟开源十年网络发展规划工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48707361' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 164</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48707361' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The EU open-sources its Ten-Year Network Development Planning tools to help model future energy networks with greater transparency and interoperability. The release enables policymakers, grid operators, and researchers to collaboratively assess grid expansion scenarios as renewables scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟将十年网络发展规划工具开源，提升对未来能源网络的建模、透明度与互操作性。此举将帮助政策制定者、输配电运营商和研究人员在可再生能源扩张场景下进行协同评估。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/60bae30b723338776871debbaf30ec4ef043e113404325cf6c0d1477497b5bca/open-energy-transition/open-tyndp' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/60bae30b723338776871debbaf30ec4ef043e113404325cf6c0d1477497b5bca/open-energy-transition/open-tyndp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48746327' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(201) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cbc.ca/news/world/vespa-italy-postwar-design-9.7252641' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Vespa at 80</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Vespa 80周年：意大利机车为何仍是两轮界的酷物</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48746327' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 164</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48746327' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 148</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vespa marks 80 years as an enduring icon of Italian design and urban mobility. Its timeless form and cultural resonance help explain why the scooter remains a staple of stylish, practical two-wheeled transport, with modern updates preserving nostalgia while improving efficiency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Vespa 已迎来80周年，被视为意大利设计的经典标志。其标志性造型与实用性使其在战后复兴的城市出行中占据独特地位，直到今天仍以复古风格与现代更新并存的方式保持魅力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(202) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cursor.com/evals' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CursorBench 3.1</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CursorBench 3.1 adds new benchmarks and evaluation features for cursor interaction performance. The update helps teams measure latency and throughput to optimize frontend UX.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CursorBench 3.1 发布，新增评测维度和工具，帮助团队衡量光标交互性能的延迟与吞吐，优化前端用户体验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ptht05hbb1ssoooe.public.blob.vercel-storage.com/assets/blog/og/cb-07-01.png' data-full='https://ptht05hbb1ssoooe.public.blob.vercel-storage.com/assets/blog/og/cb-07-01.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48739018' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(203) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mirtitles.org' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mir Books – Books from the Soviet Era</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mir Books：来自苏联时代的书籍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48739018' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 163</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48739018' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 78</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A curated collection of Soviet-era titles digitized and made accessible, preserving rare literature and historical texts. The project underscores how digital archives democratize access to primary sources, and can support enthusiasts and researchers exploring Cold War era culture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对苏联时代书籍的精选数字化收藏，提供可访问的珍稀文献与历史文本。此类数字档案让研究者和爱好者更易获取第一手资料，便于研究冷战时期的文化与科技。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mirtitles.org/wp-content/uploads/2022/04/mir-logo-favicon.png?w=200' data-full='https://mirtitles.org/wp-content/uploads/2022/04/mir-logo-favicon.png?w=200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48742483' data-tags='Science,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(204) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.conception.bio/science-and-updates/the-first-early-human-eggs-from-stem-cells' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The first early human eggs from stem cells</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>首批由干细胞培育的早期人类卵子</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48742483' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 162</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48742483' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 127</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lab researchers have produced early-stage human eggs from stem cells, marking a milestone in reproductive biology. The capability could accelerate study of human gametogenesis and infertility therapies, but ethical and safety questions will shape how quickly such work translates to clinics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员在实验室用干细胞培育出早期人类卵子，标志着生殖生物学的一个里程碑。这一能力可能加速对人类生殖发生和不孕治疗的研究，但伦理与安全问题将影响其向临床的转化速度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://framerusercontent.com/images/PfCSS8URInnpMrqsd5bQlDQBac.png' data-full='https://framerusercontent.com/images/PfCSS8URInnpMrqsd5bQlDQBac.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48732860' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(205) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/article/how-do-wombats-poop-cubes-scientists-get-bottom-mystery' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How do wombats poop cubes? (2021)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>袋熊的便便为何呈立方体形状</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48732860' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 161</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48732860' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 100</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers explain that wombats form cube-shaped droppings due to long gut transit time, dehydration, and contraction of intestinal muscles, which create flat sides. The cube shape helps prevent rolling and aids in territorial marking and stacking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者解释袋熊的便便为何呈立方体形状：肠道迁移时间长、脱水以及肠道肌肉收缩使排泄物干燥成形。立方体便便便于标记领地并叠放，避免滚落。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48766005' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(206) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/HUANGCHIHHUNGLeo/claude-real-video' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude-real-video － any LLM can watch a video</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude-real-video：任何 LLM 都能观看视频</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48766005' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 160</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48766005' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new approach demonstrates how Claude can process and understand video content, bringing video understanding to large language models. This enables more capable multimodal agents and downstream tasks like summarization, search, and reasoning over video.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个方法让 Claude 能观看并理解视频内容，将大模型真正带入多模态感知。此能力可用于视频摘要、检索和基于动态视觉信息的推理等应用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/5e94834758726554fc8d1124d1dc59e19b314c825144fa050777db6a67ac516e/HUANGCHIHHUNGLeo/claude-real-video' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/5e94834758726554fc8d1124d1dc59e19b314c825144fa050777db6a67ac516e/HUANGCHIHHUNGLeo/claude-real-video' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48714802' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(207) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/ogulcancelik/herdr' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Herdr: Agent multiplexer that lives in your terminal</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Herdr：驻留在终端中的代理多路复用器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48714802' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 159</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48714802' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 99</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Herdr is a terminal-based agent multiplexer that lets you run and coordinate multiple agents in a single shell session. It enables modular agent orchestration with shared I/O, reducing context switching for power users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Herdr 是一个在终端运行的代理多路复用器，允许在一个 shell 会话中同时管理和协同多个代理。它支持模块化编排、共享输入输出，降低高强度用户的上下文切换成本。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/b28cd976784c158726c7cae4fb84ba6a478e536757b3bcd3620770161e876985/ogulcancelik/herdr' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/b28cd976784c158726c7cae4fb84ba6a478e536757b3bcd3620770161e876985/ogulcancelik/herdr' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48707103' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(208) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnbc.com/2026/06/28/google-limits-metas-use-of-its-gemini-ai-models-ft-reports.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google limits Meta&#x27;s use of its Gemini AI models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌限制 Meta 使用其 Gemini AI 模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48707103' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 159</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48707103' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 72</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google reportedly limits Meta&#x27;s access to Gemini models, constraining Meta&#x27;s ability to build AI features. The licensing friction highlights how model access terms can influence product strategy and competitive positioning among big tech players.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，谷歌对 Meta 使用 Gemini 模型设定了访问限制，限制了 Meta 在构建 AI 功能方面的能力。这类授权条款对大厂的产品策略与竞争格局产生影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.cnbcfm.com/api/v1/image/108194138-1756981037270-gettyimages-2214393202-avdtnmymay25lot2jj.jpeg?v=1761941786&amp;w=1920&amp;h=1080' data-full='https://image.cnbcfm.com/api/v1/image/108194138-1756981037270-gettyimages-2214393202-avdtnmymay25lot2jj.jpeg?v=1761941786&amp;w=1920&amp;h=1080' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48767058' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(209) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/business/zuckerberg-says-ai-agent-development-going-slower-than-expected-2026-07-02/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zuckerberg says AI agent development going slower than expected</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>扎克伯格：AI代理开发进展慢于预期</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48767058' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 159</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48767058' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 302</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Zuckerberg acknowledges that AI agent development is proceeding more slowly than hoped, citing technical and safety challenges. The admission tempers hype around near-term autonomous tools and underscores the need for thoughtful deployment, governance, and compute investment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>扎克伯格承认 AI 代理开发进展慢于预期，指出技术与安全方面的挑战。这一表态为市场对近期开源、自主代理工具的乐观预期降温，也提示需要更稳健的治理与算力投入。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(211) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://schamper.dev/dissecting-apples-sparse-image-format-asif/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dissecting Apple&#x27;s Sparse Image Format (ASIF)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>解剖苹果的稀疏图像格式（ASIF）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48708644' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 158</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48708644' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple&#x27;s ASIF sparse image format is a storage-efficient image format used by macOS for disk images. The article delves into how ASIF organizes sparse data, its on-disk layout, and practical implications for tooling, backups, and performance when dealing with large disk images. For developers and storage engineers, understanding ASIF can inform how to read, write, and optimize sparse images.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章解析苹果的稀疏图像格式 ASIF 的数据组织与磁盘布局，阐明了它在处理大规模磁盘镜像时的存储效率与性能影响。内容涵盖对工具链、备份与读写性能的实际影响，帮助开发者和存储工程师更好地读取、写入和优化稀疏镜像。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A meta-critique on the cycle of AI-generated misinformation and newsroom response: as AI creates fake content, observers warn it threatens real journalism, pushing for transparency, verification, and sustainable business models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于 AI 生成的假新闻对新闻业的影响的元叙事：AI 假新闻抱怨自身的存在及其对真实新闻的冲击，同时引发对验证、透明度和商业模式的反思。</p>
