<p class='hn-title'>(213) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.telegraph.co.uk/business/2026/06/28/ai-boom-risks-global-financial-crash-central-bankers-warn/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI boom risks global financial crash, warn central bankers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 泛滥可能引发全球金融崩溃，央行警告</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48713697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 157</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48713697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 210</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Central bankers warn that AI-driven growth could threaten global financial stability, citing risks such as market volatility and leverage concentration. The discussion stresses the need for macroprudential tools and resilient infrastructure as AI reshapes asset pricing and liquidity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>央行家警告，AI 驱动的增长可能威胁全球金融稳定，称可能带来市场波动、杠杆集中等风险。讨论强调需要宏观审慎工具和韧性基础设施来应对 AI 重塑资产定价与流动性的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(217) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.databricks.com/blog/lakebase-ltap-rethinking-database-storage' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Postgres data stored in Parquet on S3: LTAP architecture explained</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Postgres 数据以 Parquet 存储在 S3：LTAP 架构解读</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48745855' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 156</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48745855' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The LTAP approach stores relational data in Parquet on S3 to combine SQL capabilities with cloud object storage scalability. The post outlines how this architecture enables scalable analytics while preserving some transactional semantics, and discusses trade-offs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LTAP 架构将关系数据以 Parquet 方式存入 S3，结合了 SQL 能力与云对象存储的可扩展性。文中解释了在保持部分事务语义的同时实现可扩展分析的要点及权衡。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(218) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://epoch.ai/data-insights/cve-severity-spike' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New serious vulnerabilities spiked around release of Claude Mythos Preview</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Mythos Preview 发布期出现的新严重漏洞激增</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48780056' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 155</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48780056' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 72</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Security researchers observed a spike in high-severity vulnerabilities around the release of Claude Mythos Preview, with several CVEs reported. The trend underscores the security risks that accompany major AI model releases and the need for rapid patching.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Mythos Preview 发布期出现的新严重漏洞数量激增，相关 CVE 报告增多。这一趋势凸显大型 AI 模型发布伴随的安全风险与快速修补的必要性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(219) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vosen.github.io/ZLUDA/blog/zluda-update-q1q2-2026/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zluda 6 release (run unmodified CUDA applications on non-Nvidia GPUs)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Zluda 6 发布：在非 Nvidia GPU 上运行未修改的 CUDA 应用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48730713' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 154</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48730713' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Zluda 6 releases material to run unmodified CUDA apps on non-NVIDIA GPUs, bridging CUDA software to AMD/Intel GPUs. The update expands hardware freedom for AI and HPC workloads and challenges vendor lock-in. It signals progress toward more diverse GPU ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Zluda 6 发布实现将未修改的 CUDA 应用在非 NVIDIA GPU 上运行，打破了对 CUDA 专属硬件的依赖边界。对 AI 与 HPC 工作负载的硬件选择带来更多自由，也是在推动多厂商生态的发展。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章回顾螺旋虱的灭绝与再度复发，介绍利用灭虫法实现显著控制，以及新挑战可能威胁这项成就。通过生物控制案例探讨生态与生物安全的相关经验。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(224) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://intextbooks.science.uu.nl/workshop2026/files/itb26_s1s2.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New AI tutor achieves 0.71-1.30 SD effect size in Dartmouth course [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>达特茅斯课程中的新AI辅导取得0.71–1.30标准差的效应量</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48796817' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 151</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48796817' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 90</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new AI tutoring system delivered measurable gains in a Dartmouth course, achieving an effect size in the 0.71–1.30 SD range. This demonstrates AI tutors&#x27; potential to scale personalized help, though results may vary by subject, and practical deployment raises equity and privacy considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个新的AI辅导系统在达特茅斯的一门课程中实现了可衡量的学习提升，效应量落在0.71到1.30标准差区间。这表明AI辅导在大规模提供个性化帮助方面具有潜力，但不同学科差异、以及实际部署中的公平性与隐私问题需要关注。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(226) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sqlite.org/howtocorrupt.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How to corrupt an SQLite database file</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A cautionary guide on how SQLite database files can become corrupted, detailing common failure modes and how to defend against them. It emphasizes using WAL or journaling, backups, and integrity checks to maintain data safety.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文警示性地介绍 SQLite 数据库文件可能被损坏的常见原因及防护要点，强调使用 WAL/日志、定期备份和完整性检查来保障数据安全。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(227) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/hyper-bug/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We found a bug in the hyper HTTP library</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 Hyper HTTP 库中发现一个 Bug</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48670314' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 149</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48670314' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 73</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare researchers found a bug in the Hyper HTTP library, a core Rust HTTP stack used by many projects. The issue could affect correctness or performance and has prompted a patch and escalation to upstream. The incident underscores how even mature dependencies can harbor subtle defects with wide-scale impact on web infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 的研究团队在 Rust 的 Hyper HTTP 库里发现了一个 Bug，该库是许多项目的核心 HTTP 栈。该问题可能影响正确性或性能，现已提交补丁并上报上游。此事凸显了即使是成熟的依赖也可能隐藏微妙的缺陷，对网页基础设施有广泛影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(229) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pcmag.com/opinions/the-us-used-to-demand-the-best-tech-now-we-ban-it' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The US Used to Demand the Best Tech. Now We Ban It</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国曾坚持要求最佳技术，现在却在禁止它</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48710437' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 148</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48710437' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 100</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A PCMag opinion argues the US has shifted from championing cutting-edge tech to restricting or banning certain technologies, citing export controls and procurement biases. The piece critiques policy choices that may stifle innovation and global competitiveness in software and hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PCMag 的一篇观点文章认为美国在科技政策上已从追求最先进的技术转向禁止或限制，涉及出口管制与采购规则。作者认为此类政策可能抑制创新，削弱全球竞争力。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(230) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://adamraudonis.github.io/splats4D/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fable created novel 4D splat format</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Fable 创建了新型 4D Splat 格式</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48786245' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 148</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48786245' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 56</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new 4D splat format extends splat rendering into the time dimension, enabling dynamic volumetric graphics and evolving data visualization. The approach shows how compact primitives can encode more information, potentially impacting graphics pipelines and scientific visualization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>4D Splat 格式让体渲染扩展到时间维度，支持动态图形和随时间变化的数据可视化。通过简单的基本图元即可编码更多信息，或将影响图形管线和科学可视化的实现。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48683475' data-tags='Hardware,Design,Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(231) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lucassifoni.info/blog/hiking-with-wallace/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Wallace the 6 inch f/2.8 telescope, building it, and hiking with it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Wallace 6 英寸 f/2.8 望远镜：自制与徒步携带</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48683475' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 147</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48683475' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Wallace the 6 inch f/2.8 telescope presents a compact, fast-aperture instrument built for hikers. The project details the build, gear choices, and on-trail performance, offering a blueprint for portable astrophotography gear. It illustrates how small design choices expand observing opportunities outside the observatory.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Wallace 6 英寸 f/2.8 望远镜是一件便携、对焦快的徒步向望远镜。文中分享了自制过程、选用的装备与野外实测，提供了便携天文摄影的可行思路。展示了小型设计如何扩展户外观测的边界。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48782219' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(232) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://surfingcomplexity.blog/2026/07/03/synthesis-is-harder-than-analysis/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Synthesis is harder than analysis</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>综合比分析更难</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48782219' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 147</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48782219' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that building or synthesizing systems from first principles is much harder than dissecting them, with implications for software engineering, AI safety, and model interpretability. It advocates more emphasis on constructive methods to advance robust systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为从第一性原理合成系统远比分析现有系统困难，这对软件工程、AI 安全和模型可解释性都产生影响。倡导采用更着重构建性的方法来推动鲁棒系统的发展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://surfingcomplexity.blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/image-6.png' data-full='https://surfingcomplexity.blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/image-6.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这门 2018 年的应用范畴理论课程提供实际入门，展示范函子、单元等概念如何与编程、数据组织和流程推理相连。帮助读者把高层数学思想应用到真实计算问题中。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Windows debugging note discusses a DLL that wasn&#x27;t present in memory even though the loader hadn&#x27;t formally unloaded it, revealing quirks in module management. It highlights nuances between loaded modules and actual memory residency useful for debugging.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇微软技术博客记录了一个 DLL 即使未被正式卸载，内存中却并不存在的情况，揭示了模块管理中的细微差别。此现象对调试、内存映射和性能分析具有参考价值。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(239) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/06/eff-and-allies-xs-ftc-petition-waive-privacy-violation-order-should-be-rejected' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EFF letter to FTC on X consent order [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>电子前线基金会致 FTC 关于 X 同意令的信</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48766209' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 139</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48766209' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 59</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The EFF and allied groups asked the FTC to deny a petition to waive parts of a privacy-related consent order, arguing the platform has not satisfied its obligations and that public privacy risks remain. The letter calls for sustained enforcement and transparency rather than early relief for the company.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>电子前线基金会及其盟友请求 FTC 拒绝一项豁免隐私相关同意令的申请，认为该平台并未履行义务且公众隐私风险依然存在。信中主张应继续加强执法与透明度，而非提前给予公司宽免。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/twitter-elon.png' data-full='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/twitter-elon.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(243) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Is One Layer Enough? A Single Transformer Layer Matches Full-Parameter RL Train</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48760201' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 136</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48760201' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Is One Layer Enough? A Single Transformer Layer Matches Full-Parameter RL Train</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究表明，在特定设置下，单层 Transformer 也能达到与全参数 RL 训练相当的效果，挑战了对深度的普遍依赖观。对模型设计与训练成本具有启示。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(244) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.readtheline.ca/p/al-vigier-canadas-ai-strategy-shouldnt' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Al Vigier: Canada&#x27;s AI strategy shouldn&#x27;t include secret Palantir bills</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Al Vigier：加拿大的 AI 战略应避免秘密的 Palantir 账单</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48799256' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 133</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48799256' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 56</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Al Vigier argues Canada should craft its AI strategy with transparency and public accountability, avoiding opaque deals with private contractors. The piece advocates open governance and alignment with national interests over hidden Palantir-style billing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>阿尔·维杰主张加拿大的 AI 战略应公开透明，避免与私营机构的神秘账单相连。文章倡导问责式治理，强调将公共利益置于商业交易之上。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1713345248737-2698000f143d?crop=entropy&amp;cs=tinysrgb&amp;fit=max&amp;fm=jpg&amp;ixid=M3wzMDAzMzh8MHwxfHNlYXJjaHwxMnx8YWl8ZW58MHx8fHwxNzgxNjIwMjk1fDA&amp;ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;q=80&amp;w=1080' data-full='https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1713345248737-2698000f143d?crop=entropy&amp;cs=tinysrgb&amp;fit=max&amp;fm=jpg&amp;ixid=M3wzMDAzMzh8MHwxfHNlYXJjaHwxMnx8YWl8ZW58MHx8fHwxNzgxNjIwMjk1fDA&amp;ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;q=80&amp;w=1080' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(245) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://homegames.io' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Homegames. An open-source game platform I&#x27;ve been making for 8 years</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Homegames——我8年来在打造的开源游戏平台</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48798153' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 133</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48798153' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Eight years into development, Homegames is an open-source game platform designed for modular, community-driven extension. It demonstrates how a long-running indie project can mature into a usable platform despite sustainability challenges, with architecture that invites contributions from hobbyists and developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>经过8年的开发，Homegames 成为一个面向模块化扩展、由社区驱动的开源游戏平台。该项目展示了如何让一个长期的独立项目逐步成熟为可用平台，同时也暴露出维护与持续性的挑战，以及激励社区贡献的架构设计。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(247) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.seattletimes.com/business/local-business/as-downtown-seattle-offices-empty-city-facing-years-of-zombie-towers/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>As downtown Seattle offices empty, city facing years of &#x27;zombie&#x27; towers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>西雅图市中心办公楼空置，城市面临多年‘僵尸塔’</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48787725' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 133</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48787725' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 181</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vacant downtown Seattle offices are creating &#x27;zombie towers&#x27; as work patterns shift, impacting tax revenue, city services, and urban planning. The trend could accelerate repurposing of office spaces into residential or community uses, affecting real estate and local economy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>西雅图市中心大量办公楼空置，形成长期的“僵尸塔”现象，影响税收、城市服务与城市规划。这一趋势可能推动办公楼改造为住宅或公共用途，对地产市场和地方经济产生连锁影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.seattletimes.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/07012026_TZR_Seattle_Liggett_Building-03042026-KC_t.jpg?d=1200x630' data-full='https://images.seattletimes.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/07012026_TZR_Seattle_Liggett_Building-03042026-KC_t.jpg?d=1200x630' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(248) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://worksinprogress.co/issue/how-amsterdam-invented-the-fire-department/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The firefighting system of the Van der Heyden brothers in 17th century Amsterdam</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>17 世纪阿姆斯特丹的消防系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48780913' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 133</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48780913' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A historical look at Amsterdam’s firefighting system and how the Van der Heyden brothers helped organize early municipal fire response. The piece connects early public safety engineering to modern urban resilience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讲述17世纪阿姆斯特丹的消防系统，以及范德海登兄弟如何推动早期城市消防组织。通过历史案例连接早期公共安全工程与现代城市韧性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(249) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.forbes.com/sites/suzannerowankelleher/2026/06/30/faa-supersonic-flight-no-boom/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Supersonic flight returning to US after half-century ban</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在半个世纪禁令后，超音速飞行重新回到美国</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48741654' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 133</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48741654' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 163</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The FAA signals a path toward resuming supersonic flight with lower-noise standards, enabling experiments that avoid the sonic boom that fueled the half-century ban. The move could unlock faster air travel and spur aerospace innovation, while presenting regulatory and community challenges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国联邦航空局表示有望在更低噪声标准下恢复超音速飞行测试，结束半个世纪的禁令。这将促进更快的空中旅行和航空创新，但也带来监管与社区影响的挑战。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48773079' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(252) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pandoc.org/lua-filters.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pandoc Lua Filters</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Pandoc 的 Lua 过滤器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48773079' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 131</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48773079' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pandoc Lua filters let you customize document conversion by writing small Lua scripts that operate on Pandoc&#x27;s AST. They enable repeatable, automated transformations—like formatting tweaks, metadata changes, or export customization—without altering the core pipeline. This is a powerful tool for developers building complex publishing workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pandoc 的 Lua 过滤器允许你通过编写 Lua 脚本来定制文档转换，直接操作 Pandoc 的抽象语法树。它支持可重复的自动化转换，如格式调整、元数据修改和导出定制，而无需改动核心管线。对于构建复杂出版工作流的开发者，这是一个强大的工具。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.paypalobjects.com/en_US/i/scr/pixel.gif' data-full='https://www.paypalobjects.com/en_US/i/scr/pixel.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NSA 企图削弱 MLKEM 标准化进程</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48760490' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 129</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48760490' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 76</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NSA tries to weaken mlkem standardisation</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称国家安全局试图削弱 ML-KEM 等标准化进程，引发关于开放标准与国家安全之间的辩论。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48720591' data-tags='Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(255) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://philipbohun.com/blog/0011.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft Needs Windows Lite</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软需要 Windows Lite</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48720591' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 126</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48720591' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 172</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author argues for a lighter, more modular Windows edition—Windows Lite—tailored to low-footprint devices and cloud-centric use cases. The concept emphasizes reducing system footprint, splitting core OS from optional components, and improving efficiency for constrained hardware and services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者提出需要一个更轻量、模块化的 Windows 版本——Windows Lite，面向资源受限设备和云端场景。该构想强调降低系统足迹、把核心系统与可选组件分离，并提升对受限硬件和服务的效率。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(256) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chipsandcheese.com/p/top500-at-isc26-we-have-a-new-number' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>TOP500 at ISC’26: We have a New Number 1 Supercomputer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ISC’26 TOP500：诞生新一代第一的超级计算机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48710775' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 126</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48710775' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 84</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ISC’26 TOP500 reveals a new number-one supercomputer, underscoring ongoing advances toward exascale performance. The shift reflects how HPC architectures—driven by accelerators and energy-aware design—reshape workloads in science, AI, and simulation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ISC’26 TOP500 公布新一代第一名超级计算机，凸显向百亿亿级性能迈进的持续进展。此变动反映出以加速器与节能设计为驱动的 HPC 架构正在重塑科学、AI 与仿真等工作负载。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(257) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://waag.org/en/article/why-we-moved-our-bluesky-data-eurosky/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We moved our Bluesky data to Eurosky</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们把 Bluesky 数据迁移到 Eurosky</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48733937' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 126</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48733937' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 102</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An organization recounts moving Bluesky data to Eurosky, with lessons on data portability, performance, and governance. The migration sheds light on data sovereignty choices and the trade-offs of vendor ecosystems for social platforms. It provides practical takeaways for running distributed social networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>记录将 Bluesky 数据迁移到 Eurosky 的经验教训，涵盖数据可携性、性能与治理等方面。此案例凸显数据主权与生态系统选择的权衡，对运营分布式社媒平台具有现实意义。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CachyOS 2026 年 6 月版带来功能更新、性能提升和软件包刷新，遵循滚动发布模型，通常还包含内核与桌面方面的改进。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48662018' data-tags='Hardware,DevOps,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(259) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://edera.dev/stories/numa-part-1-cores-memory-and-the-distance-between-them' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NUMA: Cores, memory, and the distance between them</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NUMA：核心、内存与它们之间的距离</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48662018' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 124</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48662018' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NUMA architecture matters because the distance between a CPU core and its memory affects latency and bandwidth. The piece explains how memory locality and interconnect topology shape real-world performance, and why thread placement and data placement strategies matter for databases, analytics, and HPC workloads. It highlights practical guidelines for software design and system tuning to minimize cross-node traffic.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NUMA 架构决定了 CPU 内核与内存之间的距离会直接影响延迟和带宽。文中讲解了内存局部性与互连拓扑如何在实际场景中拉高或拉低性能，以及为什么线程与数据放置策略对数据库、分析或高性能计算工作负载至关重要。给出降低跨节点通信开销的实用建议。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/68dac009699a76c0c70f4c43/6a3af210c4bc99808d73bd4e_Full-NUMA%20Explained_%20Why%20Memory%20Distance%20Slows%20Your%20VMs.png' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/68dac009699a76c0c70f4c43/6a3af210c4bc99808d73bd4e_Full-NUMA%20Explained_%20Why%20Memory%20Distance%20Slows%20Your%20VMs.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(260) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://craigmod.com/essays/fast_software/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fast Software, the Best Software (2019)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>快速软件，最好的软件（2019）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48792008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 123</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48792008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 69</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A reflective essay on shipping fast and iterating over perfect software, arguing that speed and usability often trump perfection in real-world development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于快速迭代优于追求完美的反思，主张在现实开发中速度与可用性往往比追求完美更重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://craigmod.com/images/about/craigmod-headshot-2025-small-01.jpg' data-full='https://craigmod.com/images/about/craigmod-headshot-2025-small-01.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(261) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.qualcomm.com/developer/blog/2026/06/qualcomm-linux-2-now-available' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Qualcomm Linux 2.0</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>高通 Linux 2.0 发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48753069' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 123</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48753069' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Qualcomm announces Linux 2.0, a major update broadening Linux support across Snapdragon platforms with new drivers, tooling, and security/performance improvements. The release emphasizes open-source collaboration and easier developer onboarding for embedded and mobile hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>高通宣布 Linux 2.0 发布，扩大对 Snapdragon 平台的 Linux 支持，并带来新驱动、工具链和改进的安全与性能。此版本强调开源协作以及在嵌入式与移动硬件上更便捷的开发者上手体验。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48730818' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(262) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cekrem.github.io/posts/parse-dont-validate-typescript/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Parse, Don&#x27;t Validate – In a Language That Doesn&#x27;t Want You To</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>解析优先，勿以验证——在一种不鼓励你验证的语言里</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48730818' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 122</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48730818' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 94</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Promotes parsing inputs into typed structures rather than validate them in a language that discourages such practice, using TypeScript as a case study. The piece advocates language-aware design to minimize runtime errors and improve correctness. It provides practical patterns for robust parsing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在一种不鼓励按常规做法的语言中，主张优先解析输入结构而非逐步验证的设计思想，结合 TypeScript 的案例，给出实用的解析模式与反例。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cekrem.github.io/images/banner.jpg' data-full='https://cekrem.github.io/images/banner.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48766286' data-tags='Science,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(263) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://growtheflowutah.org/laketracker/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Great Salt Lake Tracker</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大盐湖追踪器：Grow the Flow</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48766286' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 122</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48766286' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Great Salt Lake Tracker, part of Grow the Flow, collects lake-level observations to monitor hydrological changes and climate impacts. It demonstrates how citizen science and local data can inform water management decisions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Great Salt Lake Tracker 是 Grow the Flow 计划的一部分，收集大盐湖水位等观测数据，监测水文变化与气候影响。该项目展示了公民科学与本地数据在水资源管理中的应用潜力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48738395' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(264) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://iahmed.me/post/drone-physics/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Drone Physics</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>无人机物理学</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48738395' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 121</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48738395' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Drone Physics explains core flight dynamics—thrust, drag, lift, and moments—and how they translate into acceleration, attitude, and control in drone simulations. The post helps readers understand what to model when building physics-based drone simulations or control algorithms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文讲解无人机飞行动力学的核心要素，如推力、阻力、升力以及力矩，如何将它们转化为加速度、姿态和控制。为构建物理仿真或控制算法时需要建模的要点提供直观指引。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://iahmed.me/post/drone-physics/static/icon.png' data-full='https://iahmed.me/post/drone-physics/static/icon.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48766242' data-tags='Show HN,Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(265) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/flythenimbus/bramble' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Bramble – Local-first password manager</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Bramble——本地优先的密码管理器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48766242' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 121</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48766242' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bramble is a local first password manager that prioritizes offline storage and privacy, with optional sync that preserves user control. The Show HN signals a compelling alternative to cloud centric password managers and invites discussion on threat models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bramble 作为本地优先的密码管理器，强调离线存储与隐私保护，支持可控的同步。Show HN 的展示凸显了对比云端为主的密码管理方案的潜在优势，并引发关于威胁模型的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/510629dec1f10e7614cdb0898f70e09ab0d1ac3e953dd4389dd2586f6be0d718/flythenimbus/bramble' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/510629dec1f10e7614cdb0898f70e09ab0d1ac3e953dd4389dd2586f6be0d718/flythenimbus/bramble' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48754155' data-tags='Health,Science,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(266) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.cuanschutz.edu/news-stories/healthy-but-sedentary-individuals-show-early-decline-in-cellular-energy-production' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Healthy but sedentary people show early decline in cellular energy production</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>健康但久坐的人群细胞能量产生出现早期下降</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48754155' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 120</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48754155' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 79</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Healthy adults who lead sedentary lives show early declines in cellular energy production, signaling mitochondrial function may deteriorate before metabolic markers change. This underscores that even apparent health can be compromised by inactivity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>即使是健康但久坐的成年人，也会出现细胞能量产生的早期下降，提示线粒体功能在代谢指标改变之前就受到影响。这强调了久坐对健康的隐性风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.cuanschutz.edu/hubfs/2026-06_reduced%20mitochondrial%20efficiency%20(1).png' data-full='https://news.cuanschutz.edu/hubfs/2026-06_reduced%20mitochondrial%20efficiency%20(1).png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48712420' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(269) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.07013' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Knowledge Distillation of Black-Box Large Language Models (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>对黑箱大语言模型的知识蒸馏（2024）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48712420' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 117</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48712420' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This 2024 work studies distilling knowledge from black-box LLMs into smaller, trainable models without access to internal weights. Through comparisons of techniques like output-based distillation and data augmentation, it shows trade-offs between fidelity and efficiency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇 2024 年的工作研究在无法访问内部权重的前提下，将黑箱大语言模型的知识蒸馏到更小的模型中。通过对比输出蒸馏、数据增强等方法，揭示保真度与效率之间的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48763790' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 117</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48763790' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 230</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The 24-bit/192kHz claim often fails to justify its file size given perceptual limits and encoding realities that favor more efficient choices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章论述为何 24 位/192kHz 的音乐下载在实际听感和编码成本上并无意义，强调更务实的音质选择。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(271) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://underhanded-c.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Underhanded C Contest</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>暗箱 C 竞赛</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48754080' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 116</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48754080' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Underhanded C Contest is a long-running programming challenge that invites writers to craft C code with intentional, harmless deception to test compilers and readers. It highlights clever quirks and how code can be both creative and deceptive, underscoring the importance of readability and security.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Underhanded C Contest 是一个长期的编程挑战，鼓励人们用有意的、无害的误导来编写 C 代码，以考验编译器与读者的警觉性。它揭示了语言特性与编译器的边界，既考验创造力，也提醒关注可读性与安全性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(274) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.freepressjournal.in/education/breaking-the-bird-barrier-scientist-decodes-zebra-finch-language' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Breaking the Bird Barrier: Scientist Decodes Zebra Finch Language</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>打破鸟类语言障碍：科学家解码斑胸草雀语言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48739446' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 115</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48739446' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers claim progress in decoding zebra finch vocalizations, suggesting structure in their communication that could illuminate how non-human brains encode meaning. The work adds to the debate on animal language and has implications for neuroscience and linguistics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者在解码斑胸草雀鸣声方面取得进展，暗示它们的交流可能具有结构，能在非人脑中编码意义。这一发现推动动物沟通研究，也引发关于语言学与神经科学的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你能对你的软件自信地保证什么？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48719521' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 113</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48719521' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 59</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Questions the realism of guarantees in software, discussing what can be proven or certified, and how to communicate bounds, risks, and reliability to stakeholders.</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Derek Thompson piece revisits a 1926 report about America, revealing foresight on technology, work, and society that still resonates today. The essay uses the artifact to illuminate ongoing debates about automation and national progress.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文回顾一份源自1926年的被遗忘报告，揭示对美国生活的超前观察，涉及技术、工作与社会等议题，至今仍与当下的自动化与国家发展讨论相呼应。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(277) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://verdagon.dev/blog/ante-blending-borrowing-rc' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ante: A new way to blend borrow checking and reference counting</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ante：一种混合借用检查与引用计数的新方法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48710770' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 112</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48710770' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ante proposes a hybrid approach that blends compile-time borrow checking with runtime reference counting to improve memory safety and flexibility. By combining the strengths of both models, it aims to reduce common pitfalls in systems programming.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ante 提出把编译时借用检查与运行时引用计数融合的混合方案，以提升内存安全性与灵活性。通过结合两种模型的优势，旨在减少系统编程中的常见风险。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Zuckerberg told staff that AI agents haven&#x27;t progressed as fast as hoped, signaling a cautious, iterative approach to AI product development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>扎克伯格向员工表示 AI 代理的进展没有达到预期的速度，显示出对 AI 产品开发采取更为审慎、迭代式的策略。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48768336' data-tags='Science,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(290) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.dailymail.com/news/article-15943905/Mystery-identity-Green-Boots-climber-macabre-landmark-frozen-ice-dying-Everest-finally-solved-DNA-test.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mystery identity of &#x27;Green Boots&#x27; climber is finally solved after DNA test</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>“绿靴子”登山者身份终于通过 DNA 检测揭晓</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48768336' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 106</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48768336' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 62</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A decades-old Everest mystery has been resolved: DNA testing has identified the climber known as &#x27;Green Boots&#x27;, whose body was long used as an on-route landmark. The identification closes a long-standing question while reopening ethical debates about bodies on high-altitude routes and memorialization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>几十年的珠峰谜案有了结果：通过 DNA 鉴定确认了被称为“绿靴子”的登山者身份，这具尸体长期被用作路线地标。身份确认结束了悬而未决的问题，但也再次引发了关于高海拔遗体处置与纪念方式的伦理讨论。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Radiation Exposure Lie interrogates how risk is communicated and sometimes distorted around radiation. It highlights misperceptions that can influence policy and public behavior, reminding technologists to demand clear, data-driven risk framing. The piece underscores a broader caution about science communication in tech policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《辐射暴露的谎言》质疑有关辐射风险的传播和常见扭曲，揭示可能影响政策与公众行为的认知偏差。作者强调需要清晰、以数据为基础的风险表达，提醒科技从业者关注科技政策中的科学传播问题。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece revisits debates about sunscreen safety, efficacy, and broader sun-exposure science, questioning whether sunscreen alone is a sufficient protective strategy. It advocates a nuanced sun-safety approach, combining shade, clothing, and mindful exposure with sunscreen where appropriate.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章重新审视防晒霜的安全性与效果，以及阳光暴露科学的最新证据，质疑单靠防晒霜是否足以保护健康。提出综合的防晒策略，结合遮阳、穿着与科学使用防晒霜等做法。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(300) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://escapepod.org/2013/09/14/ep413-why-i-left-harrys-all-night-hamburgers/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I Left Harry&#x27;s All-Night Hamburgers (2013)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我离开了Harry’s All-Night Hamburgers（深夜汉堡店）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48753220' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 102</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48753220' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal essay about leaving a longtime late-night burger spot, examining the pull of local institutions against changing tastes and business dynamics. It touches on loyalty, community, and the evolving value of traditional eateries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇个人散文，讲述放弃久违的深夜汉堡店的经历，探讨本地机构、口味变迁与商业环境的冲突。涉及忠诚、社区以及传统餐饮的变现价值等话题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://escapepod.org/wp-content/uploads/2021/01/cropped-EscapePod_banner_1280.jpg' data-full='https://escapepod.org/wp-content/uploads/2021/01/cropped-EscapePod_banner_1280.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(301) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.filfre.net/2026/07/the-life-and-times-of-maxis-part-1-simeverything/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Life and Times of Maxis, Part 1: SimEverything</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Maxis 的生平与时代 第1部分：模拟一切</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48776525' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 100</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48776525' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A deep dive into Maxis, the studio behind sim-centric titles, tracing its early history and the rise of simulation-first design. Part 1 explores how the company&#x27;s focus on simulating systems shaped its games and influenced software culture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>深入梳理 Maxis 的历史与设计哲学，聚焦“模拟一切”的理念及其在早期游戏中的体现，以及它对软件文化的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(302) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.zerofs.net/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: ZeroFS – A log-structured filesystem for S3</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：ZeroFS——面向 S3 的日志结构文件系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48761493' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 98</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48761493' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ZeroFS is a log-structured filesystem for S3. It uses a log-structured approach atop S3, enabling append-only writes and efficient compaction for object storage workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ZeroFS 在 S3 之上实现日志结构文件系统，采用追加写入与高效整理的方式，提升对象存储场景下的写入效率与管理性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48792203' data-tags='Security,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(308) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.devever.net/~hl/webcrypto' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Web-based cryptography is always snake oil</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>基于网页的加密总是蛇油</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48792203' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 96</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48792203' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 104</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Web-based cryptography is convenient but often misleading because browser APIs can mislead developers about security guarantees. The piece cautions about entropy quality, misuse risks, and the gap between theory and implementation, advocating back-end cryptography and vetted libraries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>网页加密虽便捷，但常常让人误以为更安全，原因在于浏览器 API 的熵源质量、误用风险及理论与实现之间的差距。文章建议使用后端加密、经验证的库，以及在设计时明确威胁模型。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48790912' data-tags='AI,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(309) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Log is the Agent</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>日志即代理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48790912' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 96</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48790912' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The paper argues that logs can function as agents, shaping decision-making and action in AI systems by serving as a record of behavior and an interface for planning. It examines how logs can be used to ground agent behavior, enabling reproducibility and better governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>论文提出日志本身可以充当代理，在 AI 系统中通过记录行为与作为规划接口来影响决策与行动。讨论了如何用日志来支撑代理行为的可复现性与可治理性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48724127' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(310) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.mixedbread.com/blog/asymmetric-quant' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Asymmetric Quantization: Near-Lossless Retrieval with 97% Storage Reduction</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>非对称量化：实现近损检索，存储可减少约97%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48724127' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 96</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48724127' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new asymmetric quantization technique enables near-lossless retrieval while reducing storage by about 97% for embeddings. This makes large-scale vector databases more affordable and faster to query with acceptable accuracy trade-offs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一种新的非对称量化技术实现近损检索，嵌入向量存储量约减少97%。这使大规模向量数据库的成本显著下降，查询速度更快，精度权衡在可接受范围内。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.mixedbread.com/images/blog/asym-quant/asym-quant.jpg' data-full='https://www.mixedbread.com/images/blog/asym-quant/asym-quant.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AArch64 桌面实验的终结</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48728599' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 92</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48728599' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An engineer explains why the long-running AArch64 desktop experiment is winding down, citing software ecosystem maturity, driver gaps, and performance constraints. The piece signals a shift away from ARM-based desktop hopes toward server or embedded roles. It frames the decision as a pragmatic reallocation of resources.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者表明 AArch64 桌面实验逐渐走向尽头，原因包括生态系统不成熟、驱动与兼容性不足以及性能瓶颈。这意味着将 ARM 桌面的尝试转向服务器或嵌入式场景的现实需求。文章将其视为资源重新分配的务实选择。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A sprawling network of cave-like rooms and gardens carved by hand, Forestiere Underground Gardens is a striking example of early 20th-century subterranean architecture. Visitors experience a self-contained oasis with a climate-controlled interior carved from local rock.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Forestiere 地下花园是一组由人工开凿的洞室和庭园的庞大网络，是20世纪早期地下建筑的引人注目案例。游客在内部体验由本地岩石打造、实现自给自足的气候调控的“世外桃源”。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(324) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20049' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Does code cleanliness affect coding agents? A controlled minimal-pair study</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>代码清洁度会影响编程代理吗？一项受控的最小对比研究</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48798815' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 85</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48798815' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A controlled minimal-pair study investigates whether code cleanliness affects coding agents. The results suggest cleaner, more readable code helps agents produce more reliable outputs, with implications for prompt design and when outsourcing coding tasks to AI. It argues readability isn&#x27;t just a human virtue; it can guide AI tooling too.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项受控的最小对比研究发现，代码的可读性确实会影响编码代理的表现，整洁的代码能让代理产出更可靠的结果。这意味着在进行 AI 辅助编码时，仍应重视代码结构与可维护性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个关于报废飞机场的清单与地图，揭示飞机退役与回收的规模，便于航空爱好者和研究者追踪拆解市场与供应链动态。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(326) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/langchain-ai/openwiki' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenWiki: CLI that writes and maintains agent documentation for your codebase</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenWiki：用于代码库的命令行工具，自动撰写与维护文档</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48752949' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 85</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48752949' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenWiki CLI automatically creates and maintains doc content for codebases, especially agent-related documentation, easing onboarding and knowledge transfer.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenWiki 是一个命令行工具，能为代码库自动生成与维护文档，尤其是面向智能代理的资料，降低新员工上手难度。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48762372' data-tags='Programming,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(327) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://apple.github.io/foundationdb/flow.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FoundationDB&#x27;s Flow – Bringing Actor-Based Concurrency to C++11</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FoundationDB 的 Flow：将基于 Actor 的并发带到 C++11</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48762372' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 85</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48762372' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Flow is an actor-style concurrency framework used in FoundationDB that implements lightweight actors and cooperative scheduling in portable C++11. It provides a model for writing highly concurrent database internals without relying on OS threads per actor, enabling scalable, deterministic task interleaving inside a C++ codebase.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Flow 是 FoundationDB 使用的基于 actor 的并发框架，在可移植的 C++11 中实现了轻量级 actor 和协作调度。它为在 C++ 代码库中编写高并发数据库内部逻辑提供了模型，无需为每个 actor 使用操作系统线程，从而实现可扩展且更可预测的任务交错。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48679904' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(328) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thomaswc.com/blog/the_return_of_aop.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Return of Aspect Oriented Programming</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>面向切面编程的回归</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48679904' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 84</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48679904' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 68</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Return of Aspect Oriented Programming argues that AOP is resurging as software complexity demands cleaner separation of cross-cutting concerns. It reviews modern language tooling and patterns that reduce boilerplate while introducing new pitfalls. The take: AOP remains relevant, but discipline and tooling matter more than ever.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《面向切面编程的回归》指出，随着软件复杂度上升，AOP 因更好地分离横切关注点而重新受到关注。文章评估了现代语言工具与设计模式如何降低样板代码，同时也提醒潜在的实现风险。核心结论是：AOP 仍具价值，但需要合适的工具与一定的自律。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48796784' data-tags='Startups,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(329) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/tech-industry/atomic-semi-rebrands-as-fab2-and-shifts-operations-to-texas' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Jim Keller&#x27;s startup is building a factory to mass-produce small chip fabs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Jim Keller 创业公司正建厂量产小型晶圆厂</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48796784' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 84</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48796784' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Jim Keller&#x27;s startup (Fab2) shifts to Texas to build a factory aimed at mass-producing small chip fabs, signaling a push toward localized, modular semiconductor manufacturing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>吉姆·凯勒的初创公司转向德州建设工厂，目标是大规模生产小型芯片代工厂的设备与工艺，推动本地化的半导体制造。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/groRd4wteVARFynpqoPiqc-1920-80.png' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/groRd4wteVARFynpqoPiqc-1920-80.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48724995' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(330) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://reclaimthenet.org/googles-new-recaptcha-wants-your-camera-access-and-21-points-of-your-hand' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google&#x27;s New reCAPTCHA Wants Your Camera Access and 21 Points of Your Hand</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌新版 reCAPTCHA 要求访问你的摄像头及手部21个关键点</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48724995' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 84</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48724995' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google&#x27;s new reCAPTCHA variant requests camera access and analyzes 21 hand-tracking points to distinguish humans from bots. The approach raises privacy and biometric-data concerns, prompting questions about consent, data retention, and the tradeoffs between security and user experience. It adds to the ongoing debate about increasingly invasive integrity checks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌新版 reCAPTCHA 要求获取摄像头权限并分析21个手部追踪点来区分人类与机器人。这一方法引发隐私和生物数据处理的担忧，涉及同意、数据保留以及安全与用户体验之间的权衡，进一步加剧了对日益侵入性身份验证的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.reclaimthenet.org/2026/06/3FMp0HkMdAaf-scaled.jpg' data-full='https://media.reclaimthenet.org/2026/06/3FMp0HkMdAaf-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(331) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bigmessowires.com/2021/11/12/the-amazing-disk-ii-controller-card/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Apple Disk II Controller Card (2021)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Apple Disk II 控制卡</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48723102' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 84</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48723102' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Apple Disk II Controller Card revisits the hardware that made Apple II&#x27;s floppy drives practical, and chronicles design quirks, compatibility challenges, and modern recreations for hobbyists.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾 Apple Disk II 控制卡这一使 Apple II 软盘可用的关键外设，讲述其设计细节、兼容性挑战，以及面向爱好者的现代再现。</p>
