<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48760106' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(332) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nber.org/system/files/working_papers/w35310/w35310.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Is the iPhone birth control? Causal evidence from AT&amp;T&#x27;s 2007-2011 monopoly [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>iPhone 是生育控制工具吗？来自 AT&amp;T 2007–2011 垄断期的因果证据</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48760106' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 84</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48760106' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 68</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The paper exploits geographic and temporal variation from AT&amp;T’s exclusive iPhone distribution between 2007–2011 to estimate how differential early access to smartphones affected fertility. Using that natural experiment, it presents causal evidence that iPhone availability changed birth rates for certain populations, suggesting technology adoption can have measurable demographic effects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>论文利用 AT&amp;T 在 2007–2011 年对 iPhone 的独家分销造成的时空差异，来估计早期智能手机接入对生育率的影响。通过这一定然实验，研究提供了 iPhone 可用性改变特定人群出生率的因果证据，表明技术采纳会对人口统计产生可测量的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48737068' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(333) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Night_Witches' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Night Witches – all-female Soviet aviator regiment WW2</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>夜色女巫：二战时苏联女飞行大队</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48737068' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 84</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48737068' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Night Witches were an all-female Soviet WWII night-bomber regiment known for their daring low-altitude raids and endurance. The story highlights gender barriers in wartime aviation and reveals a unique facet of Soviet air warfare tactics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>“夜色女巫”是二战时期苏联的全女性夜间轰炸机大队，以低空突袭和顽强耐力闻名。她们的故事挑战了当时对女性在航空领域的刻板印象，也揭示了苏联空战战术的独特性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/c/c8/Po-2.jpg/1280px-Po-2.jpg' data-full='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/c/c8/Po-2.jpg/1280px-Po-2.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48706796' data-tags='Programming,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(334) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/rochus-keller/MUMPS/blob/main/docs/MUMPS_Primer.adoc' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The MUMPS 76 Primer – anniversary edition</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MUMPS 76 入门教程——周年纪念版</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48706796' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 82</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48706796' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>MUMPS is a programming language with deep ties to healthcare databases. The anniversary primer revisits the language’s syntax and usage, highlighting its enduring relevance and the quirks that have shaped legacy medical software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>MUMPS 是一种在医疗保健数据库领域有深远影响的编程语言。周年纪念版入门书回顾了它的语法与使用场景，强调该语言在遗留医疗软件中的持续相关性与独特之处。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/071a2f2f349924311e4d9b276b9f886858cb0c36e0d3a97d7ce33ea9bb6aba84/rochus-keller/MUMPS' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/071a2f2f349924311e4d9b276b9f886858cb0c36e0d3a97d7ce33ea9bb6aba84/rochus-keller/MUMPS' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48755075' data-tags='Design,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(335) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://magik.net/librecad/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LibreCAD in the Browser</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>浏览器中的 LibreCAD</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48755075' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 82</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48755075' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LibreCAD now runs in the browser via WebAssembly, enabling instant CAD work without desktop installs. This reflects the broader move of CAD and design tools to the web.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LibreCAD 现已通过 WebAssembly 在浏览器中运行，使用户无需桌面安装即可进行 CAD 工作，体现了 CAD/设计工具向 Web 的迁移趋势。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48746771' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(336) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.economist.com/middle-east-and-africa/2026/06/25/medieval-style-fortifications-are-back-in-the-sahel' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Medieval-style fortifications are back in the Sahel</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>萨赫勒地区再现中世纪风格的防御工事</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48746771' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 81</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48746771' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 65</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece notes a resurgence of medieval-style fortifications in the Sahel amid ongoing conflicts, reflecting how local building practices and militarization shape contemporary defense landscapes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道在持续冲突背景下，萨赫勒地区出现中世纪风格的防御工事再现，体现地方建筑与民兵化如何塑造现代防御格局。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48743462' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(337) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://parsa.wtf/cast/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>It&#x27;s not me, it&#x27;s the compiler</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不是我，是编译器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48743462' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 81</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48743462' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>It&#x27;s not me, it&#x27;s the compiler argues that compiler quirks and optimizations can explain surprising bugs more often than programmer error. It urges developers to consider compiler behavior, language rules, and miscompilations when debugging performance or correctness issues.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张很多看似个人错误的问题其实源自编译器的行为和优化，需在调试性能或正确性时考虑编译器的特性。建议开发者关注编译器对语言规则和代码生成的影响，以避免被误导。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文探讨现代芯片设计如何借鉴并演化传统体系架构，评估其在效率、鲁棒性以及延续旧思想方面的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(339) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://www.lmdb.tech/doc/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lightning Memory-Mapped Database Manager (LMDB) 1.0</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LMDB 1.0：Lightning 内存映射数据库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48766598' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 81</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48766598' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LMDB 1.0 marks a milestone for the Lightning Memory-Mapped Database, a compact, ultra-fast key-value store that uses memory-mapped I/O and supports ACID transactions. The release emphasizes stability and high performance for read-heavy workloads and embedded use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LMDB 1.0 标志着 Lightning 内存映射数据库的一个里程碑，这是一款紧凑且极快的键值存储，使用内存映射 I/O 并支持 ACID 事务。该版本强调在读密集型工作负载和嵌入式场景中的稳定性与高性能。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Personal essay tracing the origins of North America&#x27;s oat supply chain and its fragility in a global food system. It argues that rethinking sourcing, public–private collaboration, and data-tracking could remake oats into a more resilient, affordable staple.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过讲述作者父亲参与建立北美燕麦供应链的经历，揭示其脆弱性与历史根源。文章建议改造原料来源、推动公私协作、提升数据追踪能力，以使燕麦供应更具韧性且更具价格稳定性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(343) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.euronews.com/next/2026/06/30/finlands-last-analogue-landline-phones-go-silent-after-150-years' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Finland&#x27;s last analogue landline phones go silent after 150 years</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>芬兰最后一台模拟有线电话在 150 年后停止使用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48786868' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 80</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48786868' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Finland&#x27;s last analogue landline phones go silent after 150 years, marking the end of an era of copper-based communications. The shift reflects broad telecoms migration to digital networks and the remaining rural connectivity challenges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>芬兰最后的模拟有线电话在使用了约150年后停止服务，标志着铜线通信时代的终结。此举体现了全球电信向数字网络迁移的趋势，同时也暴露了偏远地区的连接挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.euronews.com/articles/stories/09/81/95/28/1200x675_cmsv2_e1b57e14-96f0-5622-9dfb-5e77aeed8406-9819528.jpg' data-full='https://images.euronews.com/articles/stories/09/81/95/28/1200x675_cmsv2_e1b57e14-96f0-5622-9dfb-5e77aeed8406-9819528.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(344) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://research.google/blog/introducing-tabfm-a-zero-shot-foundation-model-for-tabular-data/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>TabFM: A zero-shot foundation model for tabular data</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TabFM：面向表格数据的零-shot 基础模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48739919' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 79</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48739919' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TabFM is a zero-shot foundation model designed for tabular data, enabling predictions without task-specific fine-tuning. It promises simpler deployment for analytics pipelines, while evaluating how it stacks up against task-specialized approaches.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TabFM 是一个面向表格数据的零-shot 基础模型，在不进行特定任务微调的情况下也能进行预测。它有望 simplif y analytics 流程的部署，同时需要评估其与专门任务模型的性能比较。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(347) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nicole.express/2026/phase-altering-by-line.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Composite Video on the NES: Why&#x27;s it so wobbly?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NES 的复合视频：为什么会抖动？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48798247' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 78</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48798247' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The wobble of NES composite video comes from analog signal limits, color encoding quirks, and timing jitter in older hardware. The piece explains why this classic setup is inherently unstable and what modern restorers can do to stabilize or emulate the signal.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NES 复合视频抖动的根源在于模拟信号的局限、颜色编码和时序抖动等因素。文中解释了为何这套经典设置易产生不稳定，并给出硬件改造或仿真再现的思路。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48770477' data-tags='AI,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(348) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://manticoresearch.com/blog/onnx-embeddings-speedup/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Faster embeddings: how we rebuilt the ONNX path in Manticore</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>14倍更快的嵌入：我们在 Manticore 中重构 ONNX 路径</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48770477' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 78</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48770477' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Manticore redesigned its ONNX embedding path to achieve roughly 14x faster embeddings, dramatically speeding up vector-based search workloads. The post explains the engineering choices that unlocked this uplift and its practical impact on real-time AI tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Manticore 重新设计了 ONNX 嵌入路径，使嵌入速度提升约 14 倍，显著加速向量搜索工作负载。文章阐述实现这一提升的工程选择及对实时 AI 任务的实际影响。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>混淆技术：构筑密码学的最终 Boss（第一部分）</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vitalik discusses obfuscation as a difficult, controversial area in cryptography, outlining core ideas, limitations, and why deployments are debated. Part I surveys why obfuscation is hard, when it’s even possible, and what it means for practical security. The series sets up a deeper dive into next installments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Vitalik 探讨了混淆在密码学中极为复杂且存在争议的地位，梳理了核心思想、局限性以及为何在实际部署中备受争议。第一部分概述了为何混淆难以实现、在何种情形下才有可能，以及对实际安全性意味着什么，为后续讨论打下基础。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一起安全事件中，因看似无害的行为而导致特权升级，揭示了最小权限、健全的访问控制以及持续监控以防止信任被滥用的重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(360) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://deep-reinforce.com/ornith_1_0.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ornith-1.0: Self-scaffolding LLMs for agentic coding</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ornith-1.0：具自我支撑能力的LLM用于代理式编码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48709744' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 73</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48709744' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ornith-1.0 introduces self-scaffolding techniques to steer LLMs into more autonomous, agentic coding workflows. By enabling models to bootstrap their own reasoning steps and tool use, it aims to improve reliability and reduce human-in-the-loop friction in software generation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ornith-1.0 引入自我支撑（自我搭桥）式的技术，旨在让大型语言模型在编码任务中变得更自主、具代理性。通过让模型自我启动推理步骤与工具使用，旨在提升软件生成的可靠性并降低对人类干预的依赖。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://deep-reinforce.com/ornith/assets/cover.png' data-full='https://deep-reinforce.com/ornith/assets/cover.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(361) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.plover.com/lang/bismillah.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Deciphering basmala</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>解密巴斯马拉（Basmala）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48657131' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 73</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48657131' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post delves into Basmala, the opening phrase of many Islamic texts, examining its linguistic form, religious significance, and historical usage. It situates the phrase within script and culture, clarifying common misconceptions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章从语言与文化角度解析巴斯马拉（祈祷名词）在阿拉伯语中的形式、宗教意义以及历史使用情境，揭示常见误解并说明其在文本与书写中的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Chromium blog announces the UserMedia HTML element, standardizing access to cameras and microphones with a unified API and permission model. This could simplify embedding user media in web apps and improve privacy UX.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48742514' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(364) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ayushtambde.com/blog/matrix-orthogonalization-improves-memory-in-recurrent-models/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Matrix Orthogonalization Improves Memory in Recurrent Models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>矩阵正交化提升循环模型的内存利用率</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48742514' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 73</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48742514' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Orthogonalizing recurrent weight matrices reduces memory usage and stabilizes training, enabling larger or longer-sequence models with less memory pressure. This technique helps maintain gradient flow and can improve training efficiency for RNNs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对循环网络的权重矩阵进行正交化可在训练时降低内存占用并稳定梯度，从而让模型规模和序列长度更大。该方法有望提升递归模型的可扩展性和训练效率。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ayushtambde.com/matrix-orthogonalization/algorithm.png' data-full='https://ayushtambde.com/matrix-orthogonalization/algorithm.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(366) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vllm.ai/blog/2026-06-29-micro-agent-frontier-models' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Micro-Agent: Beat Frontier Models with Collaboration Inside Model API</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Micro-Agent：在模型 API 内实现协作以超越前沿模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48722802' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 71</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48722802' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Micro-Agent describes a collaboration-centric approach to frontier models via a cohesive model API. By enabling multiple agents to share context and coordinate within a single interface, it aims to boost performance on complex tasks without fragmenting tooling. It signals a shift toward more modular, collaborative AI systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Micro-Agent 介绍了一种在单一模型 API 内实现多代理协作的前沿模型方法。通过让多个代理共用上下文、在同一接口内协同工作，目标是提升对复杂任务的处理能力，同时避免碎片化工具链。这预示着 AI 系统向更模块化、协作化发展。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(367) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://old.maa.org/press/maa-reviews/mathematics-its-content-methods-and-meaning' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mathematics: Its Content, Methods and Meaning</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《数学：其内容、方法与意义》</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48695502' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 71</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48695502' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Morris Kline’s Mathematics: Its Content, Methods and Meaning surveys how math has evolved and why its methods matter beyond rules. The book has been influential in math education and philosophy, though some readers find its views debatable. It remains a staple reference for understanding math’s cultural and epistemological dimensions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这本经典著作审视数学的内容、方法与意义，强调数学发展与教育、哲学之间的联系。它在学界与教育圈影响深远，但观点也引发讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(370) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://computer.rip/2026-07-04-microwave-and-power.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Megawatts by Microwave</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用微波实现兆瓦级能量传输</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48791591' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 70</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48791591' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microwave-powered megawatts explores the idea of delivering large-scale power via microwave beams, outlining the core physics, conversion steps, and safety hurdles. While scalable wireless power transfer is still experimental, the piece explains why breakthroughs could reshape grid design and remote energy delivery, and what challenges remain.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过微波束传输大规模电力的设想被探讨，文章梳理了核心物理、能量转换过程与安全难点。尽管大规模无线供电仍处于实验阶段，文中解释了若取得突破可能如何重塑电网架构和远程供电，并指出仍需解决的挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48723677' data-tags='Security,Open Source,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(372) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/jumpserver/jumpserver' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>JumpServer: Open-Source Privileged Access Management</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>JumpServer：开源特权访问管理工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48723677' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 69</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48723677' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>JumpServer emerges as an open-source Privileged Access Management solution. It offers centralized access control, session recording, and auditing for critical systems, presenting a cost-effective alternative to proprietary PAM. For security teams, it lowers barriers to strong PAM practices and integration with existing infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>JumpServer 作为开源特权访问管理解决方案渐入人心，提供集中访问控制、会话记录与审计等能力，帮助关键系统的安全管控。对安全团队而言，它提供了一更具性价比的替代方案，易于与现有基础设施整合。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/fc216b4e5421590933175cb530916ffd95a0ddce20b98d8779a2e99379c1a938/jumpserver/jumpserver' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/fc216b4e5421590933175cb530916ffd95a0ddce20b98d8779a2e99379c1a938/jumpserver/jumpserver' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

It reveals how his rhetoric shapes SoftBank&#x27;s bets and public perception, for better or worse.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章梳理软银创始人孙正义的独特演示风格：夸张的幻灯片、宏大愿景如何影响投资者情绪与市场预期，揭示其话语风格在软银投资策略与公众认知中的作用。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48740135' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 69</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48740135' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hatari now runs in the browser, letting users emulate Atari ST family machines online without local setup. The browser-based emulator leverages WebAssembly to bring classic systems to modern devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Hatari 现已支持在浏览器中运行，用户无需本地安装即可模拟 Atari ST 家族的机器。基于浏览器的模拟借助 WebAssembly 将经典系统带到现代设备上。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48732582' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(377) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.red-lang.org/2026/06/static-linking-support.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Red Programming Language: Static linking support</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Red 编程语言：静态链接支持</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48732582' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 69</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48732582' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Red language gains official static linking support, enabling standalone executables with fewer runtime dependencies. The change simplifies distribution and embedding Red in other projects, at the cost of larger binary sizes and potential platform quirks. It broadens Red’s appeal for systems programming and portability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Red 语言新增静态链接支持，使可执行文件在依赖运行时库更少的情况下独立运行。此变更简化了分发与在其他项目中嵌入 Red 的难度，但可能增加二进制体积并带来平台相关问题。此举提升了 Red 在系统编程与跨平台场景中的适用性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blogger.googleusercontent.com/img/b/R29vZ2xl/AVvXsEgqhNLBwvJSzclWn8Ur-lhPM2BhryOgDTAx79JeXkRWOaAkQQN_MvaeyKrhKbDbS5PDXsTILEjHN3ONIDB0IBQDkhWFg6dI3aps8KKB8crLd7NQUhtrjeA6_8uOZg625rivCLBTM6XltUt9YottuRPZzAJKsKZS3OIdKOsUzqINEWic3mhZsumc90vXLj0y/w1200-h630-p-k-no-nu/screenshot.png' data-full='https://blogger.googleusercontent.com/img/b/R29vZ2xl/AVvXsEgqhNLBwvJSzclWn8Ur-lhPM2BhryOgDTAx79JeXkRWOaAkQQN_MvaeyKrhKbDbS5PDXsTILEjHN3ONIDB0IBQDkhWFg6dI3aps8KKB8crLd7NQUhtrjeA6_8uOZg625rivCLBTM6XltUt9YottuRPZzAJKsKZS3OIdKOsUzqINEWic3mhZsumc90vXLj0y/w1200-h630-p-k-no-nu/screenshot.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(378) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://notes.shixiangxi.com/en/docs/appendix/alan-kay-on-oop/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Alan Kay on the meaning of &quot;object-oriented programming&quot; (2003)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>艾伦·凯谈面向对象编程的含义（2003）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48698781' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 68</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48698781' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Alan Kay offers a thoughtful critique of object-oriented programming, clarifying what OOP should mean beyond buzzwords and how objects enable modeling and interaction. The piece situates OOP within broader computing ideas and design principles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>艾伦·凯对面向对象编程给出深刻解读，澄清其含义超越流行语，以及对象在建模与交互中的作用。这一观察将 OOP 放在更广泛的计算思想与设计原则之中。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>

<p class='hn-title'>(385) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lilianweng.github.io/posts/2026-06-24-scaling-laws/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Scaling Laws, Carefully</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48689744' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 63</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48689744' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A nuanced look at scaling laws for AI emphasizes that bigger models alone aren’t sufficient. It argues for attention to data quality, architecture, and efficient compute, while urging careful experimentation to avoid overclaiming gains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 AI 扩展规律的细致分析强调，单靠更大模型并不能保证进展。应关注数据质量、架构和高效计算，并通过谨慎的实验避免对收益的空泛夸大。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>BareMetal RAM Dumper：用于冷启动攻击实验的 Bare-metal x86 工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48787201' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 62</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48787201' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A bare-metal RAM dumper enables memory dumps during cold-boot attack demonstrations, useful for hardware security researchers exploring memory residency and data remnants. The tool lowers the barrier to reproduce classic attack scenarios on x86 hardware, but underscores ethics and legal boundaries in performing such experiments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>BareMetal RAM Dumper 是一款在裸机上执行内存转储的工具，便于在冷启动攻击演示中提取内存数据，帮助研究者研究内存留存与敏感信息泄露。该工具降低了在 x86 硬件上复现传统攻击情景的门槛，但同时必须遵循伦理和法律边界。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/35b5fc617f03aeb46c26adedb9fd2a90a2a266e44b061f08912d9d64d8bf2af0/pIat0n/BareMetal-RAM-Dumper' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/35b5fc617f03aeb46c26adedb9fd2a90a2a266e44b061f08912d9d64d8bf2af0/pIat0n/BareMetal-RAM-Dumper' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(409) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/kerlenton/mcpsnoop' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Mcpsnoop – Wireshark for MCP (transparent proxy and live TUI)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Mcpsnoop——MCP 协议的 Wireshark（透明代理与实时文本界面）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48777144' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 56</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48777144' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Unveils Mcpsnoop, an open-source tool that acts like Wireshark for MCP protocols, offering a transparent proxy and a live TUI to inspect traffic in real time. The project lowers the bar for debugging MCP communications and could inspire similar protocol-inspection tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mcpsnoop 是一个开源工具，类似 Wireshark，但用于 MCP 协议，提供透明代理和实时文本界面以实时查看流量。该项目降低了调试 MCP 通信的门槛，或将激发更多协议分析工具的创作。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pieces bills itself as a social network that prioritizes real people and meaningful connections over algorithmic feeds, focusing on privacy and user control. It aims to offer a simpler, less attention-harvesting alternative for those frustrated with mainstream platforms’ dynamics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pieces 标榜为优先考虑真实人与有意义连接的社交网络，强调隐私与用户控制而非算法驱动的信息流。它为对主流平台机制感到厌烦的用户提供了更简洁、非吸引注意力的替代选项。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(412) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/kamaludu/bash4llm/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Bash4LLM+ – A lightweight, dependency-free Bash wrapper for LLM APIs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Bash4LLM+——一个零依赖的 Bash 调用 LLM API 的封装器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48710827' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48710827' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bash4LLM+ provides a lightweight, dependency-free Bash wrapper to call LLM APIs from the command line, enabling quick automation and scripting around AI prompts. It’s a handy tool for environments where small footprints and reproducibility matter.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bash4LLM+ 提供一个轻量、零依赖的 Bash 封装器，用于从命令行调用 LLM API，方便对 AI 提示进行快速自动化和脚本化。在追求小型占用与可重复性的环境中尤其实用。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introduces a gamified approach to learning formal proofs using RPG mechanics. It demonstrates how gamification can lower the barrier to mastering proof techniques and formal reasoning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以 RPG 机制将形式化证明的学习变成游戏化体验，展示游戏化如何降低掌握证明技巧与推理的门槛，适合教育场景使用。</p>

<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(415) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kendal.codeberg.page/inkwell/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Inkwell – An RSS reader for e-ink devices</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>展示 HN：Inkwell——为电子墨水设备设计的 RSS 阅读器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48763222' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48763222' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Inkwell is an RSS reader optimized for e-ink devices, focusing on low-power refresh, offline caching, and readable formatting tailored to slow-refresh displays. It targets Kindles and similar hardware, making it easier to consume long-form text without web bloat or heavy JavaScript.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Inkwell 是为电子墨水屏设备优化的 RSS 阅读器，注重低功耗刷新、离线缓存和为慢刷新屏做的易读排版。它面向 Kindle 等设备，让用户在没有网页臃肿和大量 JavaScript 的情况下更便捷地阅读长篇内容。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://kendal.codeberg.page/inkwell/img/home.png' data-full='https://kendal.codeberg.page/inkwell/img/home.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48786713' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(416) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vimeo.com/49718712' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rob Pike – &#x27;Concurrency Is Not Parallelism&#x27; [video] (2012)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rob Pike —《并发不是并行》[视频]（2012）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48786713' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48786713' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rob Pike&#x27;s 2012 talk clarifies the difference between concurrency and parallelism and why this distinction matters in software design, especially for Go and modern multicore systems. It&#x27;s a timeless reminder that how you structure tasks matters as much as how fast you run them.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rob Pike 的 2012 年演讲区分了并发与并行的概念，解释了为何这一区分在现代多核系统的设计中至关重要，尤其影响 Go 等语言的思路。提醒开发者关注任务组织方式，而不仅仅是追求并发的速度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.vimeocdn.com/video/343013570-bcea4ef59362f7f89324cd98c10598f6ce9d572fd6bb4310825bc7aa3f56801c-d?f=webp&amp;region=us' data-full='https://i.vimeocdn.com/video/343013570-bcea4ef59362f7f89324cd98c10598f6ce9d572fd6bb4310825bc7aa3f56801c-d?f=webp&amp;region=us' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(417) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25313' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Programmable Probabilistic Computer with 1M p-bits</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>可编程概率计算机：具备 100 万 p 位</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48708496' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48708496' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers demonstrate a programmable probabilistic computer built from one million p-bits, capable of tackling sampling and optimization tasks with potential energy efficiency advantages. This hardware approach may influence AI workloads and probabilistic computing research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究团队展示了一个由一百万 p 位组成的可编程概率计算机，适用于采样与优化任务，潜在的能效优势也被提及。这一硬件路线或为 AI 工作负载与概率计算研究带来新方向。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Plein Air（写生）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48786880' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48786880' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Plein Air is an art project exploring outdoor scenes using digital media, inspired by classic plein air painting. It demonstrates how sunlight, weather, and color translate into algorithmic visuals.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Plein Air 是一个以户外场景为主题的艺术项目，将传统野外写生的灵感与数字媒介结合。它展示了如何将日光、天气和色彩转化为算法生成的视觉效果。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48746752' data-tags='Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(420) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48746752' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Launch HN: Parsewise (YC P25) – Reason Across Documents with an API</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Parsewise（YC P25）上线：通过 API 实现跨文档推理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48746752' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48746752' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Parsewise offers an API to reason across documents, enabling applications to query and derive insights from a corpus rather than a single file. This enables smarter document intelligence for teams dealing with large document collections, from research to knowledge bases. Developers can integrate multi-document Q&amp;A, search, and summarization workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Parsewise（YC P25）提供通过 API 对跨文档进行推理的能力，使应用能够从一个文档集合中提取见解，而非局限于单篇文档。这为需要处理大量文档的团队带来更智能的文档分析能力，适用于研究和知识库等场景。开发者可以将多文档问答、搜索与摘要等工作流接入到应用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(421) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://publicdomainreview.org/collection/venice-bridge-fights/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Venetian Bridge Brawls in 17th and 18th Century Art</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>威尼斯桥上打斗的艺术描绘（17–18世纪）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48688382' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 50</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48688382' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Artworks from the 17th and 18th centuries portray street brawls on Venetian bridges, offering a window into urban life, social tensions, and public space in early modern Venice. These scenes reveal how artists framed city life and influenced cultural memory.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>17–18 世纪的艺术作品描绘威尼斯桥上的街头斗殴，提供了对早期现代威尼斯城市场景、社会紧张与公共空间使用的观察。图像与叙事呈现了艺术家如何讲述城市生活并影响文化记忆。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pdr-assets.b-cdn.net/collections/venice-bridge-fights/venice-bridge-fights-00008-thumb.jpg' data-full='https://pdr-assets.b-cdn.net/collections/venice-bridge-fights/venice-bridge-fights-00008-thumb.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(422) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://anatoliybabushka.com/blog/when-to-rewrite-working-code.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Most rewrites serve the engineer, not the business</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大多数重写只服务于工程师，而非业务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48750934' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 50</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48750934' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rewrites often satisfy engineers more than business needs. The piece argues for disciplined refactoring and selective rewrites based on measurable business value, maintainability, and risk considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>重写往往更取悦工程师而非推动业务价值。文章主张基于可衡量的商业价值、可维护性和风险考量，采用有纪律的重构与选择性重写。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48750000' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 50</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48750000' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A full ClickHouse OLAP engine compiled to WebAssembly enables in-browser analytics with low latency and offline capability, potentially changing edge data processing and privacy-preserving workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个完整的 ClickHouse OLAP 引擎已编译为 WebAssembly，使浏览器端分析具备低延迟和离线能力，可能改变边缘数据处理与隐私保护场景。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48710778' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 49</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48710778' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NanoEuler is a pure C/CUDA implementation of a GPT-2 scale model from scratch, emphasizing low-level optimization and portability. The project demonstrates how a modern LLM stack can be built with minimal dependencies, highlighting performance tradeoffs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NanoEuler 是用纯 C/CUDA 从零实现的 GPT-2 规模模型，强调底层优化与可移植性。该项目展示了在极少依赖下构建现代 LLM 堆栈的可行性，并揭示了性能权衡。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(427) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dmarcwise.io/blog/dmarc-np-incompatibility-with-dnssec' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why DMARC&#x27;s new &quot;NP&quot; tag can fail with DNSSEC</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DMARC 新标签 NP 与 DNSSEC 不兼容</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48794788' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 49</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48794788' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DMARC&#x27;s NP tag can fail with DNSSEC enabled due to validation peculiarities, affecting email authentication for some domains. Practical guidance includes testing and potential workaround.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在启用 DNSSEC 时，DMARC 新增的 NP 标签可能因验证边界情况而失效，影响部分域名的邮件认证。建议在部署前测试并评估替代方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dmarcwise.io/blog/dmarc-np-incompatibility-with-dnssec.png' data-full='https://dmarcwise.io/blog/dmarc-np-incompatibility-with-dnssec.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48692274' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 49</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48692274' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pystd offers a Python standard library-like experience with substantially reduced compile-time. It targets environments where startup time matters, enabling faster iterations without sacrificing core functionality.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pystd 提供类似 Python 标准库的功能，但编译时间大幅缩短，适用于对启动时间敏感的场景。该项目旨在在不牺牲核心功能的前提下提升开发与部署速度。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48774368' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(431) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02512' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Program-as-Weights: A Programming Paradigm for Fuzzy Functions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>程序即权重：模糊函数的编程范式</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48774368' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 46</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48774368' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introduces a programming paradigm where programs carry weights to handle fuzziness and uncertainty, linking ideas from probabilistic and differentiable programming to support robust approximate computation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出一种将程序视作带权重的组件以处理模糊性与不确定性的编程范式，连接概率编程与可微分编程的思路，支持对近似计算的鲁棒性设计。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48797771' data-tags='DevOps,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(432) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.arp242.net/deps-vcs.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dependencies should be fetched directly from VCS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>依赖应直接从版本控制系统获取</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48797771' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48797771' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Advocates fetching dependencies straight from version control systems to improve determinism and reproducibility. The piece weighs security, reproducibility, and workflow trade-offs against typical package managers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>主张直接从版本控制系统获取依赖以提升确定性与可重复性，讨论与常见包管理工具相比的安全性、可重复性与工作流取舍。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48749313' data-tags='DevOps,Hardware,Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(433) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cerebrium.ai/blog/reducing-gpu-cold-starts-with-memory-snapshots-restoring-cuda-workloads-in-second' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Reduce GVisor Cold Starts with GPU Snapshotting</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 GPU 快照减少 GVisor 冷启动时间</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48749313' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48749313' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers demonstrate reducing GPU cold-start latency for gVisor-based workloads by snapshotting GPU memory and restoring CUDA state. The technique cuts startup times and improves throughput for GPU-accelerated sandboxes in the cloud. It suggests a practical path to faster, more responsive containerized GPU workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员展示通过对 GPU 内存进行快照并恢复 CUDA 状态，显著降低基于 gVisor 的工作负载在冷启动时的延迟。这一技术缩短了启动时间并提升了云中 GPU 加速沙箱的吞吐量，提供了实现更快、响应更好的容器化 GPU 工作负载的切实路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.datocms-assets.com/180613/1774904124-og-image.png' data-full='https://www.datocms-assets.com/180613/1774904124-og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48742176' data-tags='AI,Career'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(434) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ramp.com/data/ai-jobs-impact' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How employment changes when firms adopt generative AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>当企业采用生成式 AI 时就业如何改变</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48742176' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48742176' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Generative AI adoption shifts job roles, increasing demand for prompt engineering, data handling, and system integration, while potentially reducing demand for routine tasks. The change underscores new skill requirements and organizational adaptation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>当企业引入生成式 AI 时，岗位结构发生变化，对提示工程、数据处理和系统集成等能力的需求上升，部分例行性工作可能被替代。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://next.ramp.com/assets/images/data/EconomicsLabOG.webp' data-full='https://next.ramp.com/assets/images/data/EconomicsLabOG.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(436) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lcamtuf.substack.com/p/all-logic-no-bite' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>All Logic, No Bite</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>全部是逻辑，毫无攻击性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48695585' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 44</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48695585' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Critiques overreliance on formal logic at the expense of practical impact, arguing for relevance and tangible outcomes. It challenges the cult of purity in problem solving and highlights the need to consider constraints and real-world constraints. A call for balancing rigor with applicability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>批评仅靠形式逻辑而忽略实际影响，呼吁在追求严谨的同时关注现实约束与可操作性。主张在理论与应用之间取得平衡，避免空谈。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48798313' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(437) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/514-labs/dnsglobe' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DNSGlobe – Rust TUI to watch DNS propagate around the world</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DNSGlobe：用 Rust 构建的文本界面监视全球 DNS 传播</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48798313' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 44</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48798313' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DNSGlobe is a Rust-based TUI that visualizes DNS propagation around the world, offering a lightweight tool for operators to observe latency, regional differences, and caching behavior. It demonstrates how terminal apps can serve practical network insight without heavy dashboards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DNSGlobe 是基于 Rust 的 TUI 工具，用以可视化全球范围内的 DNS 传播情况，方便运维观察延迟和区域差异。展示了在运维场景中，文本界面也能提供实用的网络洞察。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(439) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/ClickHouse/RayTracer' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ray Tracer in SQL</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SQL 实现的光线追踪</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48747407' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48747407' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ClickHouse&#x27;s Ray Tracer in SQL demonstrates how expressive SQL can be when used for graphics-like workloads, implementing ray tracing entirely in SQL primitives. The project serves as a provocative proof of concept and a pointer to what’s possible with vectorized engines. It invites exploration of SQL-based rendering techniques and performance considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ClickHouse 将光线追踪完全用 SQL 原语实现，展示了 SQL 在图形化工作负载中的表达力。该项目是一种挑衅性的概念证明，指向向量化引擎潜在的可能性，同时促使人们探讨基于 SQL 的渲染技术及其性能边界。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>关于吉姆·卡瑞去世报道的新闻是一种失败模式</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48784662' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48784662' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post uses a high-profile death hoax to illustrate a failure mode in information ecosystems, where rumor, media amplification, and algorithmic feeds propagate misinformation. It invites reflection on source verification and platform design to curb such spread.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章以吉姆·卡瑞去世传闻为例，揭示信息生态中的一种失败模式：谣言、媒体放大与算法推荐共同促使错误传播。呼吁加强信息源核验与平台设计，以抑制此类误导扩散。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48732741' data-tags='Programming,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(443) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tensor4all.org/blog/introducing-tenferro-rs/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>From Julia to Rust: a differentiable tensor stack for scientific computing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从 Julia 到 Rust：用于科学计算的可微分张量栈</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48732741' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48732741' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A differentiable tensor stack is being ported from Julia toward Rust, aiming to enable scientific computing with automatic differentiation in Rust. This shift could broaden Rust&#x27;s usability for research workloads and numeric computing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个用于科学计算的可微分张量栈正在从 Julia 移植到 Rust，目标是在 Rust 中实现自动微分的科学计算能力。这一转变可能扩大 Rust 在研究工作负载和数值计算中的应用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tensor4all.org/blog/introducing-tenferro-rs/tenferro-architecture.svg' data-full='https://tensor4all.org/blog/introducing-tenferro-rs/tenferro-architecture.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48762008' data-tags='Security,Privacy,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(444) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://joeyh.name/blog/entry/no_LLM_code_in_dependencies/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>No LLM Code in Dependencies</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>依赖中不得包含由大语言模型生成的代码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48762008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48762008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that dependencies should not contain code produced by large language models, stressing provenance, auditable sources, and replacing AI-generated snippets with human-authored equivalents to reduce licensing and security risks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>主张依赖包中不得包含由大语言模型生成的代码，强调可溯源、可审计的代码来源，并用人工编写的实现替代 AI 生成的片段以降低许可与安全风险。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>主张现代 C++ 编译器与优化器能提升代码安全性与性能，建议信任工具链、专注于干净的抽象，而非手动微优化。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new build system emphasizes static typing, cross- platform support, and simple bootstrapping, aimed at safer and more reproducible toolchains. It showcases a minimal, language-agnostic approach to building compilers and projects from scratch.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这款构建系统强调静态类型、跨平台与易于自举，旨在提升工具链的安全性与可重复性。展现了从零开始构建编译器和项目的简约、与语言无关的方法。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(449) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.britishorigami.org/cp-lister-list/the-1955-exhibition-by-akira-yoshizawa/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>British Origami: the 1955 exhibition by Akira Yoshizawa (2005)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英国折纸： Akira Yoshizawa 的1955年展览（2005）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48710325' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 39</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48710325' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This retrospective covers Yoshizawa&#x27;s 1955 origami exhibition and its impact on Britain&#x27;s origami scene. It traces how his notation system and iconic models helped popularize modern origami and inspired a global community.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本篇回顾 Yoshizawa 在1955年的英国展览及其对英国折纸社区的影响。介绍他所提出的折法记谱体系和经典折纸模型如何推动现代折纸的普及，并激发全球的折纸爱好者。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(450) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/gadgets/959899/meta-ai-glasses-paywall-rate-limit' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta is adding rate limits and soft paywall to smart glasses</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 将对智能眼镜添加速率限制和软性付费墙</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48742717' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 39</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48742717' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta is imposing rate limits and a soft paywall on features accessed via its AI-enabled smart glasses. The move affects developers and users relying on AI capabilities in wearable devices, signaling tighter access control and monetization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 将对其智能眼镜中的 AI 功能实施速率限制并引入软性付费墙。此举将影响开发者与用户对可访问性与成本的权衡，可能推动更高效的使用和新的商业模式。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(455) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sneakerweb.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Sneakerweb</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Sneakerweb：球鞋互联网的全景地图</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48799781' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 38</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48799781' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Sneakerweb maps the web of sneaker culture and commerce, combining data sources to reveal how drops, scarcity, and communities interlock online. The project raises questions about data provenance, platform power, and how niche markets scale on the public internet.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Sneakerweb 试图绘制球鞋文化与电商的网络全景，整合多源数据展现发售、稀缺性与社区互动的互动关系。引发对数据来源、平台权力与小众市场如何在公网上扩展的思考。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(456) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wsj.com/business/autos/ev-batteries-are-defying-expectations-after-hundreds-of-thousands-of-miles-8579de13' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EV Batteries Are Defying Expectations After Miles</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>电动汽车电池在里程考验后仍超出预期</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48791755' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 38</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48791755' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Real-world data shows EV batteries degrade more slowly than expected after hundreds of thousands of miles, challenging optimistic longevity assumptions. Advances in chemistry, thermal management, and usage patterns are cited as drivers, with implications for resale value and total cost of ownership.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>大量实际里程数据表明，电动汽车电池的衰减低于预期，经历数十万里程后仍保持较高容量。文章归因于化学改进、热管理与使用模式的优化，并对二手价值与总拥有成本产生影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(459) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://med.stanford.edu/news/all-news/2025/09/daylight-saving-time.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Study suggests most Americans would be healthier without daylight saving time</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>研究称若取消夏令时，大多数美国人会更健康</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48728294' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48728294' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new study argues that removing daylight saving time would improve public health by reducing sleep disruption and associated risks. The piece also touches on policy implications and potential energy or economic effects of keeping standard time year-round.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项新研究指出，取消夏令时可提升公众健康，降低睡眠干扰及相关风险。文章还探讨将标准时间定为常年时区的政策含义，以及对能源与经济的潜在影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://med.stanford.edu/news/all-news/2025/09/daylight-saving-time/_jcr_content/cq:featuredimage.coreimg.jpeg/1772836045869/Zeitzer-daylight-saving.jpeg' data-full='https://med.stanford.edu/news/all-news/2025/09/daylight-saving-time/_jcr_content/cq:featuredimage.coreimg.jpeg/1772836045869/Zeitzer-daylight-saving.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(460) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wsj.com/tech/ai/ai-data-centers-water-use-901e2902' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI Data Centers Use More Water Than Most Tech Giants Report</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 数据中心用水量高于多数科技巨头披露的水平</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48774246' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48774246' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Analyzes water usage in AI data centers, arguing that actual consumption may exceed what is publicly disclosed by many tech giants. The piece calls for improved reporting, more efficient cooling, and policy considerations as AI workloads scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>分析 AI 数据中心的用水情况，认为实际水耗可能高于多家科技巨头公开披露的水平。呼吁改进披露、提升冷却效率，并就随着 AI 工作负载扩张的政策进行考量。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>数学中的联系：两种随机性的区别</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48799026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 32</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48799026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Two kinds of randomness are contrasted in math: probabilistic randomness and algorithmic or structural randomness, with implications for modeling, cryptography, and learning theory. The post clarifies when each notion matters and how they shape proofs and experiments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中对数学中的“随机性”分为两种含义：概率上的随机性与算法/结构层面的随机性。区分二者有助于建模、密码学和学习理论的推理与实验设计。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(477) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://geastack.com/blog-the-age-of-personalized-hardware-is-coming' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Age of Personalized Hardware Is Coming</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>个性化硬件时代正在到来</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Personalized hardware is expanding from consumer devices to modular systems and bespoke silicon choices, driven by AI workloads and longer device lifecycles. The trend challenges mass-market homogenization and could unlock efficient, user-specific performance, while raising questions about supply chains, privacy, and upgrade paths.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>个性化硬件时代正在到来，未来将从通用设备走向模块化、定制化的硬件设计。此趋势可能带来更高性价比与长期可升级性，但也引发供应链、隐私与升级路径等新问题。</p>

<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(479) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/adam-s/car-diagnosis' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Classify mechanical faults using Contrastive Language-Audio Pretraining</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：用对比语言-音频预训练来分类机械故障</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48749868' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 31</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48749868' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A lightweight demonstration shows how Contrastive Language-Audio Pretraining (CLAP) can classify mechanical faults from audio signals using simple prompts. It highlights a practical path toward data-efficient fault diagnosis that can reduce downtime and maintenance costs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个简短的示例展示利用对比语言-音频预训练（CLAP）从声音信号中分类机械故障，并通过简单提示实现。该方案体现了数据高效的故障诊断潜力，有望降低停机时间与维护成本。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/739c8a49b8c0e392b0b9c09cf0021e5e42b19d4f450d9eefbd313a2d5dc6b250/adam-s/car-diagnosis' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/739c8a49b8c0e392b0b9c09cf0021e5e42b19d4f450d9eefbd313a2d5dc6b250/adam-s/car-diagnosis' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
