<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(480) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://segflow.github.io/post/zero-copy-sendfile-splice/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zero-copy in Go: sendfile, splice, and the cost of io.Copy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Go 的零拷贝：sendfile、splice 与 io.Copy 的成本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48797655' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48797655' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Examines how zero-copy transfers in Go behave in practice, weighing sendfile and splice against the traditional io.Copy approach. It offers pragmatic guidance on when zero-copy saves CPU and when it adds complexity or limits portability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨 Go 中零拷贝传输的实际表现，比较 sendfile、splice 与 io.Copy 的成本与权衡。给出在何时零拷贝确实节省 CPU、何时会增加实现复杂度或影响可移植性的实用建议。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://segflow.github.io//img/hacker_emblem.png' data-full='https://segflow.github.io//img/hacker_emblem.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>In a JAMA Psychiatry study, psilocybin-assisted therapy shows potential for improvement in treatment-resistant major depression, with considerations on safety and adverse effects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 JAMA 心理医学的一项研究中，裸盖菇素辅助治疗对治疗难治性重度抑郁症显示出潜在疗效，同时评估了安全性和不良反应。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48744546' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(482) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://encore.dev/blog/redis-runtime' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We put a Redis server inside our runtime</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>把 Redis 服务器放进运行时</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48744546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48744546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Integrating Redis directly into a runtime enables in-process caching and message passing without external services, reducing latency and deployment complexity. The post walks through design trade-offs and real-world benefits for microservices and edge apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将 Redis 直接嵌入运行时，实现就地缓存和进程内消息传递，降低延迟与部署复杂度。文中讨论了设计取舍及在微服务与边缘应用中的实际收益。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>WSL 容器现已进入公开预览，使 Linux 容器工作负载在 Windows 上更原生地运行，并简化了 Linux 与 Windows 工具链的协同工作流程。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48713453' data-tags='Programming,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(484) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.jayfreestone.com/writing/you-might-not-need-a-service-worker/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>You might not need a service worker</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你可能不需要服务工作者</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48713453' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 29</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48713453' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Many modern web apps can operate offline or with lighter caching strategies without a service worker, depending on app requirements. The post argues that service workers add complexity and are not always necessary for a robust offline experience, and suggests alternatives like server-driven caching or simple hard updates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对于某些场景，现代 Web 应用完全可以在没有服务工作者的情况下实现离线能力，或采用更简单的缓存策略。文章认为服务工作者并非在所有场景中都是必要的，并提出可选的替代方案以简化实现与维护。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.jayfreestone.com/_astro/service-worker-header.BovetkbK.png' data-full='https://www.jayfreestone.com/_astro/service-worker-header.BovetkbK.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(489) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nature.com/articles/s41598-026-57582-3' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Falling fertility on the left as key driver of US birth decline</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国出生率下降的一个关键推动因素：左翼人群生育率下降</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48795939' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48795939' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Nature Scientific Reports study links falling fertility among left-leaning demographics with part of the US birth-rate decline, highlighting how demographic shifts shape national trends.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Nature 的研究发现，偏左群体的生育率下降是美国出生率下降的一个关键因素，反映出人口结构变化对国家趋势的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(490) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rookandpossum.com/papers/scanline_sweeper_preprint.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Scanline Sweeper: A Glyph Rendering Algorithm [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>扫描线清扫者：一种 Glyph 渲染算法 [pdf]</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48746429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48746429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A preprint introduces Scanline Sweeper, a glyph rendering algorithm that improves rasterization of vector outlines, potentially delivering crisper text with lower rendering cost. The paper includes mathematical approach and performance comparisons.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Scanline Sweeper 提出一种字形渲染算法，改进向量轮廓的光栅化，理论上能获得更清晰的文本显示且计算成本更低。预印本给出数学思路与性能对比。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(491) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.dw.com/en/sick-leave-germany-rising-but-not-the-worst-in-europe/a-77815488' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sick leave: Germany rising but not the worst in Europe</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>病假：德国的缺勤上升，但并非欧洲最差</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48787058' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 24</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48787058' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Germany&#x27;s sick leave rate is rising but Germany is not the worst in Europe, according to a European study. The data may reflect aging workforce, healthcare access, or labor policy, with implications for productivity and social safety nets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧洲研究显示德国的病假率正在上升，但并非欧洲最差。数据可能反映人口老龄化、医疗保健可及性或劳动政策等因素，对生产率和社会保障体系有潜在影响。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48739644' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 22</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48739644' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ship traces the voyage of Spanish Armada sailors in 1588, offering new historical routes and navigational data. The piece underscores maritime archaeology&#x27;s ability to reframe famous events through rediscovered logs and ship tracks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对1588年西班牙无敌舰队水手航线的再追踪提供了新的航线线索与航行数据。海事考古通过重新发现的日志和航线，帮助重构这一历史事件的细节。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hands-on walkthrough shows how to read and parse the BIGF archive format from Codemasters games using Ruby, including binary reading techniques and structure discovery. The post provides practical tips for modding and asset extraction.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过实操演示，展示如何用 Ruby 读取并解析 Codemasters 游戏的 BIGF 归档格式，涵盖二进制读取与结构发现等技巧。内容对游戏 modding 与资源提取有实用价值。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(494) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26967' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Protocol Prying: Vulnerability Research in AirDrop and Quick Share</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>协议窥探：AirDrop 与 Quick Share 的漏洞研究</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48788849' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 21</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48788849' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The paper analyzes protocol-level flaws in AirDrop and Quick Share that could enable eavesdropping, spoofing, or data leakage. It highlights practical attack scenarios and emphasizes the need for robust cryptographic handshakes and continual protocol scrutiny in consumer file transfer features.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>论文对 AirDrop 与 Quick Share 的协议进行了漏洞研究，揭示在协议层面可能存在窃听、伪装或数据泄露等风险。强调需要强健的握手和持续的协议审查来提升消费级文件传输的安全性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(495) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.endbasic.dev/2026/07/endbasic-0.14.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EndBASIC 0.14: Are we multimedia yet?</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EndBASIC 0.14 adds multimedia capabilities to a tiny BASIC-like environment, aiming to bring audio, video, or graphics features to a microcontroller-friendly language. The release spotlights retro computing revival and education in handling media with constrained hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>EndBASIC 0.14 为这一迷你 BASIC 语言增加多媒体能力，尝试在受限硬件上实现音频、视频或图形等功能，推动复古计算的教育与复兴。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(496) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.exeter.ac.uk/faculty-of-environment-science-and-economy/scientists-discover-guidance-system-for-migratory-songbirds/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Scientists discover guidance system for migratory songbirds</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科学家发现候鸟迁徙的导航系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48781274' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 21</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48781274' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers identify a guiding mechanism behind migratory songbirds&#x27; routes, shedding light on how birds navigate across continents. The finding has implications for neuroscience, compass-like navigation models, and biomimetic AI ideas.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员揭示候鸟在跨洲迁徙时的导航机制，为鸟类跨大陆的导航提供新线索。该发现对神经科学、类指南针的导航模型以及仿生人工智能具有启示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.exeter.ac.uk/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Flycatcher.jpg' data-full='https://news.exeter.ac.uk/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Flycatcher.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(497) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ku.edu/news/article/study-reveals-what-people-really-see-when-they-read-lips' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Study reveals what people see when they read lips</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>研究揭示人们在读唇时看到的内容</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48742566' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 21</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48742566' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study investigates visual processing during lip-reading, showing how people interpret mouth movements to infer speech, and reveals variability in reliance on different cues. Findings have implications for communication tech, education, and assistive devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究探讨了读唇时大脑的视觉处理过程，揭示人们如何通过口型推断语音以及对不同线索的依赖差异。研究结果对通信技术、教育和助力设备具有启示意义。</p>
